### [1] [Creation of the Universe in Lorentzian Classical Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37748)
*Avipsa Chakrabarti,Avik Chakraborty,Martín Molina,Ayan Mukhopadhyay,Salomón Romero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在洛伦兹引力框架下，通过引入引力连接（gravitational junctions）而非要求光滑流形，构造了"宇宙创生门"（cosmic creation gates）：在 D≥3 维时空中将 n≥2 个局部 de Sitter 区域连接于类时理想流体结面（P=wρ），并分析了其测地完备性、体积演化、非线性背反应、线性扰动及宇宙学意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇点定理与暴胀时空的过去不完备性暗示宇宙的开端超出半经典引力适用范围；作者希望证明，即便无需光滑流形，也能在洛伦兹引力（而非欧几里得量子引力的瞬子方案）内理解宇宙的创生，从而为"有限体积的宇宙开端"提供自洽的经典几何描述。

Method: 采用引力连接（junction）方法构造时空：将有限多个（n≥2）局部 de Sitter 块在类时完美流体结面上拼接，结面状态方程为 P=wρ。分析两类解：（1）旋转不变（rotationally invariant）的宇宙创生门，涵盖 D≥3 维、任意 n≥2；（2）通过非线性均匀背反应得到的精确变形创生门（过去体积有限、未来体积无限）。随后在保持体区域局部 de Sitter 的前提下计算线性化扰动，并研究因果响应与初始数据对有限体积开端的约束。

Result: (1) 当 w>-(D-3)/(D-2) 时，创生门在时间两个方向均测地完备；(2) 在适配结面流体的全局时间分层上，总空间体积在无限过去与无限未来均趋于有限正值极限；(3) 精确变形解中，结面流体始终膨胀：w>0 时冷却，允许的 w<0 时加热；(4) 线性扰动方面，三维（D=3）时一般非齐次扇区含一个混合声模与 n-1 个相对横模，四维及以上（D≥4）仅存齐次混合声模；(5) 有限体积开端需精细调节齐次与非齐次初始数据，而因果响应通常导致未来空间体积无界。

Conclusion: 宇宙的创生可以在纯洛伦兹引力内通过引力连接加以理解，无需整时空为光滑流形；有限体积的开端要求对初始数据进行精细调节，而一般因果演化倾向于产生无界未来体积。文中提出一个更宽泛的创生判据（允许过去体积的有界振荡），引力方程决定条件化的可容许历史，并讨论了其对宇宙学的启示。

Abstract: The singularity theorems and the past incompleteness of inflationary spacetimes suggest that the beginning of the Universe lies beyond the domain of semiclassical gravity. We show that the creation of the Universe can nevertheless be understood within Lorentzian gravity by allowing gravitational junctions, so that the full spacetime need not be a smooth manifold. We construct rotationally invariant cosmic creation gates joining any finite number $n\geq2$ of locally de Sitter regions in $D\geq3$ spacetime dimensions at a timelike perfect-fluid junction with $P=wρ$. For $w>-(D-3)/(D-2)$, they are geodesically complete in both temporal directions. The total spatial volume approaches finite, positive limits in the infinite past and future on the global-time foliation adapted to the junction fluid. We also construct exact deformed cosmic creation gates through nonlinear homogeneous backreaction that retain the initial volume while the future volume grows without bound. In these solutions, the junction fluid always expands, cooling for $w>0$ and heating for allowed $w<0$. Keeping the bulk regions locally de Sitter, we determine the linearized perturbations: in three dimensions, the generic inhomogeneous sector contains one hybrid sound mode and $n-1$ relative transverse modes; in four and higher dimensions, only the homogeneous hybrid sound mode survives. A finite-volume beginning requires fine-tuned homogeneous and inhomogeneous initial data, while causal response generically leads to unbounded future spatial volume. We also discuss a broader creation criterion allowing bounded past-volume oscillations. The gravitational equations determine conditioned admissible histories. We discuss implications for cosmology.

</details>


### [2] [Black Hole Entropy from Entangled Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37750)
*Ludovic Varrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Donnelly–Freidel延拓相空间中处理Schwarzschild黑洞的edge mode，将两外部区域在分叉点粘合后得到单一正则对，并将其识别为Kuchař质量–渐近时间差或Carlip–Teitelboim面积–相对快度两种规范选择。要求框架因果性将其限制于半群SL^+(2,R)，其Plancherel测度给出Bekenstein–Hawking熵（含对数修正）作为两外部区域间的纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Schwarzschild黑洞edge mode的量子化，并解释黑洞熵的微观来源，克服紫外截断依赖性，从而将Bekenstein–Hawking熵理解为两外部区域间的纠缠熵。

Method: 在Donnelly–Freidel延拓相空间中，edge mode作为动态参考系，其相空间为SL(2,R)的余切丛；将两外部区域粘合后得到单一正则对。证明Kuchař（质量、渐近时间差）与Carlip–Teitelboim（面积、相对快度）是该系统的两种规范选择，后者即crossed product构造的时间平移。要求框架因果性后框架限制在半群SL^+(2,R)，利用其Plancherel测度对框架量子化，构造引力纠缠积并对两外部区域求纠缠熵。

Result: 得到Bekenstein–Hawking熵作为两外部区域间的纠缠熵，并附加对数修正项，两者均与紫外截断无关。

Conclusion: 通过将edge mode框架限制为因果半群SL^+(2,R)并采用Plancherel测度，可以从引力纠缠积中自然导出黑洞熵（含对数修正），且不依赖紫外截断，为黑洞熵微观机制提供了新的解释。

Abstract: In the extended phase space of Donnelly and Freidel, the edge modes of a Schwarzschild black hole are dynamical reference frames whose phase space is the cotangent bundle of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$. Gluing the two exterior regions at the bifurcation point leaves a single canonical pair. Within the extended phase space, this pair can be described either by Kuchař's mass and difference of asymptotic times or by the area and the relative rapidity of the two frames, which are two gauge choices of the same system. The latter is the pair of Carlip and Teitelboim, and the relative rapidity is the time shift of the crossed product construction. We quantize the frames in this gauge and construct the gravitational entangling product of the two exteriors. Requiring the frames to be causal restricts them to the semigroup $\mathrm{SL}^+(2,\mathbb{R})$, whose Plancherel measure yields the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy as the entanglement entropy between the two exteriors, together with a logarithmic correction, both independent of the ultraviolet cutoff.

</details>


### [3] [Holomorphic fermions in $\text{AdS}_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37762)
*Calvin Y. -R. Chen,Lukas W. Lindwasser*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在二维 Anti-de Sitter 空间上，具有离散精细调控的非零质量参数的 Majorana 费米子，拥有对称无迹守恒旋量-张量。这些旋量-张量在复坐标下对应成对的（反）全纯半整数自旋流，并具有类似二维共形场论的 Laurent 级数模展开。作者利用这一性质，用任意（反）全纯函数完整参数化与运动方程相关的经典相空间，并证明该参数化可借助共形 Killing 旋量-张量协变地写出，暗示存在隐藏的共形对称性，同时发现其手征代数是对应平坦空间理论中代数的严格子代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维 Anti-de Sitter 空间上的 Majorana 费米子（在精细调控的非零质量下）具有守恒的对称无迹旋量-张量，其 Laurent 模展开与二维共形场论类似。作者希望利用这一结构，完整刻画该理论的经典相空间，并探究可能存在的隐藏共形对称性及其手征代数与平坦空间情形的差异。

Method: 在复坐标下，将守恒的对称无迹旋量-张量分解为全纯与反全纯的半整数自旋流，并对其做 Laurent 级数模展开（类似二维共形场论）；以任意（反）全纯函数参数化运动方程的经典相空间；进一步用共形 Killing 旋量-张量将该参数化协变地表示，并分析手征代数结构。

Result: 得到经典相空间的完整参数化：由任意（反）全纯函数描述；该参数化可写成依托共形 Killing 旋量-张量的协变形式，从而揭示隐藏的共形对称性；同时证明所得手征代数是平坦空间对应理论手征代数的严格子代数。

Conclusion: 二维 Anti-de Sitter 空间上精细调控质量的 Majorana 费米子展现出隐藏的共形对称性，其经典相空间可由（反）全纯函数完全描述，且其手征代数比平坦空间情形更小（严格子代数）。

Abstract: On two-dimensional Anti-de Sitter space, Majorana fermions with a discrete set of finely tuned, non-zero mass parameters possess conserved spinor--tensors that are symmetric and traceless. In complex coordinates, these correspond to pairs of holomorphic and anti-holomorphic half-integer spin currents and admit Laurent series mode expansions reminiscent of standard two-dimensional conformal field theories. We exploit this fact in order to fully parameterise the classical phase space associated with the equation of motion in terms of arbitrary (anti-)holomorphic functions. We further show that this parameterisation can be written covariantly in terms of a conformal Killing spinor--tensor, hinting at a hidden conformal symmetry. We also show they possess chiral algebras which are strict subalgebras of the one present in the corresponding theory on flat space.

</details>


### [4] [Quiver Lab -- A Computing Tool for Quiver Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37766)
*Zhenghao Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Quiver Lab 是一个基于浏览器的工具，用于绘制箭图并探索三维 N=4 理论的模空间，主要计算 Higgs 支和 Coulomb 支的 Hilbert 级数及相关不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 N=4 超对称理论中，Higgs 支和 Coulomb 支的模空间及其分次（Hilbert 级数）是研究理论的核心对象，但相关计算复杂，亟需一个便捷的交互式工具来辅助探索。

Method: Quiver Lab 提供基于浏览器的工作区，支持绘制箭图，并采用 Light 与 Expert 两种工作流程，结合 plethystic 对数、支持的精细化以及 Decay 和 Fission Hasse 图等计算方法，对模空间进行系统计算。

Result: 该工具能够计算三维 N=4 理论的 Higgs 支与 Coulomb 支 Hilbert 级数、plethystic 对数、支持的精细化，以及 Decay 和 Fission Hasse 图，并以交互式界面呈现。

Conclusion: Quiver Lab 为研究三维 N=4 理论的模空间提供了一个实用且直观的浏览器工具，通过 Light 和 Expert 工作流降低了计算门槛，本文说明了其主要计算方法。

Abstract: Quiver Lab is a browser-based workspace for drawing quivers and exploring the moduli spaces of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 4$ theories. It computes Higgs and Coulomb branch Hilbert series, plethystic logarithms, supported refinements, and Decay and Fission Hasse diagrams. This note explains the Light and Expert workflows and the main computational methods

</details>


### [5] [Go with the flow(s): radial de Sitter holography and $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37776)
*Matthew J. Blacker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在静态patch中构造了定域于Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空的midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt态，并在此基础上建立了一个与切片无关的worldtube全息框架，进而与T²及T²+Λ₂截断全息框架建立字典对应，为dS₄黑洞提供了T²+Λ₂的体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究de Sitter时空的假设对偶理论：一方面想从体（bulk）了解对偶，另一方面想用候选对偶理论反推体的性质，特别是为dS黑洞建立可操作的截断全息字典。

Method: 构造定域在Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空上的midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt态；据此在静态patch内建立切片无关的worldtube全息框架；将该worldtube方案与T²及T²+Λ₂两种截断全息框架建立字典，并把worldtube的平均哈密顿量与T²(+Λ₂)理论的种子能量（seed energy）对应起来。

Result: 得到了dS₄黑洞T²+Λ₂框架的体实现；worldtube方案的平均哈密顿量被识别为T²(+Λ₂)理论的种子能量；若要使T²(+Λ₂)能谱作为worldtube理论的平均哈密顿量出现，必须让体框架依赖于切片，文中讨论了这一权衡的后果。

Conclusion: 切片无关的worldtube全息框架能够自然给出T²+Λ₂的体实现并匹配其种子能量，但严格恢复能谱需引入切片依赖性，体现了全息字典构造中"切片无关性与能谱匹配"之间的取舍。

Abstract: What can we learn about a putative dual to de Sitter spacetime from the bulk? Conversely, what can a candidate dual theory teach us about the bulk? In this work, we construct midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt states that are localised on the Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime. Using these states, we construct a slice-independent worldtube holographic framework in the static patch. We then construct a dictionary between this worldtube proposal and both the $T^2$ and $T^2 + Λ_2$ frameworks for cut-off holography. In doing so, we provide a bulk realisation of $T^2+ Λ_2$ for dS$_4$ black holes. We identify the average Hamiltonian of our worldtube proposal as the seed energy of the $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$ theories. To recover the $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$ energy spectrum as the average Hamiltonian of a worldtube theory, one must make the bulk framework slice-dependent, and we discuss the consequences of this trade-off.

</details>


### [6] [Differential Equations for Dressed "in-in" Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37856)
*Ivo Sachs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将微分方程与分部积分恒等式方法推广到宇宙学 in-in 关联函数（可表述为“着衣”的平坦时空振幅），并以 bubble-三角形图与交换图为例，展示了该方法如何系统地提取振幅所积分为的函数类信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学 in-in 关联函数在形式上可视为“着衣”的平坦时空振幅，但其积分结构复杂，传统方法难以系统地确定这些振幅对应的函数类。作者希望借助场论中成熟的微分方程与 IBP 方法，为宇宙学关联函数提供一套可算法化、能提取函数类信息的分析框架。

Method: 将微分方程（differential equations）与分部积分恒等式（Integration-by-Parts identities, IBP）的形式体系推广到宇宙学 in-in 关联函数；把共形耦合标量场的关联函数表述为“着衣”的平坦时空振幅（dressed flat space amplitudes），并证明所得的微分方程可以算法化地构造。

Result: 该方法能有效提取所研究振幅积分后所属的函数类信息，并通过宇宙学 bubble-三角形图和交换图这两个具体例子验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 微分方程与 IBP 方法对宇宙学 in-in 关联函数同样适用且十分强大，可算法化实施，为系统研究宇宙学振幅的函数结构提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We extend the formalism of differential equations and Integration-by-Parts identities, to cosmological in-in correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields, that can be formulated as dressed flat space amplitudes. These differential equations turn out to be quite powerful for extracting information about the class of functions which the corresponding amplitudes integrate to. Furthermore, they can be formulated algorithmically. Here we illustrate this for the cosmological bubble- triangle and exchange diagram.

</details>


### [7] [Near-horizon BF bounds for the $t^+$ KK tower on extremal AdS$_5$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37908)
*Xiao-Long Liu,Zheng Miao,Yuezhang Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究IIB型t^+ Kaluza-Klein塔在等旋转极值带电AdS₅黑洞上的所有视界谐波的带电Breitenlohner–Freedman（BF）界，发现BF界满足与否取决于电荷与自旋的二次型及其在BPS曲线上的符号改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究极值带电AdS₅黑洞近视界区域对标量扰动的BF稳定性边界，厘清电荷、自旋等参数如何决定所有Kaluza-Klein层次模式是否违反BF界，从而为理解这类黑洞的（近视界）稳定性问题提供依据。

Method: 标量方程来自最大五维规范超引力在level 0的约化，以及更高level上线性的例外场论（ExFT）方程。作者对电荷与自旋构造二次型，分析其在BPS黑洞曲线上符号的改变，并研究不同区域（Ω_H>1、Φ_H>1）及大半径、固定旋转下level-4模式（自旋正比于半径）的BF界违反行为。

Result: 在电荷与旋转对齐时，电荷—自旋二次型在BPS曲线上改变符号，且所有模式在该曲线上满足BF界；在Ω_H>1区域，每个黑洞都存在违反模式，但没有任何level或自旋的有限截断能对每个此类黑洞都检测到违反；在Φ_H>1区域，满足BF界的集合被限制在一条由电荷与自旋离散性控制的窄带内；在大半径且固定旋转时，自旋与半径成正比的level-4模式即使在所有U(2)单态都满足BF界的区域也会违反该界。

Conclusion: 这些结果涉及二导数超引力中单个KK塔的近视界BF界，完整径向稳定性问题仍待解决。

Abstract: We study the charged Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound for all horizon harmonics of the type IIB $t^+$ Kaluza--Klein tower on extremal charged AdS$_5$ black holes with equal rotations. The scalar equation follows from maximal five-dimensional gauged supergravity at level zero and the linearized exceptional-field-theory equations at higher levels. For aligned charge and rotation, a quadratic form in charge and spin changes signature on the curve of supersymmetric (BPS) black holes, where every mode satisfies the bound. Above the curve, where $Ω_H>1$, every black hole has a violating mode, although no finite truncation in level or spin detects violation at every such black hole. Below the curve, where $Φ_H>1$, the BF-passing set is confined to a thin strip and controlled by charge and spin discreteness. At large radius and fixed rotation, a level-four mode with spin proportional to the radius violates the bound even where all U(2) singlets satisfy it. These results concern the near-horizon BF bound of one tower in two-derivative supergravity; full radial stability remains open.

</details>


### [8] [Quotient Quiver Subtraction -- Product Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38052)
*Sam Bennett,Amihay Hanany,Guhesh Kumaran*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于膜系统的系统化方法，通过"商箭图减法"对3d N=4规范理论的U(n)×U(n')和SU(n)×SU(n')涌现库仑分支全局对称性进行规范（gauging），并用大量例子验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前商箭图减法只能处理有限类型的库仑分支全局对称性规范，作者希望拓展该方法，以系统化地规范U(n)×U(n')与SU(n)×SU(n')这类涌现的库仑分支全局对称性，并将其置于膜（brane）系统的框架下。

Method: 作者利用膜系统引入系统化方法，将规范过程实现为两步操作，其中关键一步是把酉规范因子"辛化"（symplectisation）为辛群；即通过商箭图减法的组合操作，将酉群规范因子转化为辛群来处理。

Result: 该方法成功规范了U(n)×U(n')和SU(n)×SU(n')的涌现库仑分支全局对称性，并通过大量显式例子验证了该程序的有效性；同时建立了与Class S理论以及实现极大内爆空间（maximal implosion spaces）的三维理论之间的联系。

Conclusion: 通过商箭图减法与辛化操作相结合，可以将更多类型的库仑分支全局对称性规范纳入统一的组合学/膜系统框架，并揭示其与Class S理论及极大内爆空间理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Quotient quiver subtraction translates the gauging of Coulomb branch global symmetries into a combinatoric operation on 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ gauge theories. This work uses brane systems to introduce a systematic method to gauge $\textrm{U}(n)\times\textrm{U}(n')$ and $\textrm{SU}(n)\times\textrm{SU}(n')$ emergent Coulomb branch global symmetries. Similar to other recently-introduced techniques, the gauging operation is a two-step process; in this case, it involves the symplectisation of unitary gauge factors into symplectic groups. A host of explicit examples are used to verify the procedure, and connections are made to Class $\mathcal{S}$ theories and three-dimensional theories realising maximal implosion spaces.

</details>


### [9] [Covering Surfaces, Conical-Defect Continuation, and Multi-Entropy at Arbitrary Genus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38208)

Applications to fractional quantum gravity and its comparison with quadratic quantum gravity are discussed.

In a fixed spin$^c$ sector, this anomaly is removable by a counterterm, so it is a property of the sum over spin$^c$ structures. We discuss its consequences for gapped, Coulomb, and Higgs phases. The chiral anomaly is governed by the index of the spin$^c$ Dirac operator, so rational chiral defects carry gravitational as well as electromagnetic responses. A spin$^c$ Hall topological field theory cancels the electromagnetic part but leaves a framing anomaly. At the level of anomaly inflow, this is canceled for every rational angle by stacking a neutral, generally noninvertible Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev layer. The same framing subtlety affects the chiral defects of QED on curved spin manifolds. The complex projective plane illustrates each of these features explicitly.

Abstract: It has recently been argued that cosmological horizon thermodynamics and black hole thermodynamics are generically inequivalent in any consistent, UV complete theory of quantum gravity, where it was suggested that this could be falsified by the identification of a counterexample. The kinematic family contains the limits governed by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions and interpolates between them through the walking anomalous dimension introduced in~\cite{Alday:2026cpk}. Our result reproduces the one-loop interpolation and fixes the two-loop coefficients in their proposed all-loop ansatz. Interestingly, our results suggest that the walking anomalous dimension may be entirely determined through all loop orders by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions.

Abstract: We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. However, its many Kac poles make it difficult to use in semidefinite programming approaches to the bootstrap. It also computes global blocks, $N=1$ superconformal blocks, torus characters, and torus one-point blocks. We illustrate it with two applications. We also bound the average exchanged dimension in four-point functions of identical scalars at several central charges. These bounds have a rich structure, and many known 2d CFTs lie on or near their boundary.

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational path integral of $\mathbb{R} \times S^3$ geometries in Lorentzian signature along with the metric fluctuations which live on the $S^3$ hypersurfaces. Each metric configuration is characterised by lapse, scale factor and fluctuations, and is weighted by the exponential of the Einstein-Hilbert action. A particular boundary requirement for the scale-factor is achievable via an infinite family of covariant boundary actions, each restricting the boundary behaviour of the fluctuation field which cannot be arbitrarily chosen. The paper explores the effect of this infinite family of covariant boundary actions on the wavefunction of Universe to one-loop. These boundary actions are characterized by parameters $c$ and $ω$. Dominant contribution of initial momentum in the path-integral restricts $ω$ to be half-integer, leading to an infinite family of $c$-dependent conjugate momenta. In special case for fixed $ω$, four such $c$-dependent dominant branches of conjugate momenta emerge: two lead to deSitter, one leads to Hartle-Hawking and one to the tunnelling Universe. For all these $c$-dependent initial conjugate momenta, the lapse-saddles are relevant, however only, one of deSitter saddle and Hartle Hawking saddle have well-behaved perturbations. The one-loop wavefunction at these well-behaved saddles is computed systematically by isolating the UV-divergences, where the covariant boundary parameter $c$ acts as a UV-regulator and also helps in shifting the poles of one-loop determinant, away from the real $q_f$ axis for dS saddles, where $q_f$ is the final size of the Universe. UV-finite wavefunction for no-boundary and dS is obtained by adding suitable counterterms. The infrared behaviour of these wavefunctions show not only IR-divergences but also sensitivity toward the initial boundary choices.

Abstract: We establish the first law of black hole thermodynamics for the recently discovered Kerr swirling (KS) and accelerated Kerr swirling (AKS) black hole solutions. Guided by Noether's original insight that global symmetries give rise to conservation laws, we employ and adapt the surface charge method for computing the associated charges (mass and angular momentum) in the presence of spacetime string-like defects (conical singularities) along the axes of both KS and KTN black holes. We then establish a first law without introducing independent stringy terms unrelated to symmetries. For both KS and KTN, the quantities that play the role of the black hole entropy differ from the simple geometrical rule of one-fourth of the horizon area. This mismatch arises because we keep first-law charges associated with conservation laws. It indicates that standard formulas may not be naively used for black holes with string-like defects; they have a different topology and suggest the need for a new geometrical framework. Gravitational dressing ensures these constructions are coordinate invariant. Then we show how these finite-distance observables become memory and Shapiro time delay operators upon taking asymptotic limits. We compute leading graviton effects in correlators of geometric observables throughout. Abstract: Research in the deep sub-mK regime around 0.1 mK may open new opportunities in condensed matter physics in light of recent rapid progress in quantum technologies and quantum materials. Numerical analyses indicate that the copper nuclear-spin system is cooled to about 18 $μ$K, whereas a local heat leak to the thermometer limits the thermometer temperature to about 50 $μ$K. Abstract: Classical Hamiltonian time crystals are constrained energy minima that move along symmetry orbits at frequencies set by Lagrange multipliers. Abstract: The quantum metric length has been proposed as the localization length scale in one-dimensional flat bands. Selective mass disorder also produces divergent localization lengths or arbitrarily short tails near the flat-band frequency without changing the clean metric. These exceptions show that disorder can select localization scales beyond those predicted by the quantum metric alone.

TL;DR: 本文利用扩散蒙特卡罗（DMC）计算了均匀电子气（HEG）在Wigner-Seitz半径2≤rs≤100范围内、零温下的静态密度响应函数χ(q)与局域场因子G(q)，填补了此前仅覆盖金属密度(rs≤10)的空白，并给出了数值上可行的计算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域场因子G(q)刻画了交换和相关效应对静态微扰响应的修正，是介电屏蔽、有效电子-电子相互作用以及TDDFT交换关联核的关键输入；但此前精确的零温G(q)数据仅限于金属密度(rs≤10)，低密度(rs>10)区域缺乏准确数据。

Result: 结果与金属密度下q>k_F的既有DMC数据一致并拓展至q<k_F，与rs=2的图解蒙特卡罗(diagrammatic QMC)一致；低密度下G(q)在中间q区出现非单调结构，响应趋近非相互作用气体极限；与有限温度路径积分蒙特卡罗定量吻合，表明该有限温度结构源自基态；借助覆盖整个液相区域的参数化，将该结构归因于对关联函数及静态结构因子中的短程有序。

Conclusion: 本文提供了均匀电子气静态响应性质在未来QMC计算中的实用指南，给出了可靠且可推广的G(q)数据与参数化，并将其低密度非单调结构与短程有序联系起来。

Abstract: The local field factor $G(q)$ of the HEG describes how exchange and correlation modify the response to a static perturbation, and enters dielectric screening, effective electron--electron interactions, and the xc kernel of TDDFT. Accurate zero-temperature data for $G(q)$, however, remain limited to metallic densities, $r_s\leq10$. In this work, we present diffusion Monte Carlo calculations of the static density response function $χ(q)$ and of $G(q)$ for $2\leq r_s\leq100$, and describe and discuss the methodological steps that enabled these calculations. We show how optimization of the perturbing potential in the trial wave function has an important effect, as a poor choice can reverse the sign of the response. A closed-form expression is provided for its optimal value, together with the density dependence of the linear-response window. We further show that backflow correlations leave $χ(q)$ unchanged and that $G(q)$ depends only weakly on system size even at very low density, so that Slater--Jastrow wave functions and modest system sizes suffice. Together, these results provide a practical guide for future QMC calculations of response properties of the electron gas and real materials. Our results agree with previous DMC data at metallic densities for $q>k_F$, and extend them below $k_F$. They also agree with diagrammatic QMC at $r_s=2$. At lower densities, our results show that $G(q)$ develops a nonmonotonic structure at intermediate-$q$, where the response approaches that of the noninteracting gas. Quantitative agreement is seen with finite-temperature path integral Monte Carlo, suggesting that the previously reported finite-temperature structure is inherited from the ground state. Using a parametrization of $G(q)$ that reproduces our data throughout the liquid phase, we relate this structure to short-range order in the pair correlation function and the static structure factor.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索交错磁性是否可能表现出不连续跃迁，并寻找通过自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点增强自旋劈裂效应以服务于自旋电子学应用的途径。

Method: 在含各向异性跃迁积分的双子晶格方晶格上，对扩展t-J模型采用平均场理论进行研究，并调控化学势与各向异性跃迁强度。

Result: 发现一阶元交错磁相变产生中间交错磁态：交错磁化强度降低，但磁八极矩和时间反演奇的自旋劈裂电导显著增强；一阶相变线终止于量子临界端点，形成丰富的相图。

Conclusion: 自旋劈裂范霍夫奇点是增强自旋劈裂效应的有效途径，为自旋电子学应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Altermagnets are collinear magnets with zero net magnetization and spin-split electronic bands. Here, we explore whether altermagnetism can exhibit a discontinuous jump, a phenomenon we refer to as meta-altermagnetism. We show that meta-altermagnetism can emerge in 2D systems when magnetic-order-induced band reconstruction drives spin-split van Hove singularities (vHSs) across the Fermi level. Using mean-field theory for an extended $t$-$J$ model on a two-sublattice square lattice including anisotropic hopping integral, we find that a first-order meta-altermagnetic transition produces an intermediate altermagnetic regime with reduced staggered magnetization but strongly enhanced magnetic octupole and time-reversal-odd spin-splitter conductivity. We further show that the first-order transition line terminates at a quantum critical endpoint, giving rise to a rich phase diagram as the chemical potential and anisotropic hopping strength are varied. Our results identify the spin split vHSs as a route to enhancing spin-splitter effect for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [65] [Field theory of competing phases in the interacting Kitaev chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37761)
*Pradip Kattel,Thierry Giamarchi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合玻色化与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）研究一维相互作用Kitaev链在有限化学势下的相图与临界性，揭示拓扑超导、非公度Luttinger液体、公度电荷密度波Mott相及带绝缘相之间的竞争，并给出拓扑相的对称破缺对偶描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kitaev链研究多局限于可精确求解的无相互作用的特殊格点极限，而有限密度下掺杂会非微扰地改变超导配对与公度电荷序之间的竞争，缺乏普适的连续场论描述。作者希望建立相互作用、有限化学势下的一维拓扑超导体的解析理论框架。

Method: 采用玻色化（bosonization）连续场论与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）数值计算相结合的方法。具体而言：远离可精确求解的格点极限，建立有限密度相互作用区的连续场论描述；用Pokrovsky-Talapov（PT）临界性刻画公度-非公度相变，用Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）临界性刻画非公度-拓扑超导相变；在半经典层面及自由费米子边界理论中分析拓扑相的指数局域Majorana边缘模；并构造对偶连续费米子表示，将拓扑体相转化为传统对称破缺（SSB）相。

Result: 相图包含四个部分：拓扑超导相、中间的非公度Luttinger液体、公度电荷密度波Mott相，以及大化学势下的平庸带绝缘相。公度-非公度相变由Pokrovsky-Talapov普适类描述，非公度-拓扑超导相变由BKT普适类描述。拓扑相在拓扑区与平庸区界面处支持指数局域的Majorana边缘模。对偶费米子表示中拓扑体相对应于传统对称破缺，给出SPT-SSB对应的连续版本。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一维相互作用拓扑超导体的完整解析描述，涵盖拓扑与公度性竞争、量子临界性以及拓扑相的对称破缺对偶表示。该框架揭示了掺杂对拓扑与公度电荷序竞争的非微扰影响，并给出SPT-SSB对应的连续理论版本，为理解一维相互作用拓扑超导体提供了普适的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the interacting Kitaev chain with nearest-neighbor interactions and finite chemical potential using a combination of bosonization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations. Focusing away from special exactly solvable lattice limits, we develop a continuum field-theoretic description of the generic finite-density interacting regime, where doping nonperturbatively modifies the competition between superconducting pairing and commensurate charge ordering. We show that the phase diagram consists of an intermediate incommensurate Luttinger liquid between the topological superconducting and commensurate charge-density-wave Mott phases, as well as a trivial band-insulating regime at large chemical potential. The commensurate--incommensurate and incommensurate--topological-superconducting phase boundaries are governed by Pokrovsky--Talapov and Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless criticality, respectively. We analyze the interacting topological superconducting regime and its boundary excitations. At the semiclassical level, and explicitly in the free-fermion boundary theory, the topological phase supports exponentially localized Majorana edge modes at interfaces between topological and trivial regions. We also construct a dual continuum fermionic representation in which this topological bulk sector is described instead by conventional symmetry breaking. This gives a continuum version of the SPT--SSB correspondence between different fermionic representations. Overall, our results provide an analytical description of one-dimensional interacting topological superconductors, from the competition between topology and commensurability and the resulting quantum criticality to the dual symmetry-breaking description of the topological phase.

</details>


### [66] [Long-Range Toric Code: Finite-Temperature Topological Order in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37765)
*Jacopo Gliozzi,Taylor L. Hughes,Kyung-Su Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文引入带有长程环路相互作用的二维 toric code 模型，在保持基态拓扑序的同时约束任意子，并论证长程相互作用下该约束可在有限温度持续，从而实现自纠错量子记忆与长程纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在二维系统中实现有限温度下的拓扑序与自纠错量子记忆。普通的二维 toric code 在有限温度下无法维持拓扑序，因为任意子可自由移动。作者希望设计带长程相互作用的模型，在保持基态拓扑序的同时约束（confine）任意子，从而抑制热涨落对拓扑序的破坏。

Method: 在二维 toric code 模型中添加额外的长程环路（loop）相互作用。这些相互作用不改变普通 toric code 的拓扑有序基态，但在零温下约束任意子。针对不同相互作用范围，分析任意子迁移率在有限温度下的受限程度，进而判断拓扑序的存续情况。

Result: 当相互作用足够长程时，该约束在有限温度下依然持续，强烈限制任意子移动，产生有限温度拓扑序，表现为自纠错量子记忆并具有长程纠缠。当相互作用较短程时，有限温度下拓扑序的迹象只能维持到指数大的长度尺度，形成部分自纠错的量子记忆。

Conclusion: 通过在二维 toric code 中加入长程环路相互作用，可以在有限温度下稳定拓扑序，实现自纠错量子记忆；相互作用范围越短，拓扑序在有限温度下仅能维持到指数大的尺度，体现为部分自纠错能力。

Abstract: We introduce two-dimensional toric code models with additional long-range loop interactions. Abstract: The ground level of the linear $E\otimes e$ dynamic Jahn-Teller model has long been believed to be the doublet with vibronic angular momentum $j = \pm\tfrac12$. The proof uses only a unitary transformation that lowers $j$ by one, combined with the variational principle. We also give an explicit lower bound on the gap $E_{0,j+1} - E_{0j}$.

Abstract: The chiral central charge $c$ measures the gravitational anomaly of a two-dimensional topological phase. It fixes the heat current carried along the edge. So far, it has been accessed through thermal transport, where phonons complicate the signal, or through wave-function probes that need a pure ground state. Here we show that $c$ is a spectroscopic quantity of the edge. A weak modulation of the edge couplings that travel along the edge is absorbed according to the Virasoro algebra. Normalized by the response to a static change of the same couplings, the absorbed weights obey $D_n=2n\langle L_0\rangle+\frac{c}{12}(n^3-n)$, so that $\mathcal{C}\equiv2D_2-4D_1=c$ in any state of the ideal edge theory, thermal or not. The central charge thus appears in transition weights, although it cancels from excitation gaps. The ratio of line weights with and without a vison in the bulk, $Λ=4h_σ/c$, needs no calibration and fixes the conformal spin $h_σ$ of the non-Abelian Ising anyon. Calibrating with the shift of the measured line itself gives $c$ within about $1\%$ on 96 spins and within $1\%$ under $20\%$ random coupling errors. Interacting edge Majoranas in a fixed flux sector, treated with DMRG, keep $\mathcal{C}$ within $1.5\%$ while the edge velocity changes by $\pm13\%$. An exact simulation of a quantum-processor protocol, including the native Floquet circuit, returns $c$ within $3.5\%$ on 96 to 600 spins.

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource that gives rise to correlations with no classical counterpart in strongly correlated fermionic systems. However, its characterization in mixed quantum states remains challenging. Established entanglement diagnostics, such as logarithmic negativity, are computationally demanding in interacting systems, and their evaluation by exact diagonalization is restricted to small system sizes. Meanwhile, conventional Rényi proxies, including twisted and untwisted Rényi negativities, can be positive even for separable states. In this work, we introduce a family of \textit{relative Rényi negativities} that serve as entanglement witnesses. By relating fermionic partial time reversal to the size superoperator, we establish a path-integral representation for these Rényi negativities, enabling their evaluation in thermal states of the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model and its generalizations via a large-$N$ expansion. Applying this method, we investigate entanglement in both the original SYK model and the Maldacena-Qi coupled SYK model. We identify a special class of relative Rényi negativities which serve as plausible proxies for logarithmic negativity and uncover first-order entanglement transitions that are invisible to simple observables. These results highlight the utility of relative Rényi negativities as probes of mixed-state entanglement in strongly correlated fermionic systems and suggest the existence of a new class of transitions where these negativities serve as free energies.

Abstract: We present the heat capacity of the frustrated sawtooth-chain compound atacamite Cu$_2$Cl(OH)$_3$ in high magnetic fields up to 35 T for $\mathbf{H} \parallel b$ axis. The findings are consistent with our scenario for the high-field state in atacamite for $\mathbf{H} \parallel c$ axis, where we proposed the sawtooth chains to decouple into two spin subsystems, basal and apical spin sites, leading to a partially polarized state with $M \sim M_\mathrm{sat}/2$.

Abstract: The microscopic origin of high-$T_c$ superconductivity in cuprates remains an outstanding puzzle due to the interplay of intertwined orders. Experimental observations and theoretical studies have suggested crucial roles of phonons, with the $B_{1g}$ oxygen buckling mode emerging as a promising candidate. Abstract: Electronic liquid crystals, such as stripe phases and nematics, are self-organized states of matter with spontaneously broken rotational symmetry. They are expected to emerge in a doped Mott insulator as a compromise between short- and long-range Coulomb interactions, and are believed to be intimately connected to the emergence of high temperature superconductivity either as a competing order or an intertwined order. Moiré semiconductors provide a controllable platform to explore the relationship between stripe phases and superconductivity near a Mott insulator. Here we report the generic appearance of stripe phases in a doped moiré Mott insulator and the observation of a stripe to-superconductor quantum phase transition in twisted bilayer WSe2. The stripe phases were revealed by electrical transport studies as a thermodynamic phase transition from a high temperature isotropic phase to a low-temperature anisotropic phase accompanied by a Fermi surface reconstruction. Near the stripe-to-superconductor transition, we also observed an additional thermodynamic phase transition from metallic stripes to pinned insulating stripes. The stripe-to-superconductor transition further exhibits an intriguing self-similarity that highlights the intimate relationship between the two orders. Our results pave the path for studying intertwined orders in moiré materials.

Motivation: 现有微观多体方法能精确描述平衡态量子态，但大规模涌现序的动力学模拟仍依赖带预设自由能泛函的唯象方程，缺乏从微观哈密顿量出发的统一框架。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，为相场描述提供微观依据，并显式化其隐含的绝热闭合假设。

Method: 作者引入绝热变分原理（AVP）。当微观弛豫远快于序参量演化时，多体态绝热地跟随集体构型（类比Born-Oppenheimer分离），每个瞬时序参量构型定义一个受约束的准稳态多体态，约束的拉格朗日乘子给出驱动集体演化的热力学力。非线性Mori-Zwanzig投影理论将可逆动力学与集体变量的微观代数相连接，并将耗散与涨落归因于被消除的自由度。

Abstract: Recent advances in microscopic many-body methods have enabled increasingly accurate descriptions of equilibrium quantum states, yet large-scale simulations of emergent-order dynamics typically rely on phenomenological equations with prescribed free-energy functionals. We introduce the Adiabatic Variational Principle (AVP), a framework for deriving order-parameter dynamics directly from an interacting many-body Hamiltonian. When microscopic relaxation is fast compared with order-parameter evolution, the many-body state follows the collective configuration adiabatically, in analogy with the Born--Oppenheimer separation of electronic and nuclear motion. This assumption makes explicit the adiabatic closure implicit in conventional phase-field descriptions. Each instantaneous order-parameter configuration then defines a constrained quasi-stationary many-body state, and the Lagrange multipliers enforcing these constraints directly provide the thermodynamic forces driving the collective evolution. Nonlinear Mori--Zwanzig projection theory further connects the reversible dynamics to the microscopic algebra of the collective variables and relates dissipation and fluctuations to the eliminated degrees of freedom. Applications to charge- and spin-density-wave dynamics recover Allen--Cahn domain growth, resolve evolving domain networks together with their microscopic electronic structure, and yield generalized Landau--Lifshitz dynamics for itinerant magnetism. The AVP thus provides a solver-independent microscopic foundation for phase-field-like simulations of the nonequilibrium dynamics of emergent electronic order.

</details>

Abstract: Perovskites exist in varied structures and show structure dependent magnetic properties which is of great interest in the field of multiferroics, magnetocaloric and spintronic devices. Different Curie temperature, spinglass transition and spin reorientation transitions were observed depending on the degree of interactions and structural properties. It is interesting to study the role of orbital interactions, disorder in magnetic transitions leading to change in superexchange and double exchange interaction. On substitution at A-site or B-site results in change in magnetic interactions, as well as creates structural distortions. We also obtained results where the spin interaction of the transition metal and rare-earth interaction, magnetic anisotropy, orientations of orbitals, disorder induced interactions were observed.

Abstract: In this article, we address the possibility of topological quantum spin liquids with only short-range (spin) correlations in the whole phase diagram in a ladder system, through an exactly solvable model which represents a generalization of the two-dimensional Kitaev honeycomb spin model to a ribbon geometry. This system gives rise to $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge fields emerging in a static way in the ground state through Majorana fermions which then produce either $0$ or $π$ flux. Through a quantum field theory perspective, mathematical and Density Matrix Renormalization Group results, we present our progress on the geometrical, topological and physical classification of this model through observables navigating on the whole phase diagram presenting three spin-liquid phases with a spin gap and a metallic phase in the sense of Majorana fermions. We propose a bulk-edge correspondence revealing the presence of protected zero-energy Majorana fermions at the edge in the phases with a gap and revealing also the presence of itinerant Majorana fermions in the bulk in the topological liquid phase. The proposed approach allows us to distinguish between properties of the brickwall ladder giving rise to a special line and those of the square ladder allowing for the gapless Majorana liquid phase. This model gives some hope on finding solutions for topological quantum spin models, e.g. with $π$ flux, on a square lattice geometry. This approach also allows for a correspondence onto models of topological superconductivity.

</details>

Abstract: New insights into the paramagnetic range of PrB$_6$ below 50 K are revealed through specific-heat, thermal-expansion and magnetic measurements. The latent heats of the two successive magnetic transitions, together with an improved description of the lattice contribution, enable a detailed determination of the thermal variation of the magnetic entropy. Immediately above the first-order magnetic ordering temperature ($T_N = 6.7$ K), the magnetic entropy per Pr$^{3+}$ is close to that of a doublet, instead of the triplet crystal-field ground state identified by inelastic neutron scattering. Temperatures in excess of 30 K are required to reach the entropy of the threefold-degenerate ground state. This is reminiscent of the CeB$_6$ case and, similarly, strongly suggests a dynamical splitting of the crystal-field ground state within the boron cage. Further evidence of this splitting is provided by the analysis of the low-temperature paramagnetic susceptibility, which resembles that of a non-magnetic ground state. In addition, a large thermal-expansion anomaly is observed below 20 K, consistent with the expected centrifugal Jahn-Teller effect.

</details>

*Nick G. Jones,Paul Fendley,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Ruben Verresen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出"准相"(quasi-phases)概念，用以描述相图中终止的连续临界线附近连通的带隙区域，这些区域虽在更广相图中连通，却无法由临界理论的小扰动所及的带隙路径连接。作者在中心荷c=1的自旋链实例中，发现支持准相的商对称保护(c=3/2)与表示对称保护(c=9/5)多临界轨道折叠理论，并通过弱化Lieb-Schultz-Mattis反常构建构造原理，使不必要的临界点在红外尺度变为必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变线在量子多体相图中可出现并终止，但不清楚邻近区域能否像水与蒸汽那样被有意义地描述为不同相，因为任何尖锐刻画都必须超越通常的物质相概念。

Method: 引入准相概念：将临界点附近的带隙区域定义为准相，要求它们在更广相图中连通，但不由临界理论小扰动可达的带隙路径连接。通过自旋链中c=1终止临界线的实例，将其嵌入更大的多临界母体理论(c=3/2和c=9/5轨道折叠)中描述，并弱化Lieb-Schultz-Mattis反常作为构造原则。

Result: 找到了多临界c=3/2与c=9/5轨道折叠理论，分别描述"商对称保护"和"表示对称保护"拓扑现象；准相界面模可携带更高维线性表示；支持不同准相使不必要的临界点在红外尺度变得必要，这一条件比单纯存在余维一临界线更严格。

Conclusion: 准相为物质相内部结构揭示了比传统分类更丰富的层次：带隙区域可在同一相内由带隙路径连通却仍展示界面模等拓扑现象，且需要更大的多临界母理论来描述，表明物质相内部存在超出常规对称保护拓扑分类的结构。

Abstract: Within quantum many-body phase diagrams, continuous transition lines can appear and then terminate. However, it has remained unclear whether the neighboring regimes can be meaningfully described as distinct phases, akin to water and vapour, since any sharp characterization would have to go beyond the usual notion of phases of matter. We answer this in the affirmative by introducing quasi-phases: gapped regimes near a critical point that are connected in the broader phase diagram, but not by any gapped path accessible from small perturbations of the critical theory. The physical consequences include interface modes between regimes in the same phase of matter. More striking still, describing both the extended critical line and the gapped path connecting the regimes requires a bigger multicritical parent theory. In spin-chain examples with continuous terminating lines with central charge $c=1$, we find multicritical $c=3/2$ and $c=9/5$ orbifolds describing so-called 'quotient symmetry protected' and 'representation symmetry protected' topological phenomena. While one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases are labeled by projective representation classes, these quasi-phases can have interface modes carrying higher-dimensional linear representations. We turn this into a constructive principle through a weakening of the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis anomaly. Supporting distinct quasi-phases makes an unnecessary critical point infrared-necessary, even if it is unnecessary at the ultraviolet scale. This condition is more restrictive than simply having a codimension-one critical line, revealing richer structure within phases of matter than their conventional classification suggests.

</details>


### [85] [Algebraic theory of abelian fractons and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40596)
*Wilbur Shirley,Evan Wickenden,Bowen Yang,Agnès Beaudry,Michael Hermele*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套阿贝尔有隙分形子序与拓扑相中融合、迁移率与统计的代数理论，将阿贝尔任意子的代数描述推广到受限迁移与空间延展激发系统。核心工具是辫化融合复形（BFC），其等价类定义辫化融合理论（BFT）。文章提出并给出证据支持“非退化BFT可实现为有隙相激发理论且为相不变量”的猜想，推导了点状融合与迁移率的约束，并在仅有分点激发时提炼出互统计理论，将远程探测解释为分形子序中互统计的推广，并证明激发-探测器原理等价于非退化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿贝尔任意子的代数描述无法直接适用于具有受限迁移率与空间延展激发的分形子体系，缺乏统一的代数框架来刻画这类激发上的融合、迁移率与统计性质，也无法形式化表述远程探测与相不变性等概念。

Method: 作者针对平移不变的玻色子系统（其点状与延展激发具有有限融合阶），引入辫化融合复形（BFC）——即编码融合与迁移率的链复形，并配以编码统计的辫化链映射；其等价类构成辫化融合理论（BFT）。在该框架下，对相位进行代数分析，提炼互统计理论（融合模到其统计对偶的映射），并将远程探测形式化为激发-探测器原理。

Result: 该框架涵盖任意的平移不变Pauli码以及所有二维空间中的阿贝尔任意子理论（含手征理论）。给出证据支持“非退化BFT可实现为有隙相激发理论、且描述该相的BFT是相不变量”的猜想。推导出点状融合与迁移率约束，例如X-cube与各向异性模型的Pauli X或Z扇区均无法单独出现在非退化BFT中。对仅有分点激发的非退化理论，提取的互统计理论是BFT不变量，且在奇数融合阶下等价于完整辫化融合理论。证明了在唯一无穷空间基态假设下，激发-探测器原理（每个非平凡激发可被无穷远处算符探测，且每个非平凡探测器探测到某个激发）等价于非退化性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的BFC/BFT代数框架成功统一了分形子序与拓扑相中阿贝尔激发的融合、迁移率与统计描述，揭示了远程探测作为互统计推广的本质，并将激发-探测器原理与非退化性等价地联系起来，为分形子序的分类与相不变量研究提供了严格代数基础。

Abstract: We develop an algebraic theory of fusion, mobility, and statistics in abelian gapped fracton orders and topological phases. The framework generalizes the algebraic description of abelian anyons to systems with restricted-mobility and spatially extended excitations. We consider translation-invariant bosonic systems whose pointlike and extended excitations have finite fusion order. The basic objects are braided fusion complexes (BFCs): chain complexes encoding fusion and mobility, equipped with a braiding chain map encoding statistics. Their equivalence classes define braided fusion theories (BFTs). Our framework encompasses arbitrary translation-invariant Pauli codes, as well as all abelian anyon theories in two spatial dimensions, including chiral theories. We give evidence for the conjecture that every nondegenerate BFT can be realized as the excitation theory of a gapped phase and that the BFT describing such a phase is a phase invariant. We derive constraints on pointlike fusion and mobility, showing, for example, that neither the Pauli X nor Z sectors of the X-cube and anisotropic models can occur alone in a nondegenerate BFT. For nondegenerate theories with only pointlike excitations, we extract a simpler theory of mutual statistics, consisting of a fusion module and a map to its statistical dual. This theory of mutual statistics is an invariant of the BFT and, at odd fusion order, gives an equivalent description of the full braided fusion theory. We interpret remote detection as the generalization of mutual statistics to fracton orders. In the BFC setting, we give a precise formulation of the excitation-detector principle: every nontrivial excitation can be detected by an operator supported at spatial infinity, and every nontrivial detector detects some excitation. Assuming a unique infinite-space ground state, we prove that this principle is equivalent to nondegeneracy.

</details>


### [86] [Variational wavefunctions for ideal anyon gas: superfluid and skyrmion Hall crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40657)
*Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过构建变分波函数，在球面上研究了统计角 θ∈(0,π) 的任意子理想气体的基态，并结合复合玻色子、复合费米子、分身（parton）波函数与神经网络关联，研究了无自旋和 SU(2) 半子（semion）的配对任意子超流体，以及 θ=π/3 时 SU(3) 味任意子的两种候选态：斯格明子霍尔晶体和手性颜色超导体（color-flavor-locked 配对态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子理想气体的基态是一个长期未解问题，近期分数陈绝缘体的实验观测重新激发了研究兴趣。作者希望确定不同统计角和内部对称性下任意子的基态相，并解释扭转 MoTe₂ 中观测到的重入整数量子反常霍尔相和超导现象。

Method: 在球面上构造严格满足任意子统计的变分波函数，将理论引导的复合玻色子、复合费米子和分身（parton）波函数与神经网络关联相结合。针对自旋为 0（无自旋）和 SU(2) 半子检验配对超流体；对 θ=π/3 且具有 SU(3) 味的任意子，构造斯格明子霍尔晶体（自旋纹理产生的有效磁场中的整数量子霍尔态）和手性颜色超导体（六任意子团簇的 color-flavor-locked 配对，波函数为超行列式而非分身行列式的乘积），并与 U(1)² 和 Z₃ 规范群的 color-flavor-locked 相及晶体态比较能量。

Result: 对无自旋和 SU(2) 半子，确认了配对任意子超流体，其能量远低于最优晶体态。在 θ=π/3 的无自旋情形，液体和晶体能量更接近。对具有 SU(3) 味的 θ=π/3 任意子，发现两种候选态：斯格明子霍尔晶体和手性颜色超导体；在所用变分拟设内，颜色超导体的能量优于晶体态以及 U(1)² 和 Z₃ 规范群的 color-flavor-locked 相。据此推测扭转 MoTe₂ 中的重入整数量子反常霍尔相是掺杂任意子的斯格明子霍尔晶体，而观测到的超导可能是颜色超导体，后者可跃迁为 f+if BCS 超导体，伴随手性中心荷跃变 3。

Conclusion: 变分研究确认了统计角 θ∈(0,π) 任意子的基态相图：半子呈现配对的任意子超流体；θ=π/3 的 SU(3) 味任意子则以手性颜色超导体（color-flavor-locked 配对）为最有利态。该结果对理解扭转 MoTe₂ 的重入整数量子反常霍尔相和超导性提供了新的理论解释。

Abstract: The ground state of an ideal gas of anyons with statistical angle $θ\in(0,π)$ is a long-standing open problem, with renewed interest from the recent observations of fractional Chern insulators. In this work we construct variational wavefunctions that build in the exact anyon statistics and combine theory-guided composite-boson, composite-fermion and parton wavefunctions with neural-network correlations on the sphere. For spinless and SU(2) semions we confirm the paired anyon superfluid, well below the best crystal states. At $θ=π/3$ liquids and crystals are closer in energy for the spinless case. For $θ=π/3$ anyons with SU(3) flavor, we find two candidates. One is a skyrmion Hall crystal, in which the anyons form an integer quantum Hall state in the effective magnetic field from the spin texture. The other is a chiral color superconductor of six-anyon clusters, the color-flavor-locked pairing descendant of a holon metal with SU(3) color gauge symmetry and SU(3) flavor symmetry; its wavefunction is a hyperdeterminant rather than a product of parton determinants. Within our variational ansatz the color superconductor has better energy than both crystals and the color-flavor locked phases with U(1)$^2$ and Z$_3$ gauge groups. For twisted MoTe$_2$ this suggests that the reentrant integer quantum anomalous Hall phase is the skyrmion Hall crystal of the doped anyons, and that the observed superconductivity may be the color superconductor, which can undergo a transition to an $f+if$ BCS superconductor with a jump of the chiral central charge by 3.

</details>

*Nunzia Coppola,Giovanni Vinai,Alksandr Yu Petrov,Chiara Sacco,Hafiz Sami Ur Rehman,Sofia Scarnicci,Lucia Capogna,Cinzia Di Giorgio,Carmela Aruta,Luigi Maritato,Alice Galdi*

TL;DR: 本文通过磁光克尔效应（MOKE）和磁电阻测量，研究了由铁磁层La0.7Sr0.3MnO3（LSMO）夹在两层同金属性反铁磁SrMnO3（SMO）之间的三层异质结构，揭示了界面结构/轨道重构与铁磁-反铁磁竞争所导致的复杂磁性行为，并展示了通过改变LSMO厚度调控界面作用以定制磁性能的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用异质结构作为有效策略来调控和控制磁性行为，特别是研究SMO/LSMO/SMO三层膜中结构重构、轨道重构以及界面处铁磁性与反铁磁性竞争所引发的复杂磁性能。

Method: 采用磁光克尔效应（MOKE）与磁电阻测量相结合的方法，对SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3三层膜进行表征；通过选用两种不同的LSMO厚度，系统改变界面所扮演的角色，从而调控嵌入在两层同金属性反铁磁层（SMO）中的铁磁LSMO层的磁性能。

Result: 1）在125 K以下观察到LSMO磁易轴发生自旋重取向，偏离其室温取向，原因是界面交换作用压过衬底诱导的磁各向异性，与同金属性锰氧化物异质结构的既有模型一致；2）界面处超交换与交换相互作用的竞争导致高度受阻的界面自旋玻璃态，使矫顽场和交换偏置增强约20倍；3）界面效应驱动的磁晶各向异性改变引起各向异性磁电阻响应的变化，该变化在低温行为中占主导地位。

Conclusion: 通过合理调控铁磁LSMO与反铁磁SMO界面的作用（由LSMO厚度决定），能够有效定制三层异质结构的磁性能，证实了异质结构是调控和控制磁性行为的有效策略。

Abstract: In this work we explore the complex magnetic behaviour of SrMnO3/La0.7Sr0.3MnO3/SrMnO3 trilayers combining magneto-optic Kerr effect (MOKE) and magneto-resistance measurements. The interplay of structural and orbital reconstruction and the competition of ferromagnetism/antiferromagnetism at the interfaces is responsible of a variety of complex magnetic properties, highlighting the use of heterostructures as an effective strategy to tune and control magnetic behaviour. We demonstrate the possibility to tune the magnetic properties of a ferromagnetic La0.7Sr0.3MnO3 (LSMO) layer, embedded in two homometallic antiferromagnetic layers (SrMnO3, SMO), by properly varying the role of interfaces using two different LSMO thickness values. Spin reorientation of the LSMO magnetic easy axis away from its room temperature orientation was observed below 125 K, due to the predominance of exchange interaction at the interface over the substrate-induced magnetic anisotropy, in agreement with established models for homometallic manganite heterostructures. The competition of super-exchange and exchange interactions at the interface results in a highly frustrated interfacial spin glass state, resulting in 20-fold enhancement of the coercive field and exchange bias. The modification of the magnetocrystalline anisotropy driven by the interfacial effects leads to a change in the anisotropic magneto-resistance response of the system, which dominates the low temperature behaviour.

*Yan Zhang,Hanqi Pi,Wangqian Miao,Zhong Fang,Hongming Weng,Xi Dai,B. Andrei Bernevig,Quansheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 MoireKP，一个从定域轨道从头算哈密顿量自动构建紧凑连续模型的自动化工作流，并通过对 MoTe₂、ZrS₂ 等扭转层状材料的验证表明其模型能在目标窗口内以 3.0 meV 精度复现 TAPW 能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究摩尔（moiré）材料需要包含电子结构信息的第一性原理哈密顿量，但其尺寸巨大，导致后续计算（如多体、输运、能带分析）成本高昂。因此需要一种方法将大尺寸的定域轨道从头算哈密顿量压缩为紧凑的连续模型，同时保留低能子空间内的波函数信息。

Method: 该工作流首先构造谷分辨的截断原子平面波（TAPW）哈密顿量；然后选取低能基组；接着将晶体对称性操作投影到该基组中，以确定对称性允许的哈密顿量项；再通过将低能有效哈密顿量投影到相应的算符基上求得各项系数。模型维度、定域参考态和谐波截断既可自动选取，也可由用户指定。

Result: 工作流被应用于 AA 堆叠扭转双层 MoTe₂、AB 堆叠扭转双层 ZrS₂ 以及 A–AB 扭转三层 MoTe₂，覆盖 K、M 和 Γ 谷。所有模型在目标窗口内沿高对称 k 路径均能以 3.0 meV 以内的精度复现 TAPW 能带能量。

Conclusion: MoireKP 提供了一种基于哈密顿量的自动化构建方法，能有效地从定域轨道从头算哈密顿量得到紧凑连续模型，保留低能子空间波函数信息，并实现高精度能带复现，适用于不同堆叠和谷类型的扭转层状材料。

Abstract: First-principles Hamiltonians contain the electronic information needed to study moiré materials, but their size makes subsequent calculations costly. We present MoireKP, an automated workflow for constructing compact continuum models from localized-orbital ab initio Hamiltonians. The workflow constructs a valley-resolved truncated atomic-plane-wave (TAPW) Hamiltonian, selects a low-energy basis, and projects the crystal-symmetry operations into this basis. These representations determine the symmetry-allowed Hamiltonian terms, whose coefficients are obtained by projecting the low-energy effective Hamiltonian onto the corresponding operator basis. The model dimension, localized reference states, and harmonic cutoff can be selected automatically or specified by the user. This Hamiltonian-based construction retains wave-function information within the selected low-energy subspace. We apply the workflow to AA-stacked twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$, AB-stacked twisted bilayer ZrS$_2$, and A--AB twisted trilayer MoTe$_2$, covering K, M, and $Γ$ valleys. All models reproduce TAPW band energies within 3.0 meV in the target windows along high-symmetry $k$ paths.

</details>

*Yue Wu,Yuan Tian,Haoyu Wan,Deng Pan,Mingwei Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 平面能量虽是面心立方金属孪生能力的标准描述符和势函数常用验证指标，但本文证明它们无法决定纳米晶铝中形变孪晶能否存续。两个机器学习势在平面能量上几乎一致，却对同一孪晶前驱体预测出64%与14%截然不同的孪晶保留率，差异源于畸变部分位错核心内的力以及晶界位错滑移前缘的到达顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示平面能量（planar energetics）作为孪生能力描述符的局限性：它们虽被广泛用作面心立方金属孪晶判据和势函数验证目标，但可能无法决定性预测纳米晶铝中形变孪晶的实际存续行为，需要找到真正决定孪晶命运的因素。

Method: 采用两个机器学习势，它们重现相同的零载荷滑移曲线和接近的分层能量面；以共同的分离断层前驱体为起始构型，在相同加载条件下分别进行模拟，统计孪晶保留覆盖率；再通过分析畸变部分位错核心内沿尾随平移方向作用的力、局部牵引与全滑移平分线的相对取向，以及晶界网络中部分滑移前缘的到达顺序来追溯分歧来源。

Result: 两个机器学习势对同一分离断层前驱体在相同加载下分别预测64%和14%的孪晶保留覆盖率。分歧源于畸变部分位错核心内沿尾随平移方向作用、会移除外层断层的力。平面能量设定了裕度：外层断层使中面剪切的增量势垒减半以上，但孪晶完成与外层断层移除之间仅相差10–21 mJ m⁻²。孪晶能否存续最终由局部牵引相对于全滑移平分线的取向，以及晶界网络中部分滑移前缘的到达顺序决定。

Conclusion: 平面能量不足以决定纳米晶铝中形变孪晶的存续；真正的决定因素是畸变核心内局部牵引的取向及晶界位错滑移前缘的到达顺序。这为势函数开发和孪晶判据提出了超越平面能量描述符的新要求。

Abstract: Planar energetics are the standard descriptors of twinnability in face-centred cubic metals and the usual validation targets for interatomic potentials. Here we show that they do not determine whether deformation twins persist in nanocrystalline aluminium. Two machine-learned potentials reproducing the same zero-load slip curves and closely spaced layered energy surfaces predict, from a common separated-fault precursor under identical loading, retained twin coverage of 64\% and 14\%. The divergence traces to forces within distorted partial cores acting along the trailing translation that would remove an outer fault. Planar energetics do set the margin: an outer fault more than halves the incremental barrier for middle-plane shear, yet twin completion and fault removal remain separated by only 10--21 mJ m\textsuperscript{-2}. Whether an assembled twin persists is decided instead by the orientation of local traction relative to the full-slip bisector and by the arrival order of partial-slip fronts from the grain-boundary network.

</details>


### [90] [Odd Elasticity in Strain-Gradient Continua: Anisotropy Classification and Non-Hermitian Elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40581)
*Payam Poorsolhjouy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将奇弹性(odd elasticity)推广到三维应变梯度框架，系统推导了各点群对称性下四、五、六阶奇弹性刚度张量，并建立动力学本构与声张量分析，揭示了非厄米弹性动力学需各向异性、且横观半异向奇弹性连续体可产生奇异点与主动能量交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇弹性将连续介质力学扩展到含内能源的主动非保守系统（如具有非互易反馈的机器人主动超材料）。将这类系统建模为有效连续体需放松 Maxwell-Betti 互易性，导致刚度张量缺乏主对称性。已有框架难以刻画这类异质系统固有的长度尺度，因此需要将其推广到三维应变梯度奇弹性。

Method: 作者将奇弹性框架扩展到 3D 应变梯度奇弹性：沿手性与非手性分支施加完整的 3D 点群对称层级，系统导出四、五、六阶奇弹性刚度张量的完备集合；利用动力学虚功原理建立平衡方程、高阶边界条件及广义声张量用于弹性动力学分析；并通过数值分析横观半异向(transversely hemitropic)奇弹性连续体验证理论。

Result: 结果表明非互易模量在完全各向同性（六阶刚度）、半异向（五阶刚度）和立方对称（四阶刚度）下仍然存在；但弹性动力学分析显示在各向同性与半异向极限下，尽管存在奇刚度模量，声张量仍无条件为厄米矩阵，非厄米弹性动力学需各向异性。对横观半异向奇弹性连续体的数值分析表明，该对称性降低产生非厄米弹性动力学现象，包括色散谱中的奇异点（exceptional points）以及通过弹性波分量的同时放大与衰减实现的主动能量交换。

Conclusion: 该工作为各向异性应变梯度主动微结构的多尺度均匀化框架以及非互易应变梯度超材料设计提供了必要的理论基础。

Abstract: Odd elasticity extends continuum mechanics to active nonconservative systems containing internal energy sources, such as robotic active metamaterials with nonreciprocal feedback. Modeling these systems as effective continua requires relaxing Maxwell-Betti reciprocity, which results in stiffness tensors lacking major symmetry. In this contribution, we extend this framework to 3D strain-gradient odd elasticity to incorporate the length scales inherent in these heterogeneous systems. By applying the full hierarchy of 3D point-group symmetries along chiral and achiral branches, we derive the exhaustive set of fourth-, fifth-, and sixth-order odd elastic stiffness tensors. Further, utilizing the dynamic principle of virtual work, we formulate the equilibrium equations, higher-order boundary conditions, and the generalized acoustic tensor for elastodynamic analysis. The results demonstrate that nonreciprocal moduli survive even under full isotropy in the sixth-order stiffness, under hemitropy in the fifth-order stiffness, and under cubic symmetry in fourth-order stiffness. In contrast, the elastodynamic analysis reveals that under isotropic and hemitropic limits, despite the underlying odd stiffness moduli, the acoustic tensor remains unconditionally Hermitian. Non-Hermitian elastodynamics is shown to require anisotropy. By numerically analyzing a transversely hemitropic odd elastic continuum, we demonstrate that this symmetry reduction results in non-Hermitian elastodynamic phenomena including exceptional points in the dispersion spectra and active energy exchange through simultaneous amplification and attenuation of the elastic wave components. These results provide the necessary theoretical foundation for future multiscale homogenization frameworks for anisotropic strain-gradient active microstructures, and for the design of nonreciprocal strain-gradient metamaterials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够测量表面图案化（金属纳米线光栅）材料堆叠有效热性质的FDTR技术，并建立可预测FDTR相位响应的解析模型，以便未来将该方法扩展用于探测声子平均自由程（MFP）相关的非扩散热输运对热导率的贡献。

Method: 建立了解析热扩散模型以预测FDTR相位响应，模型纳入泵浦与探针激光的有限光斑半径（高斯强度分布）以及纳米线光栅尺寸。在a-SiO2和c-蓝宝石衬底上制备宽度100至1000 nm的Au纳米线光栅进行测量以验证模型。

Result: 在这些衬底中声子平均自由程较短，整个纳米线宽度范围内热输运保持在扩散区。模型与测量在不同激光光斑半径和纳米线宽度下均表现出极佳一致性，得到的c-蓝宝石体热导率以及Au-Cr/a-SiO2和Au-Cr/c-蓝宝石界面的界面热导与先前测量和文献值相当。

Conclusion: 经验证的模型可作为稳健基线，将该方法扩展为测量有效热导率随加热器尺寸的变化，尤其适用于非扩散热输运区中可探测声子MFP对热导率贡献的材料。

Abstract: A frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR)-based technique is presented to measure the effective thermal properties of a material stack whose surface is patterned with metallic nanoline gratings that are heated and probed by lasers. An analytical heat diffusion model is derived for predicting the FDTR phase response that incorporates a finite laser spot radius with a Gaussian intensity profile for the pump and probe lasers, as well as the dimensions of the nanoline grating. The model is validated by comparing with measurements of Au nanoline gratings, with widths ranging from 100 to 1000 nm, fabricated on a-SiO$_2$ and $c$-sapphire substrates. Due to the short phonon mean free paths (MFPs) in these substrates, thermal transport remains in the diffusive regime for the whole range of nanoline widths. The model and the measurement show excellent agreement across different laser spot radii and nanoline widths, with a bulk thermal conductivity value of $c$-sapphire and the thermal boundary conductances at the Au-Cr/a-SiO$_2$ and Au-Cr/$c$-sapphire interfaces comparable to our prior measurements and the literature. The validated model will serve as a robust baseline for extending this method to measure effective thermal conductivity as a function of the heater size. This approach is particularly relevant for materials where non-diffusive thermal transport regimes enable probing phonon MFP dependent contributions to the thermal conductivity.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面形貌通常被视为薄膜质量的指标，一般认为更光滑的薄膜光电性质更均匀。本文旨在挑战这一假设，探究表面形貌演化是否必然伴随电子异质性的增大，并揭示形貌与电子均匀性之间可能存在的解耦关系。

Method: 采用由空间位阻型5-叔丁基菲啶阳离子衍生的低维Ruddlesden-Popper覆盖层修饰3D钙钛矿，将统计型原子力显微镜（AFM）分析与共聚焦、空间分辨光致发光（PL）光谱相结合，把表面形貌与微尺度光电性质（PL特征与准费米能级分裂QFLS）进行关联分析，并对比加速老化前后原始3D薄膜与覆盖异质结构的光电空间分布。

Result: 覆盖层虽然增加了表面粗糙度和高度不对称性，但并未增大电子不均匀性；相反，异质结构相比原始3D薄膜表现出增强的PL、降低的非辐射复合以及更均匀的辐射型QFLS空间分布。在加速老化下差异更为显著：原始薄膜出现明显的发射与QFLS空间涨落，而覆盖异质结构基本保持光电均匀性，表明低维覆盖层抑制了老化诱导的光电无序。

Conclusion: 低维覆盖层能够抑制老化诱导的光电无序，证明即使存在形貌不规则性，电子均匀性仍可得以维持。这种形貌与电子性质的解耦为开发耐久、高性能的钙钛矿光伏器件提供了一条设计原则。

Abstract: Surface morphology is widely considered an indicator of thin-film quality, with smoother films generally expected to exhibit more uniform optoelectronic properties. Here, we demonstrate that substantial surface evolution does not necessarily lead to increased electronic heterogeneity in mixed-dimensional perovskite heterostructures. We investigate how low-dimensional Ruddlesden-Popper capping layers derived from the sterically hindered 5-tert-butyl-phenanthridinium cation affect the spatial distributions of photoluminescence (PL) characteristics and quasi-Fermi level splitting (QFLS) in 3D perovskites. By combining statistical atomic force microscopy (AFM) analysis with confocal, spatially resolved PL spectroscopy, we correlate surface topography with microscale optoelectronic properties.
  Although capping increases surface roughness and height asymmetry, it does not increase electronic inhomogeneity. Instead, the heterostructures exhibit enhanced PL, reduced non-radiative recombination, and improved spatial uniformity of radiative QFLS compared with pristine 3D films. Under accelerated aging, this contrast becomes more pronounced: pristine films develop substantial spatial fluctuations in emission and QFLS, whereas capped heterostructures largely preserve their optoelectronic homogeneity. These findings indicate that the low-dimensional capping layer suppresses aging-induced optoelectronic disorder, demonstrating that electronic uniformity can be maintained despite morphological irregularity. This decoupling provides a design principle for developing durable, high-performance perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [93] [Thermal laser evaporation of superconducting Nb films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38070)
*Yifei Yan,David S. Catherall,Finley B. Donachie,Dorsa Najafipanah,Austin J. Minnich*

TL;DR: 本文报道利用热激光蒸发（TLE）在c面蓝宝石上沉积铌（Nb）薄膜，得到的薄膜室温电阻率21.60 μΩcm、残余电阻比5.71、超导转变温度9.24 K（仅比块体低10 mK）、转变宽度29 mK，证明TLE是一种适合难熔元素的高质量物理气相沉积技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热激光蒸发（TLE）是一种新兴的物理气相沉积技术，利用连续波激光可蒸发最难熔的元素。评估TLE沉积难熔超导元素薄膜的质量，对于验证该技术的实用性和可靠性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用自制的热激光蒸发系统，配备1070 nm连续波光纤激光器，以533 W激光功率聚焦至约500 μm直径的光斑作用于Nb靶材，在c面蓝宝石衬底上沉积Nb薄膜，并对薄膜的厚度、粗糙度、电阻率、残余电阻比及超导转变温度等性能进行表征。沉积速率达0.19 Å/s。

Result: 沉积得到厚度22.47 ± 0.05 nm、RMS粗糙度0.23 ± 0.02 nm的Nb薄膜，室温电阻率为21.60 ± 0.05 μΩcm，残余电阻比（RRR）为5.71，超导转变温度Tc为9.24 K（仅比块体值低10 mK），转变宽度为29 mK。在不加热衬底的条件下，该薄膜的电学和超导性能达到或超过其他沉积技术制备的同类薄膜。

Conclusion: 本工作进一步支持了TLE作为一种适用于难熔元素及化合物的多功能物理气相沉积方法的共识，证明其可在不加衬底加热的情况下制备出高质量的难熔超导薄膜。

Abstract: Thermal laser evaporation (TLE) is an emerging physical vapor deposition (PVD) technique that uses continuous-wave lasers to evaporate even the most refractory elements. The deposition and characterization of superconducting refractory elements are of interest to assess the quality of films deposited by TLE. Here, we report the deposition of niobium (Nb) on c-plane sapphire using a home-built TLE system equipped with a 1070 nm continuous-wave fiber laser. A deposition rate of 0.19 Å/s was achieved with 533 W of laser power focused to a diameter of approximately 500 $μ$m on a Nb target. A film with a thickness of $22.47 \pm 0.05$ nm and a root-mean-square (RMS) roughness of $0.23 \pm 0.02$ nm exhibited a room-temperature resistivity of $21.60 \pm 0.05$ $μΩ\mathrm{cm}$ and a residual-resistivity ratio (RRR) of 5.71. The superconducting transition temperature, $T_\mathrm{c}$, of the film was measured as 9.24 K, only 10 mK below the bulk value, with a transition width of 29 mK. Among films deposited without substrate heating, the electrical and superconducting properties of the present films meet or exceed those deposited by other techniques. This work contributes to the emerging consensus regarding TLE as a versatile PVD method for refractory elements and compounds.

</details>

*Daniel A. Bustamante Lopez,Deepankar Sri Gyan,Xiaojiang Li,Vladimir A. Stoica,Sujit Das,Peter Meisenheimer,Akash Surampalli,Mohit Tanwani,Xianghan Xu,Aiden Ross,Long-Qing Chen,Sang-Wook Cheong,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh,Venkatraman Gopalan,Wanzheng Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 文章报道了在自组装极性斯格明子中、中红外激发下发现的巨超快二次谐波产生（SHG），其瞬态强度表现出源于高阶非线性的独特功率依赖关系，并由此确立了自组装极性拓扑作为量子信息处理与高速光通信平台的潜力。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现自组装极性斯格明子在红外激发下产生巨且超快的SHG，瞬态SHG强度呈现源于高阶非线性的独特功率依赖；高阶非线性在极性超晶格的多种畴结构中普遍出现；相场模拟证实局域介电常数调制可满足epsilon-near-zero条件，从而大幅增强非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 自组装极性拓扑具有强本征光学非线性，可在无需光子工程的条件下解锁显著增强的非线性光学响应，为量子信息处理与高速光通信提供了一个有前景的材料平台。

Abstract: Giant optical nonlinearities enable advanced materials control and bridge exotic light-matter phenomena to next-generation photonics. However, materials exhibiting strong higher-order optical nonlinearity beyond the second order are typically rare. Here, we report the discovery of a giant and ultrafast second-harmonic generation (SHG) in self-assembled polar skyrmions under mid-infrared excitations. The transient SHG intensity exhibits a distinctive power dependence originating from higher-order nonlinearity. More generally, we show that large higher-order nonlinearity emerges across diverse domain structures in polar superlattices. Phase-field simulations demonstrate a strong and periodic local permittivity variation within each polar texture at equilibrium, and further confirm the fast polarization oscillation in polar skyrmions with mid-infrared excitations. This intense local permittivity modulation can satisfy epsilon-near-zero conditions, unlocking dramatically enhanced nonlinear optical responses without photonic engineering. Consequently, the self-assembled polar topologies with strong inherent optical nonlinearity could establish a platform for quantum information processing and high-speed optical communications.

### [95] [Ordered patches cap the stability of disordered frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38501)
*Joohan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究证明：无序虽能提升框架在静水压力下的稳定性，但其中的有序区域限制了这一增益。一旦有序团块受力超过晶体的可局域化失稳阈值，框架就无法保持线性稳定；而均质正定性丧失若不发生局域化，则可留下稳定窗口，但其前提是应变保持晶体对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究结构无序能否、以及在多大程度上保护框架结构免于压力诱发的失效，并厘清其中有序区域（晶态团块）对稳定性增益的约束机制。

Method: 在刚性节点框架受静水压力作用的前提下，构造含任意大、正密度的晶体团块系综（团块中心携带按比例缩放的晶体预应力），分析其线性稳定性；将声学判据推广至含预应力情形，逐点群分析稳定窗口所需的应变对称性条件；并以二维 Truchet 框架与 876 个细长杆网为实例进行数值验证。

Result: 受应力超过晶体局域化失稳阈值的晶体团块必然导致框架失稳；均质正定性丧失且所有 Bloch 模（含声学极限）保持稳定时不会局域化，留下稳定窗口；该窗口对所有点群都要求应变保持晶体对称性。Truchet 框架中植入有序畴使阈值逼近理论上限（差距在 1% 以内），短程有序簇上发生局域化失效；876 个细长杆网中 798 个首先因可局域化失稳而失效，其余 78 个稳定窗口中位数为 7.6%。

Conclusion: 无序带来的稳定性增益受其内部局部有序所容许的可局域化失稳所封顶；除非团块与应力屏蔽，否则无序无法保护晶体免于破坏其对称性的失效，对立方晶体至多只能抵御体积坍缩。

Abstract: Structural disorder can stabilize frameworks against pressure-induced failure, yet the ordered regions it contains limit this gain. We show that, in rigid-jointed frames under hydrostatic pressure, an ensemble containing arbitrarily large patches of a crystal at positive density, whose centres carry a scaled crystal prestress, cannot stay linearly stable once those patches are stressed beyond the crystal's threshold for localizable instabilities. A homogeneous loss of positive definiteness while all Bloch modes, including the acoustic limit, remain stable cannot localize, leaving a window for stabilization. Extending an acoustic criterion to prestress, we show for all point groups that such a window requires a strain that keeps the crystal's symmetry. Unless the patches are shielded from stress, disorder cannot protect a crystal against a failure that breaks its symmetry, and protects a cubic crystal at most against volume collapse. In two-dimensional Truchet frames, planted ordered domains bring the threshold within 1% of the bound, and correlations short of long-range order localize failure on ordered clusters. Across 876 slender-strut nets, 798 fail first by a localizable instability; the other 78 have a median window of 7.6%. Stability gained through disorder is capped by the localizable instabilities of the local order it permits.

</details>


### [96] [Crystoria: A Large-Scale Crystal Dataset Linking Thermodynamic and Dynamical Stability for Materials AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38195)
*Ke-Xin Pang,Ze-Feng Gao,Yan Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍Crystoria，一个包含264万余晶体结构的大规模数据集，旨在将动力学稳定性表征扩展到现代材料数据的规模，其中67.68%被判定为动力学稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模晶体数据库使数百万结构与热力学性质可用于材料信息学，但动力学稳定的表征因计算代价远高于常规结构与能量计算而仍缺乏可比规模的数据。这一不匹配导致迅速扩张的晶体结构空间与可获得的物理信息之间存在关键缺口，制约了材料研究与机器学习。

Method: 系统筛选低能量与亚稳无机晶体，标准化结构身份，并将机器学习加速的结构弛豫与基于声子的动力学筛选相结合，构建大规模晶体数据集。

Result: Crystoria包含2,649,640个晶体结构，覆盖89种元素、230种空间群中的219种，以及含1-48个原子的原胞；其中1,793,353个结构（67.68%）被分类为动力学稳定，并揭示动力学稳定性在化学与晶体学空间中的显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过将动力学稳定性扩展到数百万晶体结构的规模，Crystoria为材料人工智能提供了物理可靠的基础，并支持对动力学晶体景观的系统性探索。

Abstract: Large-scale crystal databases have transformed materials discovery by making millions of structures and thermodynamic properties accessible to materials informatics. Yet dynamical stability remains sparsely characterized at a comparable scale because its evaluation is substantially more computationally demanding than conventional structure and energy calculations. This mismatch creates a critical gap between the rapidly expanding crystal-structure space and the physical information available for materials research and machine learning. Here we introduce Crystoria, a large-scale crystal dataset designed to bring dynamical stability to the scale of modern materials data. We construct Crystoria by systematically curating low-energy and metastable inorganic crystals, standardizing their structural identities, and combining machine-learning-accelerated structural relaxation with phonon-based dynamical screening. The resulting dataset contains 2,649,640 crystal structures, spanning 89 elements, 219 of the 230 space groups, and primitive cells containing 1-48 atoms. Among them, 1,793,353 structures (67.68%) are classified as dynamically stable, revealing substantial variation in dynamical stability across chemical and crystallographic space. By bringing dynamical stability to the scale of millions of crystal structures, Crystoria provides a physically grounded foundation for materials artificial intelligence (AI) and enables systematic exploration of the dynamical crystal landscape.

</details>


### [97] [Finite-Temperature Magnetism and Longitudinal Spin Transport in Compensated Half-Metal Ferrimagnets with Near-Zero Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38302)
*Jiahao Shentu,Jie Li,Jianting Dong,Meng Zhu,Chen Wang,Qing Zhang,Yizhuo Song,Wenqi Sun,Jia Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al的电子结构、有限温度磁性及自旋输运性质，证明其为0 K下100%自旋极化且磁矩完全补偿的半金属FCF，并在Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结中获得0 K巨大隧穿磁电阻、300 K仍高达232%的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全补偿亚铁磁体（FCF）具有零温近零磁矩、完美自旋极化电子结构与超快自旋动力学，可最小化杂散场并降低翻转电流，是实现高速、低功耗自旋电子器件的重要候选材料。但针对此类半金属FCF在磁隧道结中的有限温度磁性与自旋输运性能尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 以Heusler型全补偿半金属亚铁磁体Mn3Al为研究对象，结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与蒙特卡罗模拟：首先计算其电子结构，确认半金属性与完全补偿磁性；随后表征有限温度磁性质；最后预测Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结的隧穿磁电阻效应。

Result: Mn3Al为半金属FCF，0 K下具有100%自旋极化和完全补偿的磁化；300 K时净磁化低于11.6 emu/cc，说明其近补偿亚铁磁有序在室温下保持稳健；Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al磁隧道结在0 K呈现巨大隧穿磁电阻，且在300 K仍高达232%。

Conclusion: Mn3Al及同类半金属全补偿亚铁磁体是面向磁电阻型自旋电子应用的优秀候选材料，为高速、低功耗自旋电子器件的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fully compensated ferrimagnets, characterized by nearly vanishing magnetic moments at zero temperature, perfect spin-polarized electronic structures, and ultrafast spin dynamics, represent a highly promising materials platform for next-generation spintronic devices. By minimizing stray magnetic fields and reducing switching currents, FCFs enable a viable path toward high-speed, low-power spintronic devices. In this work, we present a systematic investigation of the electronic structure, finite-temperature magnetism, and spin-dependent transport properties of the Heusler-type fully compensated half-metallic ferrimagnet Mn3Al in magnetic tunnel junctions. We demonstrate that Mn3Al is a half-metallic FCF with 100% spin polarization and fully compensated magnetization at 0 K. Combining density functional theory with Monte Carlo simulations, we characterize its finite-temperature magnetic properties and show that Mn3Al exhibits a net magnetization below 11.6 emu/cc at 300 K, confirming the robustness of its nearly compensated ferrimagnetic order at room temperature. Furthermore, we predict that the Mn3Al/MgO/Mn3Al MTJ yields a giant tunneling magnetoresistance effect at 0 K, which remains as high as 232% at 300 K. Our results establish Mn3Al and related half-metallic FCFs as excellent candidates for magnetoresistance-based spintronic applications.

</details>


### [98] [First-Principles Identification of Higher-Order Configurational Energy Terms and Their Magnetic-State Dependence in Lithium-Rich Oxyfluorides: From QUBO to HUBO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38321)
*Joonho Bae*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文利用自旋极化 DFT 发现锂富集氧氟化物中存在显著的、QUBO 无法表示的三阶非加和构型能量贡献，并验证了将模型从 QUBO 扩展到 HUBO 可显著降低拟合误差，同时指出能量映射对磁态具有依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QUBO 模型只能表示二次（成对）相互作用，无法刻画无序岩盐正极材料构型能量中的高阶非加和贡献，因此需要探索将其扩展至高阶无约束二值优化（HUBO）的必要性与可行性。

Method: 采用自旋极化密度泛函理论（DFT），在两种无序岩盐正极材料 Li₂MnO₂F（LMOF）和 Li₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅O₂F（LMTiOF）上构建保组分的 DFT 能量映射；在固定几何构型下比较 QUBO 与三次 HUBO 的拟合表现，并通过直接 DFT 能量差验证三阶非加和性；同时比较不同磁初始化下的结果以考察磁态依赖性。

Result: 固定几何能量映射表明，三次 HUBO 能够捕捉 QUBO 所缺失的非加和贡献，并在两种材料中大幅降低拟合误差；直接 DFT 能量差证实了第三阶非加和性的存在；磁初始化比较显示 LMTiOF 的贡献强烈依赖于收敛后的电子态。所提取的项描述的是保组分交换变量之间的有效耦合，而非唯一确定的微观三原子相互作用。

Conclusion: 在构建构型能量模型时应纳入高阶项，同时在建立 DFT 能量映射时必须考虑磁态依赖性。QUBO 到 HUBO 的扩展对于准确刻画锂富集氧氟化物无序岩盐正极的构型能量学是必要且有效的。

Abstract: In this work, we use spin-polarized density functional theory (DFT) to identify substantial third-order, nonadditive configurational energy contributions in lithium-rich oxyfluorides that quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) models cannot represent. We test the extension from QUBO to higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) in two disordered-rocksalt cathode materials, Li$_2$MnO$_2$F (LMOF) and Li$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$O$_2$F (LMTiOF), using composition-preserving DFT energy maps. Using fixed-geometry energy maps, we show that cubic HUBO captures nonadditive contributions absent from QUBO models and substantially reduces fitting errors in both materials. Direct DFT energy differences confirm third-order nonadditivity, while comparisons of magnetic initializations reveal a strong dependence of the LMTiOF contribution on the converged electronic state. These results support the inclusion of higher-order terms when modeling configurational energetics and highlight the need to account for magnetic-state dependence in constructing DFT energy maps. The extracted terms describe effective couplings among composition-preserving exchange variables, rather than uniquely identified microscopic three-atom interactions.

</details>


### [99] [Strain-Induced BCC-to-FCC Transformation in Tungsten Uncovered by Molecular Dynamics Simulations with Machine Learning Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38477)
*Qiu-Hong Hu,YueYue Wang,Xinyi Leng,Hezhu Shao,Luru Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用经严格验证的机器学习势分子动力学模拟，作者证明应变可诱导钨发生BCC→FCC多晶型转变，FCC相含量在低温和存在位错时可超过20 at.%，且转变遵循Bain和Nishiyama-Wassermann取向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨在常规实验中未观察到多晶型转变，但这可能只是由于常规实验难以达到所需的力学/热力学条件，而非钨本质上不能发生相变。研究旨在证明应变诱导BCC→FCC转变在钨中确实可行，从而为制备高延展性的双相钨提供依据，解决钨高温结构应用中长期存在的延展性难题。

Method: 采用经过严格验证的机器学习势（MLP）进行分子动力学（MD）模拟，研究钨在应变加载下的BCC→FCC相变行为；分别考察完美晶体以及在位错存在条件下、不同温度下的转变动力学，并通过结构分析确认相变遵循的取向关系。

Result: 模拟表明应变可诱导钨发生BCC→FCC转变；在低温和存在位错时FCC相含量可超过20 at.%；在完美晶体中，中等温度有利于相变动力学；结构分析证实转变遵循Bain和Nishiyama-Wassermann取向关系。

Conclusion: 研究确立了钨中应变诱导多晶型转变的可行性，为实验制备具有增强延展性的双相钨提供了关键指导，有望解决钨在高温结构应用中的长期挑战。

Abstract: Polymorphic transformations occur in a minority of metals. Tungsten is not one of them. This observation does not rule out its intrinsic phase transition possibility, but may be due to inaccessible thermodynamic and mechanical conditions in conventional experiments. Herein, we investigate the body centered cubic (BCC) to face centered cubic (FCC) transformation in tungsten via a rigorously validated machine learning potential (MLP) molecular dynamics (MD) approach. Our simulations demonstrate that strain induced BCC to FCC transformation indeed occurs in tungsten. The FCC phase exceeds 20 at.% at low temperature and in the presence of dislocations. In the perfect crystal moderate temperatures favor phase transformation kinetics. Structural analysis confirms the transformation following the Bain and Nishiyama Wassermann orientation relationship. These results establish the viability of strain induced polymorphic transformation in tungsten and provide a critical guideline for the experimental production of duplex tungsten with enhanced ductility addressing a longstanding challenge in tungsten material engineering for high-temperature structural applications.

</details>


### [100] [Two-Dimensional Magnetic Materials from Ferromagnetism to Altermagnetism: Fabrication, Symmetry, Evidence, and Device Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38491)
*Guojian Zhao,Ziang Meng,Li Liu,Zhiyuan Duan,Jingyu Li,Sixu Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan,Shiheng Bu,Chen Liang,Xiaoning Wang,Peixin Qin,Zhiqi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述以净磁化和动量空间自旋劈裂为坐标，系统梳理二维磁性材料，提出 M1–M4 报告分类，并评估其器件就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁性受成分、厚度、堆叠、应变、缺陷、衬底、铁性序及界面等多种因素共同支配，导致相归属与器件评估缺乏统一且可靠的分类和判据。

Method: 以净磁化与动量空间自旋劈裂/自旋极化图样为主坐标，辅以亚晶格结构及相反自旋亚晶格的对称关系，提出 M1（层分辨原子级薄层）、M2（非层分辨薄片/薄膜）、M3（块体基准）、M4（理论候选/设计路线）四类报告体系，并独立记录证据类别与有序温度来源；据此比较铁磁、亚铁磁、反铁磁与交变磁体的层分辨形式，并对器件就绪度作定性比较。

Result: 当前交变磁性的实验支持主要来自 M2 薄膜和 M3 块体基准，完全验证的原子级薄 M1 交变磁相仍是开放的实验目标；多数提出的单层/少层体系属于 M4，存在争议的案例可指明相归属所需的测量。器件就绪度按工作温度、读出、开关、磁畴控制、稳定性、可扩展性、界面敏感性与集成难度等维度存在差异。

Conclusion: 可靠的相归属与器件评估需对同一材料形态在匹配条件下进行互补测量，最好针对同一样品或原位进行；实现完全验证的 M1 交变磁相是二维磁学有待突破的关键实验目标。

Abstract: Atomically thin magnets are governed by composition, thickness, stacking, strain, defects, substrates, ferroic order, and interfaces. This Review organizes two-dimensional (2D) magnetism using net magnetization and momentum-space spin splitting or spin-polarization patterns as primary coordinates, supplemented by sublattice structure and the symmetry relations between opposite-spin sublattices. Ferromagnets generally carry a net moment; ferrimagnets can be fully compensated. Conventional antiferromagnets may retain spin-degenerate bands, while altermagnets permit nonrelativistic alternating spin splitting under dimension-specific symmetry constraints. M1-M4 reporting classes introduced here distinguish layer-resolved atomically thin forms, non-layer-resolved thin flakes or films, bulk benchmarks, and theoretical candidates or design routes; the evidence category and ordering-temperature provenance are reported independently. Current experimental support for altermagnetism comes mainly from M2 thin-film and M3 bulk benchmarks, leaving a fully verified atomically thin M1 altermagnetic phase as an open experimental goal. Most proposed monolayer and few-layer systems therefore fall within M4, and contested cases clarify the measurements needed for phase assignment. Device readiness is compared qualitatively using operating temperature, readout, switching, domain control, stability, scalability, interface sensitivity, and integration difficulty. Reliable phase assignment and device assessment require complementary measurements from the same material form under matched conditions, ideally on the same specimen or in situ.

</details>


### [101] [Reciprocal transverse Seebeck and Peltier effects in MoSi2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38506)
*Aina Sakamoto,Shoya Ohsumi,Shogo Yoshida,Ryuji Okazaki,Yoshiki J. Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在具有轴依赖导电极性的非磁性单晶 MoSi2 中，实验证实了横向下 Seebeck 效应与横向 Peltier 效应这对互逆过程，首次在同一均匀材料中直接对比并确立了无需工程界面的横向热电互转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向热电转换（电荷流与热流在正交方向互转）是纵向热电转换的替代方案，但横向 Peltier 效应（横向 Seebeck 效应的逆过程）研究较少，且在同一均匀材料中直接对比两种效应的研究仍然有限。

Method: 选用 MoSi2 单晶（轴依赖导电极性材料，Seebeck 系数符号随晶轴变化）。通过多端电压测量确定感生内电场方向，以确立横向 Seebeck 响应；并在施加交流电流下利用锁相热成像直接观测与横向 Peltier 效应对应的横向温度调制。
