We explore experimentally the two transverse optical regimes of cubic Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te (x<0.7), with ab initio calculations in support: (1) the phonon-polariton regime at quasi-infinite (photon-like) wavelengths averaging over the alloy disorder, probed by forward-Raman scattering, and (2) the purely-mechanical phonon regime down to minimum wavelengths nearly matching the lattice constant zooming into the alloy disorder, achieved by inelastic neutron scattering. The short Zn-Te bond is found to exhibit a compact optical duo distinguishing homo from hetero local bond environments (1 bond: 2 mode), as explained by the percolation model. This deviates from the historical Zn(1-x)Mn(x)Te MREI (modified-random-element-isodisplacement) scheme inspired from classical Raman and infrared studies, in which the optical modes are blind to the bond environment (1-bond: 1-mode). So, the test is positive, reinforcing the status of the percolation scheme as a robust decryptor of (even small) lattice distortions induced by alloying through the lattice dynamics.

</details>


### [134] [Néel vector switching by magnetization-dependent layer-resolved spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40215)
*Sheng Pan,Weiwei Wang,Jie-Xiang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 文章通过第一性原理计算研究了反铁磁拓扑绝缘体MnBi2Te4的层分辨自旋轨道矩，发现其自旋霍尔电导由磁化无关项与两个随Néel矢量演化的自旋反常霍尔项组成，可实现有限七重层薄膜中Néel矢量的无场可逆翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用层分辨自旋轨道矩实现反铁磁序的电流驱动控制，并通过局部自旋-电荷转换与层/界面工程定制翻转行为。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算MnBi2Te4各层的自旋霍尔电导，将其分解为磁化无关自旋霍尔项与两个对Néel矢量偶对称的自旋反常霍尔项，并分析表面层与内层因局域反演不对称性造成的差异，进而模拟有限七重层薄膜中Néel矢量的翻转。

Result: 层分辨自旋霍尔电导包含磁化无关项和两个随局域磁序演化的自旋反常霍尔项；局域反演不对称使表面层与内层响应不同；该响应能够实现有限七重层薄膜中Néel矢量的可逆、无场翻转；自旋反常霍尔项提供磁化依赖的反馈，重新分配层分辨力矩并重塑翻转轨迹。

Conclusion: 层分辨自旋轨道矩为电流驱动反铁磁序翻转提供了有效途径，磁化无关自旋霍尔项足以单独实现翻转，而自旋反常霍尔项通过磁化依赖反馈优化翻转动力学，为层与界面工程定制器件性能奠定基础。

Abstract: Layer-resolved spin-orbit torques offer a route to controlling antiferromagnetic order through local spin-charge conversion. First-principles calculations show that the layer-resolved spin Hall conductivity of the antiferromagnetic topological insulator $\mathrm{Mn{Bi}_{2}{Te}_{4}}$ consists of a magnetization-independent spin Hall term plus two spin anomalous Hall terms that are even in the Néel vector yet evolve with the local magnetic order, while local inversion asymmetry differentiates surface and inner layers. This response enables reversible, field-free switching of the Néel vector in finite-septuple-layer films. Although the magnetization-independent spin Hall term alone can reverse the order, the spin anomalous Hall terms provide magnetization-dependent feedback that redistributes the layer-resolved torques and reshapes the switching trajectory, offering a route to tailoring current-driven switching through layer and interface engineering.

</details>


### [135] [Large Magnetocaloric Effect and Adiabatic Temperature Change in the Tellurate: Gd2TeO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40296)
*Abhijit Nayak,Gurunathan Angusamy,Kusampal Yadav,S. K. Panda,Devajyoti Mukherjee,Raivo Stern,Tanmoy Chakrabarty,Krishnamurthy Jyothinagaram*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究多晶Gd2TeO6的结构、磁性、热力学与磁热性能，发现其在低温下无长程磁有序，磁熵变接近理论极限，是极具潜力的低温磁制冷材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索尚未被用于低温磁制冷的稀土碲酸盐Gd2TeO6，以寻找高性能的低温磁制冷候选材料，弥合低温冷却应用对高效磁热材料的需求。

Method: 采用粉末X射线衍射确认晶体结构；通过磁化率、等温磁化测量与Curie-Weiss拟合表征磁性；利用比热测量及Debye-Einstein分析分离晶格与磁贡献；结合GGA+U第一性原理计算研究竞争反铁磁态；并评估磁熵变与绝热温变等磁热性能。

Result: Gd2TeO6为正交晶系（P212121），含两个晶体学上不同的七配位Gd位点；2 K以上无常规长程磁有序，约4 K以下出现低场磁不可逆；Weiss温度为-1.25 K，表明弱反铁磁相互作用为主；7 T时磁化达理论饱和值的97%且几乎无磁滞。2.5 K下，0-2、0-5、0-7 T的磁熵变分别为21.40、48.51、54.81 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹，体积熵变达400.50 mJ cm⁻³ K⁻¹；14 T时磁熵变为63.51 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹，接近理论极限64.25 J kg⁻¹ K⁻¹；0-7 T绝热温变约22.7 K。

Conclusion: Gd2TeO6具有近理论极限的磁熵变、大绝热温变和可忽略的磁滞，且第一性原理计算揭示其存在竞争反铁磁态，表明它是一种极具前景的低温磁制冷候选材料。

Abstract: We investigate the structural, magnetic, thermodynamic, and magnetocaloric properties of polycrystalline Gd2TeO6, a rare-earth tellurate unexplored for cryogenic magnetic refrigeration. Powder X-ray diffraction confirms single-phase orthorhombic symmetry (P212121), featuring two crystallographically distinct seven-coordinate Gd sites. Magnetization measurements reveal no conventional long-range magnetic ordering down to 2 K, although low-field magnetic irreversibility emerges below approximately 4 K. Curie-Weiss analysis yields a Weiss temperature of -1.25 K, indicating weak predominantly antiferromagnetic interactions. The magnetization reaches approximately 97% of the theoretical saturation moment at 7 T with negligible hysteresis. Heat-capacity measurements reveal no sharp zero-field anomaly above 2 K, while a broad field-induced maximum indicates redistribution of low-energy magnetic entropy. Debye-Einstein analysis separates the lattice and magnetic contributions. At 2.5 K, the maximum magnetic entropy changes (-DeltaSM) reach 21.40, 48.51, and 54.81 J kg-1 K-1 for field changes of 0-2, 0-5, and 0-7 T, respectively. The corresponding volumetric entropy change reaches 400.50 mJ cm-3 K-1. Measurements up to 14 T yield (-DeltaSM) = 63.51 J kg-1 K-1, approaching the theoretical limit of 64.25 J kg-1 K-1. The estimated adiabatic temperature change is approximately 22.7 K for 0-7 T. First-principles calculations with GGA+U approximation reveal competing antiferromagnetic states, highlighting Gd2TeO6 as a promising candidate for cryogenic magnetocaloric applications.

</details>


### [136] [Soliton-Lattice Origin of Incommensurability in High-Pressure Antimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40399)
*M. Smirman,K. Sun,H. Sheng,I. I. Mazin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Sb-II高压主客相的不公度性源于孤子晶格：局域化的主客错位装饰在4/3公度参考结构上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主客晶体中不公度性的微观起源长期未解，作者旨在揭示Sb-II高压主客相不公度结构的微观机制。

Method: 利用第一性原理计算揭示波纹状且具迟滞性的错位势能面，据此建立Frenkel-Kontorova模型，描述形如(4n+1)/(3n+1)的孤子晶格近似结构；再用机器学习势计算这些近似结构、用第一性原理弛豫验证局域错位核心，并在有限温度下考察孤子晶格的热展宽行为。

Result: 计算表明孤子间距存在能量最优值，局域错位核心得到第一性原理验证；有限温度下热展宽的孤子晶格沿客链呈现原子密度波，与实验衍射结果一致。

Conclusion: 孤子错位图像为Sb-II中主客不公度性提供了微观机制，阐明了不公度性源于调制公度参考结构的局域错位缺陷。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of incommensurability in host-guest crystals remains unresolved. Here we show that the high-pressure antimony host-guest phase Sb-II is a soliton lattice, where localized guest-host misregistries decorate a
  commensurate 4/3 reference structure. First-principles calculations reveal a corrugated and hysteretic registry landscape, motivating a Frenkel-Kontorova description that accounts for soliton-lattice approximants of the form
  $(4n+1)/(3n+1)$. Machine-learning-potential calculations of these approximants show an energetically preferred soliton spacing, and their localized misregistry cores are also verified by first-principles relaxations. At finite
  temperature, the thermally broadened soliton lattice appears as an atomic density wave along the guest chains, consistent with experimental diffraction. This soliton-registry picture provides a microscopic mechanism for host-guest
  incommensurability in Sb-II.

</details>


### [137] [Light-induced chiral altermagnets from conventional antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40410)
*Jorge Cardenas-Gamboa,Devika Jayadeep,Aritz Leonardo,Gregory A. Fiete,Jeroen van den Brink,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过相干晶格控制（非线性声子学）在传统反铁磁体中光学诱导交变磁性（altermagnetism）的方案，并以Fe₂TeO₆为原型验证了线偏振中红外脉冲驱动瞬态手性畸变从而产生非相对论自旋劈裂的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体将补偿共线磁序与动量依赖的非相对论自旋劈裂相结合，为自旋电子学提供了新机遇；然而目前缺乏利用晶格自由度实现其超快光学调控的系统途径。作者希望通过对称性引导的相干声子工程，在常规反铁磁体中可逆地"打开"交变磁性。

Method: 首先进行对称性分析，筛选出既能解除传统反铁磁体自旋简并、又保留连接相反自旋子晶格的旋转对称性的声子畸变模式，其中几何手性声子将交变磁性的出现与结构手性联系起来。随后以Fe₂TeO₆为原型，利用红外活性声子与光学沉默、破缺对称模式之间的负双二次耦合（negative biquadratic coupling），由线偏振中红外脉冲驱动结构失稳。结合基于第一性原理的晶格动力学模拟与电子结构计算，验证光诱导瞬态手性畸变及随之产生的非相对论自旋劈裂。

Result: 晶格动力学揭示了光诱导下瞬态手性畸变的出现；电子结构计算证实，光诱导的非平衡结构展现出交变磁性所特有的非相对论自旋劈裂，从而在原理上实现了铁碲氧（Fe₂TeO₆）中交变态的超快开关。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一条对称性引导的、通过非线性声子学实现交变磁态超快光学控制的路径，为交变磁体的动态调控与器件应用奠定了理论框架。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine compensated collinear magnetic order with momentum-dependent nonrelativistic spin splitting, offering new opportunities for spintronic applications. Here, we propose a route to optically induce altermagnetism through coherent lattice control. Using symmetry analysis, we identify phonon distortions that lift the spin degeneracy of conventional antiferromagnets while preserving the rotational symmetries connecting opposite-spin sublattices. Among these, geometric chiral phonons provide a particularly appealing pathway, linking the emergence of altermagnetism to structural chirality. Taking Fe$_2$TeO$_6$ as a prototype, we demonstrate that a linearly polarized mid-infrared pulse can drive a structural instability through negative biquadratic coupling between an infrared-active phonon and an optically silent, symmetry-breaking mode. First-principles-based lattice dynamics reveal the emergence of a transient chiral distortion, while electronic-structure calculations establish that the light-induced structure exhibits nonrelativistic spin splitting characteristic of altermagnetism. Our results establish a symmetry-guided pathway toward the ultrafast optical control of altermagnetic states through nonlinear phononics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [A $B_{1g}$ phonon unlocks $d$-wave superconductivity in the repulsive Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37746)
*Wen O. Wang,Tianshu Li,Xiaoyue Ma,Rong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Shruti Agarwal,Benjamin Cohen-Stead,Emily Z. Zhang,Brian Moritz,Steven Johnston,Zhi-Xun Shen,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用数值精确的有限温量子蒙特卡洛方法，作者证明当掺杂的 t-t' 排斥 Hubbard 模型耦合一个 B1g 声子时，会产生真正的 d 波超导，且转变温度显著高于单一相互作用的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探寻一种物理上合理的微观机制，能够在 Hubbard 模型中实现稳健且具有较高转变温度的 d 波超导，并为进一步提高超导转变温度指明方向。

Method: 采用数值精确的有限温量子蒙特卡洛方法，在掺杂的 t-t' 排斥 Hubbard 模型中引入 B1g 声子耦合，研究超导配对对称性、转变温度及其随掺杂、Hubbard 排斥强度和声子频率的变化规律。

Result: B1g 声子与 Hubbard 排斥的协同作用产生真正的 d 波超导，转变温度超过任一单独相互作用；更强的 Hubbard 排斥可在可及范围内与声子合作进一步提高配对尺度；结果按延迟尺度 2πT/Ω 组织时，在不同声子频率下遵循共同趋势；并在掺杂上呈现明确的超导穹顶结构。

Conclusion: B1g 声子是 Hubbard 模型中实现稳健 d 波超导的一个合理微观要素，并提出了一条具体、物理上有依据的提高超导转变温度的路线。

Abstract: Using numerically exact finite-temperature quantum Monte Carlo, we demonstrate that a $B_{1g}$ phonon coupled to the doped $t$-$t'$ repulsive Hubbard model produces genuine $d$-wave superconductivity at a substantial transition temperature, in excess of either interaction alone. The observed trends suggest that stronger Hubbard repulsion may cooperate with the phonon to further raise the pairing scale. The enhancement persists at stronger repulsion within the accessible window, limited by the fermion sign problem, and follows a common trend across phonon frequencies $Ω$ when organized by a retardation scale $2πT/Ω$. We also find a well-defined superconducting dome in doping, echoing a characteristic feature of correlated superconductors. Our results identify the $B_{1g}$ phonon as a plausible microscopic ingredient for robust $d$-wave superconductivity in the Hubbard model and suggest a concrete, physically motivated route toward materials with higher superconducting transition temperatures.

</details>


### [139] [Parity-Resolved Many-Body Kinetics of a Topological Qubit under Nonequilibrium Phonon Pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37793)
*Han Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究电离辐射产生的非平衡声子脉冲对由两根2μm邻近化纳米线构成的Majorana量子比特的影响，通过结合晶格Lutchyn-Oreg模型、微观电-声子耦合与宇称分辨多体动力学，追踪声子引起的激发、弛豫与逻辑相干性损失，并给出抑制退相干的设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中，电离辐射产生的声子爆发会引发关联错误，威胁Majorana量子比特的拓扑保护与相干性，因此需要定量理解非平衡声子如何导致泄漏、准粒子中毒和相干性损失。

Method: 采用由两根2μm邻近化纳米线构成的Majorana量子比特，结合晶格Lutchyn-Oreg模型、微观电-声子耦合以及宇称分辨的多体动力学，追踪声子脉冲作用下的激发、弛豫和逻辑相干性；并与匹配线隙和Majorana衰减长度的Kitaev链比较，同时研究磁场、化学势和混合态半导体权重的影响。

Result: 无准粒子逃逸时，复合在约130ns内恢复初始Majorana占据，但激发与恢复过程中交换的声子会保留逻辑态信息，造成永久相干性损失；对镜像对称线，每根线的分式相干损失为两逻辑态平均激发概率的0.75–1.0倍（即使Majorana分裂为零），破坏镜像对称可在小分裂下抑制退相干；因子化Boltzmann动力学忽略准粒子关联，会高估残余宇称翻转。在相同沉积能量密度下，不同声子谱给出不同激发概率与相干损失。低电子密度抑制高能声子激发，而高密度控制中硬壁边界使1meV中心脉冲的激发效率比线提高5×10^4倍；磁场和化学势调谐通过改变自旋和电子-空穴结构产生不同声子灵敏度；当隧穿耦合等于母超导隙时，计入混合态半导体权重使近阈值激发率降低四倍。

Conclusion: 该框架将声子谱与泄漏、准粒子中毒和相干性损失联系起来，可用于指导Majorana器件的设计，表明镜像对称性破坏、电子密度、边界条件以及材料参数调谐对声子诱导退相干有重要影响。

Abstract: Phonon bursts from ionizing radiation cause correlated errors in superconducting quantum processors. We study prescribed nonequilibrium phonon pulses in a Majorana qubit formed by two $2\,μ\mathrm{m}$ proximitized nanowires. Combining the lattice Lutchyn--Oreg model, microscopic electron--phonon coupling, and parity-resolved many-body dynamics, we track excitation, relaxation, and logical coherence. Without quasiparticle escape, recombination restores the initial Majorana occupations in about 130 ns, while phonons exchanged during excitation and recovery can retain logical-state information, leaving permanent coherence loss. For mirror-symmetric wires, the fractional coherence loss per wire is 0.75--1.0 times the excitation probability averaged over the two logical states, even at zero Majorana splitting; breaking mirror symmetry can suppress dephasing at small splitting. Factorized Boltzmann kinetics neglects quasiparticle correlations and overestimates residual parity flips. At equal deposited energy density, phonon spectra yield different excitation probabilities and coherence losses. Comparisons with Kitaev chains matched to the wire's gap and Majorana decay length reveal that low electron density suppresses excitation by energetic phonons, whereas hard-wall boundaries in a high-density control increase excitation efficiency by a factor of $5\times10^4$ relative to the wire for pulses centered at 1 meV. Magnetic-field and chemical-potential tuning yield different phonon sensitivities at the same bulk gap through changes in spin and electron--hole structure. Including hybrid states' semiconductor weights reduces near-threshold excitation rates fourfold when the tunnel coupling equals the parent superconducting gap. Our framework connects phonon spectra to leakage, poisoning, and coherence loss to guide Majorana-device design.

</details>


### [140] [Influences of $N_2$ flow rate on the superconducting properties and dimensionality of epitaxial TiN thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38250)
*Shilong Chen,Yuchuan Liu,Yihui Sun,Xiaoni Wang,Wei Peng,Yu Wu,Gang Mu,Zhirong Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究在MgO衬底上外延生长了35 nm厚的TiN超导薄膜，系统研究了N₂流量和本底真空度对薄膜表面形貌、超导转变温度(Tc)、剩余电阻率比(RRR)、正常态电阻率(ρn)、上临界场及超导维度的影响，发现1.5 sccm N₂流量下薄膜性能最优，并首次揭示样品质量优化会导致面内上临界场偏离二维超导行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TiN薄膜因化学/热稳定性好、电导率优异而具有低损耗特性，在超导量子计算领域备受关注。然而，生长参数（尤其是N₂流量和真空度）与超导性能之间的系统关系，以及超导维度的影响因素，仍缺乏全面理解。

Method: 采用直流反应磁控溅射技术，在MgO衬底上外延生长35 nm厚TiN薄膜；表征了晶体结构、膜厚和表面形貌；系统研究了N₂流量对本底真空度、表面形貌、超导转变温度Tc、剩余电阻率比RRR、正常态电阻率ρn、上临界场及超导维度的影响。

Result: 薄膜表面非常平整，均方根粗糙度仅零点几纳米。N₂流量为1.5 sccm时性能最优：Tc最高(4.93 K)、RRR最大、ρn最低、面外上临界场在Tc附近的斜率最低。此外，本底真空度对超导性能影响显著，真空度退化会降低Tc。上临界场表现出显著的各向异性；面内上临界场数据表明，样品质量优化会导致偏离二维超导行为，表明超导维度不仅由膜厚决定、还受样品质量影响。

Conclusion: 本研究为全面理解生长参数与薄膜超导性能之间的关系提供了有益参考，并提出了超导维度除膜厚以外还受样品质量影响的新视角，对超导量子计算中高性能TiN薄膜的制备具有指导意义。

Abstract: TiN thin films have received great attention in the field of superconducting quantum computing due to their low loss characteristics derived from stable chemical and thermal properties, as well as excellent electrical conductivity. In this study, TiN thin films with a thickness of 35 nm were epitaxially grown on MgO substrates using the DC reactive magnetron sputtering technique. The crystal structure, film thickness, and surface morphology of the thin films were characterized. The films exhibit a very flat surface with a root mean square roughness of only a few tenths of a nanometer. The influences of nitrogen ($N_2$) flow rate on the surface morphology, superconducting transition temperature ($T_c$), residual resistivity ratio (RRR), normal-state resistivity ($ρ_n$), upper critical field, and superconducting dimensionality were investigated systematically. The experimental results show that the TiN film deposited with a $N_2$ flow rate of 1.5 sccm exhibits optimal performance, including the highest $T_c$ (4.93 K), the largest RRR, the lowest $ρ_n$, and the lowest slope of the out-of-film upper critical field near Tc. Moreover, it is found that the background vacuum inside the growth chamber has a significant impact on the superconducting properties of TiN films, and the degradation of vacuum degree can lead to the decrease of $T_c$. The upper critical field reveals a significant anisotropic feature. Importantly, the in-film upper critical field data demonstrates that the optimization of sample quality leads to deviation from two-dimensional superconducting behavior, which provides a new perspective on the influencing factors of superconducting dimensionality beyond the thickness of films. The present results provide useful references for a comprehensive understanding of the relationship between growth parameters and the superconducting properties of thin films.

</details>


### [141] [Direct real-space imaging of sign-reversal superconducting state by scanning Josephson tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38421)
*Tianheng Wei,Yanzhao Liu,Wei Ren,Zhen Liang,Ziqiang Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM），在SrTiO3(001)上外延生长的单层高温Fe(Te,Se)薄膜中观测到半晶胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制，并结合单粒子隧道谱，证明其配对对称性为符号反转的无节点d波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体因多带体系配对对称性复杂，传统相位敏感实验（基于约瑟夫森隧道）面临困境，难以确定其配对对称性；需要一种能提供亚晶胞尺度超导相位信息的新方法。

Method: 采用扫描约瑟夫森隧道显微镜（SJTM），在SrTiO3(001)衬底上外延生长的单层高温Fe(Te,Se)薄膜上进行测量；通过s波超导针尖观测约瑟夫森隧道调制，并结合单粒子隧道谱（显示全超导能隙）进行分析。

Result: 观测到半晶胞周期的约瑟夫森隧道调制；在连接次近邻Fe原子的连线上，约瑟夫森隧道几乎消失；单粒子隧道谱显示出完整的超导能隙。实验结果与符号反转的无节点d波配对对称性一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了亚晶胞尺度的超导相位信息，有助于理解高温超导机制；该工作可作为利用SJTM进行原子分辨约瑟夫森干涉实验的起点，推广至各类非常规超导体研究。

Abstract: Phase-sensitive experiments based on Josephson tunneling have been considered as a pivotal method to determine the pairing symmetry in high-temperature (high-Tc) cuprate superconductors. However, in iron-based superconductors, traditional phase-sensitive experiments face setbacks because of the complexity of pairing symmetry in multiband systems. Here, using scanning Josephson tunneling microscopy (SJTM), we report the half-unit-cell periodic Josephson tunneling modulations in monolayer high-Tc Fe(Te,Se) film epitaxially grown on SrTiO3(001). The Josephson tunneling almost vanishes along the lines connecting next-nearest-neighbor Fe atoms under an s-wave superconducting tip. Combined with single-particle tunneling spectra showing the full superconducting gap, our experimental results are consistent with the sign-reversal nodeless d-wave pairing symmetry. Our findings provide sub-unit-cell scale superconducting phase information for understanding the mechanism of high-Tc superconductivity. We anticipate our work as a starting point for atomically resolved Josephson interference experiments using SJTM in various unconventional superconductors.

</details>


### [142] [Layer-Parity Control of the Superconducting Diode Effect in Monolayer NbSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38449)
*Yusuf Wicaksono,Toshikaze Kariyado*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用Wannier基自洽有限Q Bogoliubov-de Gennes方法研究单层NbSe₂的超导二极管效应，揭示磁邻近层的层宇称与取向如何选择螺旋型或三角型二极管通道，并解释了磁场无关整流现象的对称性起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清超导二极管效应的物理机制：模型研究表明整流反映自由能关于最小值的非对称性，而非平衡对动量本身；需要定量厘清磁邻近的层宇称与取向如何控制二极管响应。

Method: 采用Wannier基、自洽有限Q的Bogoliubov-de Gennes方法，并结合费米面自适应积分以收敛临界电流；通过层奇/偶交换场分解与C₃对称性分析，研究面内与面外交换对平衡对动量Q_eq和二极管效率的影响。

Result: 面内交换下Q_eq与二极管效率均与层奇交换对比度成线性关系：两个Se子层同向邻近产生互易响应，反向邻近使响应加倍；面外分量通过破对机制几乎不改变Q_eq，却使效率降低一个量级以上并在低温反转；纯面外交换保持Q_eq=0，却产生受层偶交换控制的三角二极管效应，在低温下高于0.65 meV时大于面内效应。两侧面外邻近反向时效应相消，同向且每层交换相同时效应约增强四倍。

Conclusion: 磁层宇称在螺旋型与三角型二极管通道之间做出选择；三角通道是NbSe₂/CrBr₃中零场整流的本征对称性允许贡献，并预言两侧面外邻近的反向/同向配置分别相消或增强该效应。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect is often associated with finite-momentum pairing, although model studies indicate that rectification reflects the asymmetry of the free energy about its minimum rather than the equilibrium pair momentum itself. Using a Wannier-based, self-consistent finite-$\mathbf Q$ Bogoliubov-de Gennes approach with a Fermi-surface-adaptive quadrature that converges the critical currents, we show that the layer parity and orientation of magnetic proximity select the diode response of monolayer NbSe$_2$. For in-plane exchange, the equilibrium pair momentum $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ and the diode efficiency are both linear in the layer-odd exchange contrast: parallel proximity on the two Se sublayers is reciprocal, and antiparallel proximity doubles the response. An out-of-plane component separates the two observables. It leaves $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}$ nearly unchanged but, through pair breaking, suppresses the efficiency by more than an order of magnitude and, at low temperature, reverses it. Purely out-of-plane exchange, for which $C_3$ symmetry forbids a helical term, keeps $\mathbf Q_{\rm eq}=0$ yet produces a trigonal diode effect, controlled by the layer-even exchange and larger than the in-plane one above $0.65$~meV at low temperature. Magnetic layer parity thus selects between a helical and a trigonal diode channel. The trigonal channel is an intrinsic, symmetry-allowed contribution to the field-free rectification reported in NbSe$_2$/CrBr$_3$ and predicts that two-sided out-of-plane proximity cancels it when antiparallel and, at the same exchange per layer, enhances it about fourfold when parallel, in the computed range.

</details>


### [143] [Separating the Density Wave Phase from Zero-Resistance Superconductivity in La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38959)
*P. Puphal,A. Suthar,B. Wehinger,V. Sundaramurthy,T. Priessnitz,M. Knauft,P. Sosa-Lizama,Y. -M. Wu,M. Hepting,J. Nuss,M. Isobe,P. A. van Aken,Y. E. Suyolcu,B. Keimer,H. Takagi,P. Reiss*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过研究三层Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐La4Ni3O10的高质量单晶，发现增强的面内双轴压缩压力可在比以往报道低60%的压力下诱导零电阻态，并将低温密度波相与超导完全分离，表明面内双轴压力是促进镍酸盐超导的有力手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper镍酸盐中超导的关键因素难以确定，因为结构变化、氧含量与位置、电子有序相以及达到超导所需的高压之间存在复杂的相互依赖关系。研究旨在解耦这些复杂因素。

Method: 采用一系列洁净的三层La4Ni3O10单晶，施加增强的面内双轴压缩压力，并系统测量电阻等输运性质，观察零电阻态出现的压力条件及其与密度波相的关系。

Result: 增强的双轴压缩压力在比先前报道低60%的压力下诱导出零电阻态；同时完全分离了低压密度波相与超导，排除了密度波序参量的软量子临界涨落作为该体系配对胶水的可能性。

Conclusion: 面内双轴压力是促进镍酸盐超导的有力工具，统一了不同Ruddlesden-Popper结构中实现超导所需的压力尺度，并与近期发现的薄膜样品建立了联系。

Abstract: Identifying the key ingredients for superconductivity under pressure in the Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates remains a formidable challenge, due to a complex interplay between structural variations, oxygen content and location, electronically ordered phases, and the need for high pressures to reach the superconducting phase. To disentangle these complex inter-dependencies, here we study a series of clean single crystals of trilayer La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. We discover that enhanced biaxial, compressive pressures induce a zero-resistance state at a 60\% lower pressure compared to previous reports. At the same time, we completely separate the low-pressure density wave phase from superconductivity, effectively ruling out that soft, quantum critical fluctuations of the density wave order parameter act as the pairing glue in this system. Our results establish biaxial, in-plane pressures as a powerful tool for promoting superconductivity in nickelates, unifying the required pressure scales for superconductivity in different Ruddlesden-Popper structures, and providing a connection to the recently discovered thin film samples.

</details>


### [144] [Coexistence of superconductivity and magnetic order in BaFe$_2$S$_3$: evidence for orbital-selective delocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38966)
*Y. Oubaid,N. S. Dhami,P. Fertey,C. V. Colin,C. Ritter,A. Forget,D. Bounoua,J. -P. Rueff,M. Verseils,P. Foury-Leylekian,V. Balédent*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过高压下的X射线/中子衍射、X射线发射谱与电输运测量，研究了准一维自旋梯化合物BaFe2S3（BFS）中超导与磁性的共存机制，发现压力导致长程条纹反铁磁序保持稳健，而局域铁磁矩被压制近80%，揭示了一种轨道选择性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超导如何从关联电子态中涌现是凝聚态物理的核心挑战。BFS作为缺乏二维正方晶格的准一维自旋梯化合物，为研究铁基超导体中超导与磁性的相互作用提供了独特平台。

Method: 作者结合高压X射线衍射、中子衍射、X射线发射光谱（XES）以及电输运测量，系统绘制了BFS在压力下进入超导态过程中结构、磁性与电子性质的演化图景。

Result: 观测到显著的“压力诱导解耦”现象：长程条纹型反铁磁序在直至超导起始的压力范围内异常稳健，但局域铁磁矩被压制近80%。该二分性指向轨道选择性机制——部分Fe 3d电子保持局域以维持磁性，其余电子退局域化形成巡游的超导态。超导在长程磁序仍存在时即已出现，表明二者在BFS中共存，这归因于多轨道电子自由度的选择性响应。

Conclusion: BFS中超导与长程反铁磁序共存，其根源在于多轨道电子自由度的选择性响应：局域电子维持磁性，巡游电子形成超导，为理解准一维体系中超导与磁性的相互关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: Understanding how superconductivity emerges from correlated electronic states is a central challenge in condensed matter physics. The quasi-one-dimensional spin-ladder compound \bfs\ serves as a unique platform to study this interplay, as it lacks the two-dimensional square lattice characteristic of most iron-based superconductors. Here, we combine high-pressure x-ray and neutron diffraction, x-ray emission spectroscopy, and electrical transport to map its structural, magnetic, and electronic evolution into the superconducting regime. We \revised{observe} a striking pressure-induced decoupling: while the long-range stripe-type antiferromagnetic order remains remarkably robust under pressure up to the onset of superconductivity, the local iron magnetic moment is suppressed by nearly 80\%. This dichotomy \revised{points to an orbital-selective mechanism}, where specific Fe $3d$ electrons remain localized to sustain magnetism while others delocalize to form the itinerant and superconducting state. \revised{Superconductivity emerges while long-range magnetic order is still present, revealing that the two coexist in \bfs, which we attribute to} the selective response of multiorbital electronic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [145] [Local Josephson response near nonmagnetic impurities and vortices in a two-orbital $s_{\pm}$ superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39192)
*Yuanyuan Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究自洽两轨道s±超导体中非磁性杂质和涡旋附近的局域约瑟夫森响应，发现杂质可反转局域耦合且无π相移，涡旋态下响应相位在空间上与凝聚体缠绕行为不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多带s±超导体中局域约瑟夫森隧穿响应对杂质和涡旋的敏感依赖，揭示反常谱贡献、尖端-样品耦合与局域配对场之间的复杂关系，以理解此类体系的相位敏感测量。

Method: 采用自洽两轨道s±超导模型，计算非磁性杂质和自洽两涡旋态下的局域约瑟夫森电流响应；考察局域s波尖端（对两轨道等耦合）、尖端能隙变化及隧穿强度对电流各分量（如m=1正弦分量）的影响，并分析准粒子干涉特征。

Result: 干净样品中两轨道反常谱贡献近相消；非磁性杂质重排这些贡献，可在无π相移下反转局域耦合，且偏离中心位点的负耦合出现在更弱杂质势下；杂质诱导的变化集中在低准粒子能区。零场下耦合随尖端能隙变化而变号，增大隧穿强度可反转m=1正弦电流分量但不改变样品态。相干峰能处，杂质强度主要改变已有准粒子干涉特征的相对强度。自洽两涡旋态中，约瑟夫森响应相对凝聚体发展出空间变化相位，两核附近响应与凝聚体共享绕数，而更大回路包含额外的响应绕数。

Conclusion: 局域约瑟夫森符号与相位由反常谱和尖端-样品耦合共同决定；杂质的谱重排可在无配对场π相移的情况下反转局域耦合，多带超导体的局域约瑟夫森响应为探测其反常谱与相位结构提供了敏感手段。

Abstract: We study the local Josephson response near nonmagnetic impurities and vortices in a self-consistent two-orbital $s_{\pm}$ superconductor. For a local $s$-wave tip that couples equally to the two orbitals, anomalous spectral contributions of opposite sign nearly cancel in the clean sample. Nonmagnetic impurities redistribute these contributions and can reverse the local coupling without a corresponding $π$ phase shift of the bond pair fields. Negative coupling appears at off-center sites at weaker impurity potentials than at the impurity site. The impurity-induced change is concentrated at lower quasiparticle energies, while states over a broader energy range contribute to the total clean and impurity couplings. In zero field, the coupling also changes sign as the tip gap is varied, and increasing the tunneling strength can reverse the $m=1$ sine component of the current without changing the sample state. At the chosen coherence-peak energy, increasing impurity strength mainly changes the relative intensities of existing quasiparticle-interference features. In a self-consistent two-vortex state, the Josephson response develops a spatially varying phase relative to the condensate. The response and condensate share the winding close to each core, while larger loops enclose additional winding of the Josephson response. The local Josephson sign and phase reflect the anomalous spectrum and the tip--sample coupling.

</details>


### [146] [Thermalization-Controlled Superconducting Diode-Like Transport in Disordered Molybdenum and NbN Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39523)
*Akhilesh Kumar,Shivani Soni,Gomathi Natarajan,Ramanathaswamy Pandian,Thameemul Ansari M,Pooja Gupta,Sanjay Rai,Edward Prabu Amaladass*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 作者在Mo薄膜中观察到类似超导二极管的电流方向不对称与迟滞行为，但发现该现象强烈依赖于测量系统的热化条件，从而证明其本质是由焦耳热引发的电热效应，而非内禀的非互易超导电性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应以超导态中方向依赖的临界电流为特征，但在输运测量中，电流诱导焦耳热与有限热弛豫可能产生类似的电流方向不对称和迟滞，需要区分热效应与内禀非互易超导，以免误判。

Method: 采用直流磁控溅射在Si/SiO2衬底上制备128 nm厚Mo薄膜（Tc=5.8 K），分别在闭循环制冷机（热化较差）与14 T低温系统（热化改善）中测量电流-电压特性；并以NbN薄膜作参考对比，分析不同电流极性、扫描历史与热边界条件下的开关行为。

Result: 闭循环制冷机中Mo薄膜表现出显著的电流方向不对称和迟滞；而在热化改善的14 T系统中同一薄膜响应明显更对称。由于焦耳热P=I²R与电流极性无关，所观察的不对称并非源于极性依赖的加热，而是热弛豫、扫描历史与热边界条件共同作用的结果。NbN参考薄膜呈现相似行为，进一步支持电热效应主导的结论。

Conclusion: 在输运测量中鉴别超导二极管效应时，必须区分热开关/迟滞与内禀非互易超导电性；本文结果表明，表观二极管样响应在很大程度上受电热效应影响，改善热化后不对称被抑制。

Abstract: Superconducting diode effects are characterized by direction-dependent critical currents in the superconducting state. Here, we investigate diode-like transport in 128-nm-thick Mo thin film deposited on Si_SiO2 substrate by DC magnetron sputtering. The thin film exhibit a superconducting transition temperature TC of 5.8 K. Pronounced current-direction asymmetry and hysteresis are observed in current-voltage characteristics measured in a closed-cycle refrigerator. In contrast, the same thin film exhibits a substantially more symmetric response in a 14T cryogenic system with improved sample thermalization. We attribute this strong dependence on the measurement environment to electrothermal effects arising from current-induced Joule heating and finite thermal relaxation. As the current approaches the superconducting-to-resistive switching condition, the emergence of a resistive component leads to a rapid increase in Joule heating, raising the sample temperature and modifying the switching condition. Conversely, during the resistive-to-superconducting transition, residual heating and finite thermal relaxation reduce the switching current. Since P is equal to I2R it is independent of current polarity, the observed asymmetry is not associated with polarity-dependent Joule heating but with the interplay of thermal relaxation, sweep history, and thermal boundary conditions. The suppression of the asymmetry under improved thermalization, together with similar behavior observed in NbN reference films, indicates that the apparent diode-like response is strongly influenced by electrothermal effects. These results highlight the need to distinguish thermal switching and hysteresis from intrinsic nonreciprocal superconductivity when identifying superconducting diode effects in transport measurements.

</details>


### [147] [Majorana Zero Modes in Fractional Vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39942)
*Lecheng Fang,Haotian Li,Haijiao Ji,Quanxin Hu,Hong Ding,Noah F. Q. Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文论证：铁基超导体中整数涡旋分裂出的分数量子涡旋（FV）所承载的马约拉纳零模（MZM）因拓扑保护而在各 FV 核心之间空间展开，而费米子束缚态则局域于单个 FV 核心；这一差异导致局域隧穿与非局域交叉 Andreev 反射中均出现零偏压电导峰，可作为 MZM 的替代性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳零模是拓扑量子计算的候选平台，铁基超导体中的涡旋是主要实验平台之一。近期实验发现整数 Abrikosov 涡旋可分裂为携带分数磁通量子的分数涡旋（FV），但 MZM 在 FV 系统中的空间分布及其可观测特征尚不明确，因此需要厘清 MZM 与普通费米子束缚态在 FV 上的区别，以提供新的实验鉴别手段。

Method: 作者利用拓扑保护性质分析分数量子涡旋体系中 MZM 与费米子束缚态的空间局域行为：MZM 受拓扑保护而在各个 FV 核心之间空间展开（非局域分布），而费米子束缚态仍局域于单个 FV 核心。据此推导局域隧穿谱与非局域交叉 Andreev 反射（crossed Andreev reflection）的电导特征。

Result: 研究显示：FV 承载的 MZM 在空间上展开分布于多个 FV 核心，而费米子束缚态保持局域于单个 FV 核心。这一差异使得局域隧穿和非局域交叉 Andreev 反射中都出现零偏压电导峰，从而提供了一种区别于传统判据的 MZM 实验信号。

Conclusion: 分数量子涡旋中 MZM 的空间展开特性与费米子束缚态的局域特性形成鲜明对比，可通过局域隧穿与非局域交叉 Andreev 反射中的零偏压电导峰加以区分，为铁基超导体中 MZM 的探测提供了一条新的实验路径。

Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZMs) are promising for topological quantum computation, with vortices in topological superconductors serving as a leading platform. Recent experiments show that integer Abrikosov vortices in iron-based superconductors can split into fractional vortices (FVs) carrying fractional flux quanta. We show that due to topological protection, MZMs hosted by FVs are spatially unfolded over the FV cores, while fermionic bound states remain localized at individual FV cores. This yields zero-bias conductance peaks in both local tunneling and nonlocal crossed Andreev reflection, offering an alternative MZM signature.

</details>


### [148] [Selective effects of selenium substitution on superconductivity and magnetic memory in 4Hb-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39969)
*Shalev Gur,Sajilesh K. P.,Shubam Purwar,Irena Feldman,Ilay Mangel,Nitzan Ragoler,Amit Kanigel,Beena Kalisky*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过硒（Se）取代对范德华异质结构 4Hb-TaS2 的局域无序进行调控，发现超导对其高度稳健，而磁记忆（自发涡旋）被强烈抑制，表明硒取代并非创造而是选择性地削弱隐藏磁记忆的宏观表现，揭示超导相与隐藏记忆相之间存在尖锐分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序在关联量子材料中作用关键，但当多种电子序共存时其效应可能具有高度选择性。4Hb-TaS2 同时具有超导、电荷序倾向、强散射以及通过自发涡旋体现的磁记忆，为研究无序的选择性效应提供了理想平台。

Method: 作者采用硒（Se）取代作为对 4Hb-TaS2 无序景观的受控局部扰动，系统改变 Se 浓度，并对比研究超导与磁记忆（自发涡旋响应）随无序程度变化的演化行为。

Result: 研究发现超导在 Se 取代下保持显著稳健，而磁记忆在较高 Se 浓度下被强烈抑制。这一结果表明 Se 取代并不是产生隐藏磁记忆的来源，而是选择性地削弱了其宏观表现。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 4Hb-TaS2 中稳健的超导相与隐藏记忆相之间存在清晰分离，且自发涡旋响应对无序高度敏感，加深了对多序共存关联材料中无序选择性效应的理解。

Abstract: Disorder plays a central role in correlated quantum materials, but its effects can be highly selective when several electronic orders coexist. The van der Waals heterostructure 4Hb-TaS2 provides such a case, combining superconductivity, charge-order tendencies, strong scattering, and magnetic memory manifested through spontaneous vortices. Here we use Se substitution as a controlled local perturbation of the disorder landscape in 4Hb-TaS2. We find that superconductivity remains remarkably robust while the magnetic memory is strongly suppressed at higher Se concentrations. These results show that Se substitution does not create the hidden magnetic memory, but instead selectively weakens its macroscopic manifestation. More broadly, they reveal a sharp separation between robust superconducting and hidden-memory phases, and a highly disorder-sensitive spontaneous-vortex response in 4Hb-TaS2.

</details>


### [149] [Evidence for an effective gauge field in flakes of superconducting 4Hb-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39979)
*Shalev Gur,T. R. Devidas,Eylon Persky,Roni Anna Gofman,Irena Feldman,Avior Almoalem,Sudip Kumar Saha,Mark H. Fischer,Avraham Klein,Jonathan Ruhman,Amit Kanigel,Beena Kalisky*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文证明4Hb-TaS2中的磁记忆效应在无涡旋的Meissner态下依然存在，排除俘获磁通解释，指向一种隐藏电子序对超导凝聚体施加有效磁通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非常规超导体的正常态对揭示超导起源至关重要。4Hb-TaS2中超导态表现出异常的磁记忆效应，此前实验表明记忆可在Tc以上编码、Tc以下通过自发涡旋显现，暗示时间反演对称性破缺。但由于记忆与涡旋相互纠缠，无法确定效应源于俘获磁通、弱常规磁性还是真正的隐藏电子序。

Method: 作者研究了剥离的4Hb-TaS2薄片，在无涡旋的Meissner态下检验磁记忆效应，并测量训练样品在零场下产生的磁通空间分布。

Result: 发现磁记忆在涡旋自由的Meissner区域内持续存在，表现为超导屏蔽响应的稳定剩余改变；零场下训练样品产生的磁通空间分布与常规弱磁性不一致。

Conclusion: 结果排除了基于俘获涡旋的解释，并表明存在一种隐藏电子相，该相在超导凝聚体上印刻了有效的磁通。

Abstract: Understanding the normal state of unconventional superconductors is central to uncovering the origin of superconductivity itself. Unconventional superconductivity often emerges from correlated electronic states characterized by intertwined symmetry-breaking orders. A striking example is 4Hb-TaS2, whose superconducting state exhibits a remarkable range of phenomena, including an unusual magnetic memory effect. Previous experiments showed that magnetic memory can be encoded above Tc and later revealed through spontaneous vortices below Tc, suggesting time-reversal symmetry breaking. However, since memory and vorticity are intertwined, this prevents establishing whether the effect results from trapped flux, weak conventional magnetism, or a true hidden electronic order. Here, we show that magnetic memory persists in the vortex-free Meissner regime of exfoliated 4Hb-TaS2 flakes, where it appears as a stable remanent modification of the superconducting screening response. Our results rule out explanations based on trapped vortices. Furthermore, the spatial profile of the flux generated by trained samples at zero field is inconsistent with conventional weak magnetism, suggesting a hidden electronic phase that imprints an effective flux on the superconducting condensate.

</details>


### [150] [Chiral superconductivity on a closed Klein bottle: a chirality wall and its Majorana seam spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40251)
*Junhao,Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋无关的π-通量晶格超导体，其正常态动量空间基本域为克莱因瓶。在该模型中，滑动提升（glide lift）与隐藏的反幺正对称性各自独立地迫使BdG陈数归零。对角配对在其实部非零时恰好打破反幺正阻碍——这是手征相的必要而非充分条件。作者导出明确Pfaffian以判定陈宇称，并在实配对截面中识别出三类解析的能隙闭合族。对于闭合实空间克莱因瓶上的手征态，指定配对与粘合产生ΔC=2的手征墙；平移不变展开分解出两条同向传播的马约拉纳分支，其有限尺寸能级依赖于周长和完整（holonomy），近零对由允许的局部接缝微扰在一阶解除简并。截圆柱对照具有相同的低能通道数，故粘合重连了既有的无能隙内容。文章还区分了字面粘合与deck投影：粒子-空穴对称交换deck本征块，排除将二者解释为独立的BdG商哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在正常态动量空间为克莱因瓶（非定向紧曲面）的π-通量晶格超导体中，配对相容性如何受滑动对称与隐藏反幺正对称约束，并理解在闭合实空间克莱因瓶上粘合手征态时，接缝处手征墙及马约拉纳低能谱的具体行为。

Method: 在自旋无关π-通量晶格超导模型上，分析滑动提升与隐藏反幺正对称对BdG陈数的独立制约；采用对角配对并考察其实部非零时对反幺正阻碍的打破；导出Pfaffian判定陈宇称；在实配对截面内识别三类能隙闭合族；在闭合实空间克莱因瓶上指定配对与粘合构造ΔC=2手征墙，经平移不变展开求解马约拉纳分支及其有限尺寸能级；以截圆柱为对照比较低能通道数；并用粒子-空穴对称区分字面粘合与deck投影。

Result: 滑动提升与隐藏反幺正对称各自独立迫使BdG陈数消失；对角配对实部非零是打破反幺正阻碍的必要条件，但非充分条件。得到明确Pfaffian并识别三类解析能隙闭合族。在手征态闭合克莱因瓶上得到ΔC=2手征墙，平移不变展开给出两条同向马约拉纳分支，其近零对由允许的局部接缝微扰一阶提升；截圆柱与粘合态低能通道数一致，表明粘合重连既有无能隙内容。粒子-空穴对称交换deck本征块，故二者不能视为独立BdG商哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个模型特异的配对相容性与闭合曲面接缝谱的刻画：对角配对实部非零仅为手征相的必要条件；闭合克莱因瓶上的手征墙及其马约拉纳低能谱是粘合与完整依赖的，且非受保护零模。能量代价在固定配对背景下评估，未主张存在受保护零模或自洽的墙解。

Abstract: We study a spinless $π$-flux lattice superconductor whose normal-state momentum-space fundamental domain is a Klein bottle. Within this model, a glide lift and a hidden antiunitary symmetry independently force the Bogoliubov--de Gennes (BdG) Chern number to vanish. A diagonal pairing breaks the antiunitary obstruction exactly when its real part is nonzero; this is necessary, not sufficient, for a chiral phase. We derive explicit Pfaffians determining Chern parity and identify three analytic families of gap closings in a real-pairing section. For a chiral state on a closed real-space Klein bottle, the prescribed pairing and gluing produce a $ΔC=2$ chirality wall. A translation-invariant unfolding resolves two co-propagating Majorana branches. Their finite-size levels depend on circumference and holonomy; the near-zero pair is lifted at first order by allowed local seam perturbations. A cut-cylinder control has the same low-energy channel count, so gluing reconnects existing gapless content. We also distinguish literal gluing from deck projection: particle--hole symmetry exchanges the deck eigenblocks, precluding their interpretation as independent BdG quotient Hamiltonians. The contribution is a model-specific account of pairing compatibility and closed-surface seam spectra. The energy cost is evaluated for a fixed pairing background; neither a protected zero mode nor a self-consistent wall solution is claimed.

</details>


### [151] [Chiral $p$-Wave Superconductivity Proximate to a Fractional Chern Insulator and a Topological Charge Density Wave in a Moiré Chern Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40469)
*Yunze Ouyang,Fengcheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文研究扭曲MoTe₂自旋极化的moiré Chern能带在短程排斥作用下的行为，发现分数陈绝缘体（ν=2/3）和拓扑电荷密度波（ν=3/4）附近出现超导性，并揭示了超导、分数拓扑与电荷序之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索moiré Chern能带中由强关联效应驱动的非常规超导及其与分数拓扑态、电荷序的邻近关系，以解释实验观察并理解拓扑平带中的配对机制。

Method: 结合精确对角化（exact diagonalization）、Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）分析、Kohn-Luttinger理论以及超流刚度与配对关联的计算；并通过绝热连接到吸引模型验证超导态的本质。

Result: 在填充 0.7≲ν≲0.77 范围内发现配对束缚与超流刚度，呈手性p波配对关联，与实验一致；弱排斥下Kohn-Luttinger理论给出费米面不稳定性且配对对称性相同；超导态BdG陈数C_BdG=−1，与ν=1处正常态陈数相反。

Conclusion: 结果表明超导态与分数陈绝缘体、拓扑电荷密度波紧密相邻，体现了超导、分数拓扑与电荷序之间的深度关联，为拓扑平带中非常规超导的起源提供了统一图像。

Abstract: We study a spin-polarized moiré Chern band of twisted MoTe$_2$ with short-range repulsion, finding superconductivity proximate to a fractional Chern insulator at filling $ν=2/3$ and a topological charge density wave at $ν=3/4$. Exact diagonalization reveals pair binding over $0.7\lesssimν\lesssim0.77$, consistent with experiment, along with finite superfluid stiffness, chiral $p$-wave pairing correlations, and adiabatic connectivity to an attractive model. At weak repulsion, Kohn-Luttinger theory supports a Fermi-surface instability with the same pairing symmetry. The superconducting state has a Bogoliubov-de Gennes Chern number $C_{\mathrm{BdG}}=-1$, opposite to the normal-state Chern number at $ν=1$. Our results reveal interplay among superconductivity, fractional topology, and charge order.

</details>
