### [182] [Exact Solution of the $(2+1)$-Dimensional Dunkl-Klein-Gordon Oscillator with a Uniform Magnetic Field in Non-Commutative Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37820)
*R. D. Mota,D. Ojeda-Guillén*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文求解了含 Dunkl 导数广义化的非对易相空间中的 Klein-Gordon 振子（含与不含均匀磁场两种情形），通过 Seiberg-Witten 映射引入非对易几何，精确求得了本征函数与能谱，并分析了非相对论极限及能谱行为，验证了退化一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Dunkl 导数（引入反射宇称对称性）与非对易相空间几何、外磁场相结合，研究 Klein-Gordon 振子的量子行为，以揭示这些扩展对能级结构的宇称依赖修正，并验证与已有对易 Dunkl 模型的一致性。

Method: 先在标准对易相空间中引入 Dunkl 导数并评估反射宇称对称性；再利用 Seiberg-Witten 映射将系统推广到非对易相空间；对含/不含均匀磁场两种情形精确求解本征函数与能谱（含磁场时对应广义 Landau 能级）；计算系统非相对论极限并对能谱做图形分析。

Result: 得到精确本征函数与能谱：Dunkl 形变参数、非对易相空间几何与磁场共同产生能级的宇称依赖修正；含磁场时出现广义 Landau 能级；当外磁场或非对易参数趋于零时，解正确退化为文献中已有的对易 Dunkl 模型。

Conclusion: Dunkl 形变、非对易相空间与磁场的联合效应导致能级出现宇称依赖的修正，且所得结果在极限情形下与既有对易 Dunkl 模型自洽一致，表明该框架是对已知模型的有效推广。

Abstract: We solve the Klein-Gordon oscillator in a non-commutative phase space, generalized using Dunkl derivatives, both with and without interaction with a uniform magnetic field. For each of these cases, we begin by introducing the Dunkl derivative into the standard commutative phase space to evaluate the reflection parity symmetries. Then, using the Seiberg-Witten map, we extend the generalized system to a non-commutative phase space. In both cases, we determine the eigenfunctions and the energy spectrum exactly (which correspond to generalized Landau levels when a magnetic field is present). Furthermore, we compute the non-relativistic limit of the system and graphically analyze the behavior of the energy spectra. Our results demonstrate that the combination of the Dunkl deformation parameters, the non-commutative phase space geometry, and the magnetic field produces a parity-dependent modification of the energy levels. Finally, we verify that when the external magnetic field or the non-commutative parameters vanish, our exact solutions correctly reduce to the commutative Dunkl models established in previous works.

</details>


### [183] [Bulk gap stability and spectral flow for fermions with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40299)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了弱长程多体相互作用保持自由费米子系统的体带隙，并可通过一个准局域谱流将自由基态输运到相互作用系统的基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体相互作用对自由费米子系统体带隙（bulk gap）的影响：在无需强局域性假设的条件下，论证弱长程相互作用不会破坏带隙，并给出自由基态与相互作用基态之间可构造的联系，从而推广此前依赖于局域（或快速衰减）相互作用的稳定性结果。

Method: 考虑自由费米子相互作用 $Φ_h$（可含配对项），其无限体积极化 Majorana 系数算子 $h$ 在 $(-g,g)$ 内无谱；再加入一个偶自伴多体相互作用 $\mathcal V$。对充分小的 $|λ|$，构造相互作用体系统 $Φ_λ=Φ_h+λ\mathcal V$ 的纯基态 $ω_λ$ 及准局域自同构 $β_{0,λ}$（谱流），使得 $ω_λ=ω_0\circβ_{0,λ}$。证明核心是：①通过激发数比较证明带隙保持，给出间隙下界 $g-C_*|λ|\|\mathcal V\|_*$；②利用伴随论文中演化相互作用的多项式传播界构造谱流。对双体相互作用于一致离散点集 $Γ\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ 的情形，给出跳迁/配对系数与相互作用项按 $(1+|x-y|)^{-ϖ}$（$ϖ>2d$）衰减的充分条件；对拉伸指数衰减情形谱流生成元也保持拉伸指数衰减。

Result: 对于充分小的 $|λ|$，构造出相互作用巨正则系统的纯基态 $ω_λ$ 与准局域自同构 $β_{0,λ}$，满足 $ω_λ=ω_0\circβ_{0,λ}$；每个 $ω_λ$ 的 GNS 哈密顿量带隙至少为 $g-C_*|λ|\|\mathcal V\|_*$（$\|\cdot\|_*$ 为相应的相互作用范数）。结论容许自由系数与多体相互作用均呈多项式衰减；谱流生成元保持多项式相互作用范数的空间指数，其自同构满足该指数下的局域性界。

Conclusion: 弱长程多体相互作用不破坏自由费米子系统的体带隙，且自由基态可经准局域谱流连续连接到相互作用基态。在多项式衰减（$ϖ>2d$）乃至拉伸指数衰减条件下该稳定性成立，谱流构造保持了相应的空间衰减指数。

Abstract: We prove that weak long-range many-body interactions preserve the bulk gap of a free fermion system and that a quasi-local spectral flow transports the free ground state to ground states of the interacting system. Let $Φ_h$ be a free fermionic interaction, possibly including pairing terms, whose infinite-volume Majorana coefficient operator $h$ has no spectrum in $(-g,g)$, and let $\mathcal V$ be an even self-adjoint many-body interaction. For sufficiently small $|λ|$, we construct pure ground states $ω_λ$ of the interacting bulk system $Φ_λ=Φ_h+λ\mathcal V$ and quasi-local automorphisms $β_{0,λ}$ such that $ω_λ=ω_0\circβ_{0,λ}$, where $ω_0$ is the free ground state of $Φ_h$. Each $ω_λ$ has a GNS Hamiltonian with gap at least $g-C_*|λ| \|\mathcal V\|_*$, where $\|\cdot\|_*$ is the relevant interaction norm.
  Our result allows polynomial decay of both the free coefficients and the many-body interaction. For two-site interactions on a uniformly discrete point set $Γ\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, a sufficient condition is decay as $(1+|x-y|)^{-\varpi}$ with $\varpi>2d$, for both the hopping and pairing coefficients and the interaction terms. The spectral-flow generator preserves the spatial exponent of the polynomial interaction norm, and its automorphisms satisfy locality bounds at that exponent. If $h$ and $\mathcal V$ have stretched-exponential decay, so does the spectral-flow generator.
  The proof constructs the spectral flow and establishes the gap through an excitation-number comparison, using polynomial propagation bounds for evolved interactions proved in a companion paper.

</details>


### [184] [Algebraic Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37904)
*Kasia Rejzner*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对代数量子场论（AQFT）框架的介绍性综述，系统阐述如何通过可观测量代数、局域化结构与态来刻画量子系统，并展示该方法在自由场、局域代数、超选择理论、弯曲时空及量子信息等方向的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AQFT将可观测量及其时空局域化的描述与具体态的选择相分离，从而无需假定各表示共享同一希尔伯特空间即可比较真空态、热态和带荷表示。这一特性使AQFT成为理解量子场论（特别是连续时空中的无穷多自由度系统）与普通有限自由度量子力学差异的理想框架。

Method: 从熟悉的量子力学出发逐步建立AQFT框架；构造自由标量场；利用因果性与正能量条件约束局域测量；借助Reeh–Schlieder定理、局域von Neumann代数结构和分裂性质揭示连续QFT与有限自由度量子的本质区别；通过超选择理论将可观测量电荷与统计及内部对称性相联系；并运用局域协变性、微扰方法和同调方法将讨论推广到弯曲时空、相互作用与规范理论。

Result: 论述了Reeh–Schlieder定理、局域von Neumann代数的结构及分裂性质等关键差异，表明连续量子场论与有限自由度量子力学在局域代数层面上有根本不同；超选择理论建立了电荷、统计与内部对称性之间的对应关系；AQFT方法可成功推广至弯曲时空、相互作用与规范理论，并在测量理论和量子信息方面取得进展。

Conclusion: AQFT通过代数化、局域化的公理化框架为量子场论提供了严谨统一的语言，能够不依赖特定希尔伯特空间表示而系统地处理真空、热和带荷等不同物理情形，并为弯曲时空、规范理论及量子信息研究提供了有力的概念工具。

Abstract: Algebraic quantum field theory describes a quantum system through its observables, their localisation in spacetime, and the states that assign them expectation values. The separation of the specification of observables from the choice of a state makes it possible to compare vacuum, thermal and charged representations without assuming that they share a preferred Hilbert space. We develop the framework from familiar quantum mechanics, construct the free scalar field, and explain how causality and positive energy constrain local measurements. The Reeh--Schlieder theorem, the structure of local von Neumann algebras and the split property reveal key differences between quantum theory in the continuum (i.e. QFT) and ordinary quantum mechanics with finitely many degrees of freedom. Superselection theory relates observable charges to statistics and internal symmetries. Finally, local covariance and perturbative and homological methods extend the discussion to curved spacetime, interactions and gauge theories. We also discuss measurement theory and quantum information aspects of AQFT.

</details>


### [185] [Local determination of low-energy states in gapped fermion systems with weak long-range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40337)
*Stefan Teufel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对体态带隙费米子系统，在弱相互作用下低能态的定量局域确定性质：区域U内与体态一致的相互作用下，所有能量在[0,3g/4]的低能态在U深处的局域期望值都接近体态基态，误差随到U补集的距离衰减，与U外相互作用及所选低能态无关。由此推出唯一代数额基态、LPPL原理、边界局域化激励及小关联性等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究体态带隙费米子系统中低能态的局域结构，旨在从数学上严格证明：在弱偶数体相互作用下，低能态的局域性质只由局域相互作用决定，而不受远处缺陷或边界项的影响，即建立"局域扰动只影响局域"的定量化原理。

Method: 将自由基态到相互作用体态基态的准绝热输运（quasi-adiabatic transport）与一个新条件——局域激发可探测性（local excitation detectability, LED）相结合。伴随论文建立了弱相互作用下的带隙稳定性并构造输运，本文则在足够弱耦合下证明LED。由此得到的抽象定理是非微扰的，只要输运与LED假设成立即适用。

Result: 证明了对低能态的定量局域确定，并由此得到四个强推论：(i)体态相互作用具有唯一代数额基态；(ii)强LPPL原理成立，任意远处缺陷仅以空间衰减误差改变局域基态期望；(iii)有限样本中的低能激发在所有时间上保持靠近边界局域化，至多为空间衰减尾巴，且对有界项一致成立；(iv)从U深处到U外的关联很小，并给出用空间分离和到U补集距离表示的定量界。

Conclusion: 在弱相互作用且允许多项式空间衰减的体态带隙费米子系统中，低能态在区域内部被定量局域确定；准绝热输运配合LED条件提供了一个非微扰的抽象框架，可用于严格推导唯一基态、LPPL原理、边界局域化及关联衰减等局域化结论。

Abstract: We consider bulk-gapped fermion systems whose infinite-volume bulk is obtained by adding sufficiently weak even many-body interactions to a free Hamiltonian with spectral gap $g>0$, allowing polynomial spatial decay of both parts. We prove quantitative local determination of low-energy states: whenever another interaction agrees with the bulk interaction inside a finite or infinite region $U$, with bulk terms crossing its boundary unchanged or removed, all its states with excitation energies in $[0,3g/4]$ have local expectations close to those of the bulk ground state deep inside $U$. The error decays with the distance to the complement of $U$, independently of the interaction terms supported outside $U$ and of the chosen low-energy state.
  Quantitative local determination has strong consequences. (i) The bulk interaction has a unique algebraic ground state. (ii) A strong "local perturbations perturb locally" (LPPL) principle holds: arbitrary distant defects change local ground-state expectations only by a spatially decaying error. (iii) Low-energy excitations in finite samples remain localized near the boundary for all times, up to spatially decaying tails, uniformly over arbitrary even boundary terms. (iv) Correlations from deep inside $U$ to outside $U$ are small, with quantitative bounds in terms of spatial separation and distance to $U^c$.
  To prove local determination of low-energy states, we combine quasi-adiabatic transport of the free ground state to the interacting bulk ground state with a new condition, local excitation detectability (LED). A companion paper establishes gap stability and constructs the transport for weak interactions, while we prove LED at sufficiently weak coupling. The abstract theorem is nonperturbative and applies whenever the transport and LED hypotheses hold.

</details>


### [186] [From gauge-covariant Noether invariants to electromagnetic velocity-dependent mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38128)
*A. S. de Castro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从标准 Noether–Bessel-Hagen 构造出发，将相对论规范不变的世界线守恒荷显式约化到非相对论速度依赖力学的广义常数，保留准不变性边界项，并给出统一磁场的规范依赖代表与规范不变平移荷、Darwin 相互作用的 O(c^-2) 多体实现，以及闭粒子-场系统的总能-动量 Killing 荷，最终建立起连接三种既有描述的显式约化图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子的时空对称性只需在规范变换意义下保持电磁势，需厘清相对论规范不变守恒荷与非相对论速度依赖力学的广义常数之间的精确对应，并避免在三种既有描述（相对论世界线、坐标时拉格朗日、粒子-场系统）间混淆各不相同的守恒量。

Method: 采用标准 Noether–Bessel-Hagen 构造，以作用量 S_p=-mc∫ds+q∫A 及正则世界线一形式 P=-m u^♭+qA，取满足 L_X A=dχ_X 的 Killing 矢量生成 C_X=P(X)-qχ_X；全程保留准不变性边界项，逐生成元取其低速极限得到坐标时拉格朗日的 Noether–Bessel-Hagen 荷，进而推出广义线动量、角动量和能量；再用均匀磁场、Darwin 相互作用场消去以及总能-动量 Killing 荷作具体验证。

Result: 得到从相对论规范不变世界线电荷到非相对论速度依赖力学广义常数的显式约化映射：均匀磁场给出基本规范依赖代表及规范不变平移荷；Darwin 相互作用经场消去后提供保守的 O(c^-2) 多体实现；闭粒子-场系统由总能-动量 Killing 荷支配。三种描述的守恒量得以区分而非等同。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个显式约化映射，将相对论规范不变世界线电荷、非相对论速度依赖力学的广义常数和粒子-场系统的总能-动量 Killing 荷这三种既有描述联系起来，同时明确指出它们的守恒量彼此不同，不可简单等同。

Abstract: For a charged particle, a spacetime symmetry need preserve the electromagnetic potential only up to a gauge transformation. Starting from the standard Noether--Bessel-Hagen construction, we make explicit the reduction from the relativistic gauge-invariant worldline charge to the generalized constants of nonrelativistic velocity-dependent mechanics, retaining the quasi-invariance boundary term throughout. With the convention $S_p=-mc\int ds+q\int A$, the canonical worldline one-form is $P=-m u^\flat+qA$, and a Killing vector satisfying $\mathcal L_XA=dχ_X$ generates $C_X=P(X)-qχ_X$. Its low-velocity limit is the Noether--Bessel-Hagen charge of the coordinate-time Lagrangian, from which generalized linear momentum, angular momentum and energy follow generator by generator. A uniform magnetic field gives an elementary gauge-dependent representative but gauge-invariant translational charge. The Darwin interaction then supplies the conservative $O(c^{-2})$ many-particle realization after field elimination, while a closed particle--field system is governed by the total stress-energy Killing charge. The result is an explicit reduction map linking three established descriptions without identifying their distinct conserved quantities.

</details>


### [187] [Homogenization theory of topological Anderson insulators: genuine gaps and pseudo-gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38150)
*Guillaume Bal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为无序诱导的拓扑安德森绝缘体建立了一套均匀化理论：高频小尺度涨落经二阶（Born）公式给出一个一阶修正M，改变系统的拓扑相；半平面涨落产生携带体差不变量的界面；随机涨落下均匀化谱隙变为赝隙，作者定义沿避开稀有区域的割线的指标，并证明其几乎必然等于Born近似均匀化算子的体差不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑安德森绝缘体的非平庸拓扑相由无序诱导产生，但缺乏严格的数学理论来描述快速振荡涨落如何宏观地改变拓扑。文章旨在为常系数椭圆系统在尺度ε振荡涨落下的均质化与拓扑相变提供严格框架。

Method: 对常系数椭圆系统施加尺度为ε的振荡涨落，利用二阶Born公式得到一阶修正M，并证明算子按范数预解收敛到均匀化极限；构造半平面涨落界面并考察其体差不变量；对随机涨落定义沿避开稀有区域割线的指标，针对低阶矩消失的一阶合金与包含BHZ模型的临界标量无序二阶系统证明该指标几乎必然等于Born近似的体差不变量。

Result: 证明了适当振幅的周期涨落不平均掉而是产生一阶校正M并改变拓扑相；半平面涨落生成的界面在ε足够小时携带均匀化算子的体差不变量且对局部缺陷稳定；随机情形下均匀化谱隙因稀有区域被填满而变为赝隙，但定义的割线指标几乎必然等于Born近似均匀化算子的体差不变量。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从微观涨落到宏观拓扑不变量的严格均匀化桥梁：周期与半平面涨落分别给出明确的Born修正与稳定界面拓扑，随机涨落则在避开稀有区域的割线上仍保持Born近似下的体差拓扑不变量，从而为拓扑安德森绝缘体提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: A topological Anderson insulator is an insulator whose non-trivial topological phase is induced by disorder. We develop a homogenization theory of this phenomenon for constant-coefficient elliptic systems perturbed by fluctuations oscillating at scale $\varepsilon$. Periodic fluctuations of every order below that of the operator, with appropriate amplitudes, do not average out: they produce an order-one correction $M$ given by a second-order (Born) formula, and the operator converges in the norm-resolvent sense to its homogenized limit. In particular, electric potentials and magnetic fields of zero mean, usually regarded as topologically trivial perturbations, generate corrections $M$ that change the topological phase. Fluctuations confined to a half-plane create an interface that carries the bulk-difference invariant of the homogenized operator for all $\varepsilon$ sufficiently small, a property that is stable under local defects. For random fluctuations, rare regions typically fill the homogenized gap, which becomes a pseudo-gap, and the standard interface index need no longer be defined. We define an index along cuts deformed so as to avoid the rare regions, and prove that it equals almost surely the bulk-difference invariant of the operator homogenized in the Born approximation, for first-order alloys with vanishing low moments and for a class of second-order systems with scalar disorder at criticality that includes the BHZ model.

</details>


### [188] [Nonequilibrium Thermodynamics of Semilinear Stochastic Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38166)
*Zelin Liu,Hao Ge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为半线性随机偏微分方程（SPDE）建立了严格的非平衡热力学数学框架，以关联 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程的不变高斯测度（未必时间可逆）为参考测度，定义热力学量、导出熵平衡方程，给出熵产生率的显式表达式，并证明其消失等价于时间可逆性；同时建立了样本路径熵产生与 Radon-Nikodym 导数之间的对应关系及涨落定理，并将理论应用于多个具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机过程非平衡热力学理论主要针对有限维或特定可逆情形，缺乏对半线性 SPDE 这类无限维、且参考测度未必时间可逆系统的严格数学框架。作者旨在将熵产生、涨落定理、housekeeping 热与自由能耗散等概念系统地推广到这类具有空间结构的随机系统。

Method: 以关联 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程的不变高斯测度作为参考测度（不要求时间可逆），用概率密度定义热力学量并推导熵平衡方程；给出熵产生率的显式公式并证明其为零与时间可逆性的等价关系；将样本路径熵产生表示为前向路径测度关于时间反演路径测度的 Radon-Nikodym 导数的对数，并证明其满足涨落定理；对非平稳系统将样本路径熵产生分解为 housekeeping 热与自由能耗散两部分，两者各自满足积分涨落定理、均非负，且自由能耗散均值等于相对熵的减少；最后将理论应用于半线性随机热方程（含空间相关噪声）、随机聚合物模型、散度自由漂移的非线性模型及与不同温度热库接触的两个耦合随机热方程。

Result: 建立了完整的熵平衡与熵产生理论，证明平稳态熵产生率为零等价于时间可逆性；证明样本路径熵产生满足涨落定理；非平稳情形下熵产生可分解为 housekeeping 热与自由能耗散，二者均满足积分涨落定理且期望非负，自由能耗散均值等于相对熵的下降；并在四个具体 SPDE 模型上验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为半线性 SPDE 的非平衡热力学提供了一套严格、系统的数学框架，将涨落定理、housekeeping 热、自由能耗散等现代随机热力学概念成功推广到无限维空间系统，并通过多个物理模型展示了其适用性，为研究空间扩展系统的非平衡统计力学奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous mathematical framework for the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations. The invariant Gaussian measure of the associated Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, which need not be time-reversible, serves as the reference measure. We define thermodynamic quantities in terms of probability densities and derive the entropy balance equation. We obtain explicit expressions for the entropy production rate and prove that its vanishing in the stationary state is equivalent to time reversibility. Sample-path entropy production is identified with the logarithm of the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the forward path measure with respect to the time-reversed path measure and is shown to satisfy fluctuation theorems. For nonstationary systems, sample-path entropy production decomposes into housekeeping heat and free-energy dissipation, each satisfying an integral fluctuation theorem. Both have nonnegative expectations, and the mean free-energy dissipation equals the decrease in relative entropy with respect to the stationary distribution. The theory is applied to semilinear stochastic heat equations with spatially correlated noise, a random polymer model, a nonlinear model with a divergence-free drift, and two coupled stochastic heat equations in contact with heat baths at different temperatures.

</details>


### [189] [Finite-volume and thermodynamic-limit lower bounds for the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model via the Lieb--Yamazaki method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38464)
*Maciej Janowicz,Joanna Kaleta,Filip Krzyżewski,Andrzej Zembrzuski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用 Lieb–Yamazaki 正算子分解，为相干驱动的 Bose–Hubbard 模型推导出基态能量的严格下界，得到一个二次型界定哈密顿量，并通过位移与 Bogoliubov 变换对其对角化，从而得到显式稳定性条件、任意维度的有限体积下界、热力学极限能量密度、激发谱及高斯态可观测量，并数值检验了界紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为相干驱动的非平衡 Bose–Hubbard 模型建立严格的基态能量下界与稳定性判据，克服该模型缺乏变分原理、难以直接求基态的困难，从而在任意维度下获得可靠的有限体积和热力学极限结果。

Method: 采用 Lieb–Yamazaki 正算子分解，将原哈密顿量以严格不等式下界为一个二次型界定哈密顿量；随后通过位移变换和 Bogoliubov 变换将其对角化，解析地导出稳定性条件、有限体积能量下界、热力学极限能量密度及激发谱，并计算高斯态下的填充数、粒子数涨落和二阶关联函数，最后用数值计算评估下界的紧致程度。

Result: 得到显式的稳定性条件与任意维度下的有限体积能量下界；确立了热力学极限能量密度并刻画了界定哈密顿量的激发谱；导出了高斯态可观测量的解析表达式，识别出反聚束和亚泊松统计的参数区域；数值结果表明所提下界在代表性参数区间内具有良好的紧致性。

Conclusion: 通过正算子分解结合高斯态方法，可以为相干驱动 Bose–Hubbard 模型提供严格的、可解析处理的能量下界与稳定性判据，同时揭示其量子统计特性（反聚束、亚泊松性），为后续研究非平衡玻色系统提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We derive rigorous lower bounds on the ground-state energy of the coherently driven Bose--Hubbard model using a Lieb--Yamazaki positive-operator decomposition. The resulting quadratic bounding Hamiltonian is diagonalized by displacement and Bogoliubov transformations, yielding explicit stability conditions and finite-volume bounds in arbitrary dimension. We establish the thermodynamic-limit energy density and characterize the excitation spectrum of the bounding Hamiltonian. We also derive its Gaussian-state observables, including filling, number fluctuations, and second-order correlations, and identify regimes of antibunching and sub-Poissonian statistics. Numerical calculations assess the tightness of the bounds across representative parameter regimes.

</details>


### [190] [A Primer on Instanton Physics - A Short Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38717)
*Pervez Hoodbhoy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 这是一份面向高年级本科生和低年级研究生的六讲讲义，系统介绍量子力学和量子场论中的瞬子（instanton），前四讲讲解粒子力学，后两讲介绍半经典场论中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为高年级本科生和刚刚进入研究生阶段的学生提供瞬子的入门教学，通过先打好粒子力学基础再过渡到场论应用，降低学习门槛。

Method: 采用六讲系列讲座的教学形式，前四讲专注粒子力学以建立必要背景，后两讲涵盖半经典场论中的瞬子应用。

Result: 完成了从粒子力学到场论中瞬子概念的渐进式介绍，构建了一套完整的基础教学框架。

Conclusion: 该系列讲座通过循序渐进的结构，为初学者搭建了理解量子力学和量子场论中瞬子的完整入门路径。

Abstract: These six lectures, delivered at the University of New Brunswick 6-21 October 2026, are an introduction to instantons in quantum mechanics and quantum field theory for senior undergraduate and beginning graduate students. The first four lectures deal solely with particle mechanics, building the necessary background for the last two that cover applications in semi-classical field theory.

</details>


### [191] [From Entanglement Bootstrap to DHR Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38821)
*Kohtaro Kato,Yoshiko Ogata*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维量子自旋系统框架下，建立了纠缠引导（entanglement bootstrap）与 Doplicher–Haag–Roberts（DHR）超选择扇区理论之间的直接联系，证明 Haag 对偶性，并给出简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间的一一对应，同时为每个融合道构造了密度矩阵与正规化正交织子之间的仿射双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子（anyon）既可由熵方法（纠缠引导）描述，也可由算子代数方法（DHR 超选择扇区）描述，但此前二者之间缺乏直接桥接，往往需借助可精确求解的格点模型作为中介。本文旨在无需这类中介模型，直接建立两种描述框架的等价对应。

Method: 在二维量子自旋系统的纠缠引导假设下，运用算子代数方法处理超选择扇区：首先证明 Haag 对偶性，随后建立简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间的一一对应；对每个融合道，进一步构造从纠缠引导融合空间上的密度矩阵到相应算子代数融合道中正规化正交织子的仿射双射。

Result: 作者证明了 Haag 对偶性成立，并建立了简单纠缠引导任意子类型与不可约超选择扇区之间的一一对应；对每个融合道，构造出纠缠引导融合空间密度矩阵与算子代数融合道正规化正交织子之间的仿射双射，从而不需要经过可精确求解的格点模型就直接连通了熵描述与算子代数描述。

Conclusion: 本文成功地在二维量子自旋系统中直接桥接了纠缠引导与 DHR 超选择扇区理论，表明任意子的熵描述与算子代数描述本质一致，为任意子理论的统一理解提供了不依赖可解模型的新途径。

Abstract: We establish a direct connection between the entanglement bootstrap and the Doplicher--Haag--Roberts theory of superselection sectors for two-dimensional quantum spin systems. Under the entanglement-bootstrap assumptions, we prove Haag duality and establish a one-to-one correspondence between simple entanglement-bootstrap anyon types and irreducible superselection sectors. For each fusion channel, we further construct an affine bijection from density matrices on the entanglement-bootstrap fusion space to normalized positive intertwiners in the corresponding operator-algebraic fusion channel. These results connect the entropic and operator-algebraic descriptions of anyons directly, without passing through exactly solvable lattice models.

</details>


### [192] [The geometry of Floquet states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38845)
*Vladimir Y. Cherynak,John R. Klein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"良态"周期哈密顿量（即具有非退化 Floquet 谱的周期哈密顿量）的模空间，利用 Floquet 态构造其几何模型，并完全确定该空间的有理上同调环；同时提出两个关于绝热驱动下渐近量子演化的猜想，作为 Floquet 版本的绝热定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期含时量子系统（如周期驱动量子系统）的动力学由 Floquet 理论刻画，但具有非退化 Floquet 谱的周期哈密顿量所构成的空间缺乏系统的几何与拓扑描述，限制了对这类系统全局性质以及绝热演化渐近行为的理解。

Method: 利用非退化 Floquet 谱对应的 Floquet 态构造周期哈密顿量模空间的几何模型，该模型借助位形空间、旗流形及主丛的语言表达；在此基础上计算该空间的有理上同调环。

Result: 给出了良态周期哈密顿量模空间的显式几何模型，并作为主要定理的推论，完全确定了该空间的有理上同调环。

Conclusion: 该工作将周期哈密顿量的拓扑结构归结为位形空间、旗流形与主丛的几何描述；此外，针对单参数良态周期哈密顿量族的绝热驱动，提出了描述渐近量子演化的两个猜想，构成 Floquet 情形的绝热定理。

Abstract: This paper studies the moduli space of "good" periodic Hamiltonians, i.e., periodic Hamiltonians with nondegenerate Floquet spectrum. The corresponding Floquet states determine a geometric model for the moduli space of such Hamiltonians. The model is expressed in terms of configuration spaces, flag manifolds, and principal bundles. A corollary of the main result is the complete determination of the rational cohomology ring of the space of good periodic Hamiltonians.
  The paper also considers the adiabatic driving of a 1-parameter family of good periodic Hamiltonians. We formulate two conjectures describing the resulting asymptotic quantum evolution. These are Floquet analogues of the adiabatic theorem in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [193] [A First-Order Phase Transition in Spin-Imbalanced BCS Theory of Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38961)
*Marius Lemm,Barbara Roos*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了自旋不平衡（自旋依赖化学势）的平移不变 BCS 超导泛函中会发生一阶相变：在低于线性稳定阈值的共存耦合处，Cooper 对密度出现非零跳跃、最小化能量出现折点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Caldas–Mota 的数值工作与 Freiji–Hainzl–Seiringer 的解析工作提示，在自旋不平衡 BCS 模型中可能存在一种"正常态全局不稳定但局部仍稳定"的非常规一阶相变。本文旨在对该现象给出严格数学证明。

Method: 研究自旋 1/2 费米子、自旋依赖化学势 μ₊≠μ₋ 的平移不变 BCS 泛函。核心方法是通过将 BCS 能量最小化分解为两部分：在固定配对下对单粒子密度矩阵取最小化的"内层"问题，该内层问题可经 Legendre–Fenchel 变换精确求解，从而得到一个矫顽力（coercivity）估计。

Result: 对每个充分小的固定自旋不平衡和充分低的温度，存在一个严格低于正常态线性稳定阈值 λ_lin 的共存耦合 λ_fo，在该耦合处 Cooper 对密度发生非零跳跃，且最小化能量出现折点（kink），从而证明一阶相变发生。

Conclusion: 严格确立了自旋不平衡 BCS 泛函中一阶相变的存在性：正常态在全局上失稳但仍保持局部稳定，相变发生在低于线性稳定阈值的共存耦合处，表现为序参量的不连续跳跃与能量的一阶 kink。该方法论上的矫顽力估计为分析此类问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study a translation-invariant BCS functional of superconductivity for a system of spin-$1/2$ fermions with spin-dependent chemical potentials, $μ_+\neqμ_-$. Numerical work of Caldas and Mota and analytical work of Freiji, Hainzl, and Seiringer has suggested an unusual first-order phase transition in spin-imbalanced BCS models, in which the normal state becomes globally unstable while remaining locally stable. Here, we prove that a first-order phase transition occurs in such spin-imbalanced BCS functionals. For each sufficiently small fixed spin imbalance and sufficiently low temperature, there is a coexistence coupling $λ_{\mathrm{fo}}$ strictly below the linear-stability threshold $λ_{\mathrm{lin}}$ of the normal state at which the Cooper pair density exhibits a nonzero jump and the minimized energy has a kink. The methodological insight is a coercivity estimate obtained by decomposing the BCS energy minimization into two parts, where the ``inner'' problem minimizes over the one-particle density matrices at fixed pairing and can be exactly solved by a Legendre--Fenchel transform.

</details>


### [194] [Minimally-coupled Quasilinear Symmetric Hyperbolic Systems, Naturally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39107)
*Eleanor March,James Owen Weatherall*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在自然方程（自然丛上射丛的闭子对象）的框架下研究对称双曲性与适定性，提出将非自然方程“自然化”，并利用“极小耦合”从单流形上的方程构造自然方程（在同构意义下唯一）。文中定义了极小耦合自然方程的对称化子、双曲化与约束，并由此恢复标准的存在唯一性结果（相对于任意背景结构，对极小耦合系统而言即一个度量）。此外，极小耦合与自然性共同意味着：一阶拟线性极小耦合自然对称双曲自然方程的因果锥必与度量光锥重合，且与自然双曲化相关联的某个二阶张量相对于相应Levi-Civita导数算子必为无散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于在自然方程这一几何框架下统一处理对称双曲性与适定性问题，克服非自然方程缺乏背景无关性的缺陷，并通过极小耦合提供一种唯一且几何上自然的方程构造方法，从而为偏微分方程的适定性理论建立与微分几何结构相容的坚实基础。

Method: 本文采用自然丛与射丛的范畴论/微分几何语言：将方程视为自然丛上射丛的闭子对象；对非自然方程构造其“自然化”；利用极小耦合把定义在单个流形上的方程提升为自然方程（并证明其同构唯一性）；在该框架下定义对称化子、双曲化与约束，进而借助任意背景度量（对极小耦合系统）恢复存在唯一性定理，最后结合自然性论证因果锥与光锥一致以及相关张量的无散性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 非自然方程可被自然化；(2) 极小耦合提供了在同构意义下唯一的自然方程构造方法；(3) 极小耦合自然方程的对称化子、双曲化与约束可被明确定义，并恢复标准存在唯一性结果；(4) 对一阶、拟线性、极小耦合的自然对称双曲自然方程，其因果锥必与度量光锥重合；(5) 与自然双曲化相关联的某个二阶张量相对于相应Levi-Civita导数算子必为无散。

Conclusion: 本文表明自然性与极小耦合的几何框架能够系统且唯一地刻画对称双曲方程的适定性结构，并带来强结构性结论：因果锥与度量光锥重合、相关张量无散。该框架为理解背景无关的偏微分方程适定性提供了统一且严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider symmetric hyperbolicity and well-posedness in the context of natural equations, which are closed subobjects of jet bundles over natural bundles. We show how non-natural equations can be "naturalized", and we discuss how a precise version of "minimal coupling" offers a (provably unique, up to isomorphism) method for constructing natural equations from certain equations defined on a single manifold. We discuss how to define symmetrizers, hyperbolizations, and constraints for minimally coupled natural equations, and we show how these can be used to recover standard existence and uniqueness results, relative to any choice of background structure (a metric, for minimally coupled systems). Additionally, we show that minimal coupling and naturality together imply that the causal cones associated with a (first order, quasilinear, minimally coupled) naturally symmetric hyperbolic natural equation must always coincide with the metric lightcones, and that a certain rank-2 tensor, associates with a natural hyperbolization, must always be divergence free relative to the associated Levi-Civita derivative operator.

</details>


### [195] [Higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39382)
*Takashi Ichikawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由 DHS 联络定义的高亏格多重对数函数，证明在分离退化下，DHS 联络可由椭圆曲线上的 BL 联络与带标记点黎曼球上的 KZ 联络粘合构造，从而给出高亏格多重对数作为变形参数及其对数的幂级数，其系数由多重 zeta 值与椭圆多重 zeta 值表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亏格多重对数函数的结构复杂，缺乏类似低亏格情形的显式刻画；作者希望借助一族黎曼曲面的分离退化，将高亏格对象分解为已知的低维对象（椭圆曲线与黎曼球）上的结构，从而获得显式描述。

Method: 考虑任意亏格、带标记点的黎曼曲面族上的 D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer（DHS）联络；在黎曼曲面的分离退化下，证明 DHS 联络可由椭圆曲线上的 Brown-Levin（BL）联络与带标记点黎曼球上的 Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov（KZ）联络通过粘合（gluing）构造。

Result: 证明 DHS 联络在分离退化下可由 BL 联络与 KZ 联络粘合得到；相应地，相关的高亏格多重对数可显式表示成变形参数及其（多重）对数形式的幂级数，级数系数由多重 zeta 值与椭圆多重 zeta 值给出。

Conclusion: 该结果将对高亏格多重对数的研究化归为椭圆曲线与黎曼球上已知结构的组合，提供了显式的幂级数展开表达，并用多重 zeta 值和椭圆多重 zeta 值完全确定其系数。

Abstract: We study higher genus polylogarithms associated with the D'Hoker-Hidding-Schlotterer (DHS) connection defined on families of Riemann surfaces of any genus with marked points. Under separating degenerations of Riemann surfaces, we show that the DHS connection can be constructed by gluing the Brown-Levin (BL) connections on elliptic curves and the Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ) connections on pointed Riemann spheres. Therefore, the associated higher genus polylogarithms can be described explicitly as power series in deformation parameters and their logarithms for the families of Riemann surfaces whose coefficients are expressed by multiple zeta values and elliptic multiple zeta values.

</details>


### [196] [Generalized Frobenius Structures on Hurwitz Spaces, Their Open Extensions and Associated Principal Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39411)
*Shilin Ma*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在任意亏格的 Hurwitz 空间上构造广义 Frobenius 结构，利用扩展主微分与 Cauchy 核的围道积分推导出无弥散 Lax 表示的主方程族及其近对偶版本，并构造开 WDVV 方程的解及相应秩一扩张的主方程族，最后证明这些构造容许 Shramchenko 变形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Hurwitz 空间上的 Frobenius 结构与可积系统理论联系起来，推广至任意亏格，并研究其主方程族、开 WDVV 方程及可变形性。

Method: 利用扩展主微分构造广义 Frobenius 结构；通过涉及 Cauchy 核的围道积分推导显式的无弥散 Lax 表示；构造原结构与近对偶结构的开 WDVV 方程解，并推导所得秩一扩张的显式主方程族；最后讨论 Shramchenko 变形。

Result: 得到任意亏格 Hurwitz 空间上广义 Frobenius 结构的主方程族及其近对偶版本的显式无弥散 Lax 表示；构造出原结构与近对偶结构的开 WDVV 方程解，并给出相应秩一扩张的显式主方程族；证明这些构造均可进行 Shramchenko 变形。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Hurwitz 空间上广义 Frobenius 结构与其主方程族、开 WDVV 方程解及秩一扩张和 Shramchenko 变形之间的完整联系，为相关可积系统与拓扑场论研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We construct generalized Frobenius structures on Hurwitz spaces of arbitrary genus using extended primary differentials. Through contour integrals involving the Cauchy kernel, we derive explicit dispersionless Lax representations for the principal hierarchies of these structures and their almost-dual counterparts. We also construct solutions to the open WDVV equations for both the original and almost-dual structures, and derive explicit principal hierarchies for the resulting rank-one extensions. Finally, we show that these constructions admit Shramchenko deformations.

</details>


### [197] [Arithmetic localization for quasiperiodic long-range operators with monotone potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39448)
*Hongyi Cao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维拟周期算子的均匀安德森局域化，势函数为α-Hölder单调，长程跳跃满足指数衰减且具有小参数ε；在ρ>αβ条件下证明均匀局域化，证明推广了Jitomirskaya与Kachkovskiy的算术局域化策略，且不依赖Lyapunov指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟周期算子的算术局域化框架主要适用于短程（或衰减足够快）的Schrödinger算子，且常依赖Lyapunov指数的分析。将局域化结果推广到长程跳跃（衰减率仅需ρ>αβ）是核心动机。

Method: 采用算术局域化策略，将Jitomirskaya和Kachkovskiy关于单调拟周期Schrödinger算子的方法推广到长程跳跃情形；通过对频率共振的指数强度β、势的Hölder指数α与跳跃衰减率ρ的关系（ρ>αβ）以及小参数ε的显式条件进行傅里叶/多尺度分析，证明特征函数指数衰减，且全程不依赖Lyapunov指数。

Result: 在ε满足显式小性条件且ρ>αβ时，证明了此类一维长程跳跃、α-Hölder单调势拟周期算子的均匀安德森局域化。

Conclusion: 在仅要求跳跃按指数率ρ衰减、且ρ>αβ的条件下即可实现均匀安德森局域化，将算术局域化策略成功扩展到长程跳跃模型，并摆脱了对Lyapunov指数的依赖。

Abstract: We study one-dimensional quasiperiodic operators with $α$-Hölder monotone potentials and long-range hopping satisfying $|T(x,y)|\leq \varepsilon e^{-ρ|x-y|}$. Under an explicit smallness condition on $\varepsilon$, we establish uniform Anderson localization for such operators in the regime $ρ>αβ$, where $β$ is the exponential strength of frequency resonances. Our proof extends the arithmetic localization strategy for monotone quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators by Jitomirskaya and Kachkovskiy to the long-range setting, without relying on the Lyapunov exponent.

</details>


### [198] [A Combinatorial Formula and a Hilbert Scheme Realization of a Rank-One Virasoro Irregular Conformal Block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39786)
*Hajime Nagoya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出秩一 Virasoro 非正则共形块 (0,0,1) 型在非正则奇点处形式展开的组合公式与几何实现，并在 c=1 时证明了作者猜想，将系数表为 skew-tableau 数与 Littlewood–Richardson 系数的非负整数组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正则 Virasoro 共形块在一般中心荷下缺乏具体的组合/几何描述；作者希望给出其非正则奇点处形式级数的显式组合公式与几何实现，并解决 c=1 情形下展开系数的非负性与整性猜想。

Method: 将归一化共形块实现为与 C^2 上 Hilbert 点概型相关的局部化等变 Hilbert–Fock 空间上的系数矩阵元；运用筛选积分的扇形渐近分析与 Laguerre–Jack 求值相结合的方法；用升次微分算子的流表示筛选核，并借助单盒 Pieri 规则及 Okounkov–Olshanski 的平移 Jack 系数公式计算其指数的 Jack 系数。

Result: 对一般中心荷得到 (0,0,1) 型非正则共形块在非正则奇点展开的组合公式和几何实现；在 c=1 时证明作者猜想，将参数无关系数表为 skew-tableau 数与 Littlewood–Richardson 系数，并建立其非负性与整性。

Conclusion: 文章完成了秩一 Virasoro 非正则共形块 (0,0,1) 型在非正则奇点形式展开的组合化与几何化，并在 c=1 时用对称函数方法证明了系数的非负整性猜想。

Abstract: For general central charge, we give a combinatorial formula and a geometric realization of the formal expansion of the rank-one Virasoro irregular conformal block of type $(0,0,1)$ at its irregular singular point. We realize the normalized block as a coefficientwise matrix element on the localized equivariant Hilbert--Fock space associated with Hilbert schemes of points on $\mathbb C^2$. At $c=1$, we prove the author's conjecture and express the parameter-independent coefficients in terms of skew-tableau numbers and Littlewood--Richardson coefficients, establishing their nonnegativity and integrality. The proof combines sectorial asymptotics of screening integrals with Laguerre--Jack evaluations. We express the screening kernel using the flow of a degree-raising differential operator and compute the Jack coefficients of its exponential from the one-box Pieri rule and the shifted Jack coefficient formula of Okounkov and Olshanski.

</details>


### [199] [An Egorov-type Theorem for Analytic Wick Observables in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39806)
*Julien Malartre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在半经典参数 ε>0 下，为具有超指数衰减系数的解析 Wick 可观测量在时间依赖二次哈密顿量作用下的传播，给出了一个由收敛展开式表示的精确 Egorov 型公式，推广了 S. Breteaux 关于多项式符号的结果，并涵盖空间截断的 P(φ)₂ 模型及 Höegh-Krohn 模型的一个变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Egorov 定理刻画了量子力学中可观测量在半经典极限下随时间演化的传播性质。已有的精确结果（如 Breteaux 的工作）仅适用于多项式符号，本文旨在将精确 Egorov 型公式推广到更一般的解析 Wick 可观测量（系数超指数衰减），从而覆盖更广泛、更具物理意义的量子场论模型。

Method: 针对时间依赖的二次哈密顿量，作者构造了可观测量传播的精确 Egorov 型公式，将其表示为关于半经典参数 ε 的收敛展开（而非通常的渐近或形式展开）。工作在 Wick 符号的解析类上进行，该类要求系数满足超指数衰减条件，并利用解析性与衰减性保证展开的收敛性，进而将结果应用于具体模型。

Result: 得到了解析 Wick 可观测量在时变二次哈密顿量作用下传播的精确 Egorov 型公式，该公式以 ε 的收敛级数形式给出；成功将 Breteaux 的多项式符号结果推广到超指数衰减的解析符号类，并验证其适用于空间截断的 P(φ)₂ 模型和 Höegh-Krohn 模型变体。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一般解析 Wick 符号类下的精确 Egorov 型传播公式，其 ε 展开收敛而非仅形式有效，显著扩展了先前多项式符号的理论框架，并通过两个具体量子场论模型展示了该结果的适用范围与价值。

Abstract: We provide an exact Egorov-type formula, given by a convergent expansion in the semi-classical parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, for the propagation of a certain class of analytic Wick observables with super-exponentially decaying coefficients, under the action of a time-dependent quadratic Hamiltonian. This work generalizes a result established by S. Breteaux for polynomial symbols, and includes the spatially cutoff $P(\varphi)_2$ model, as well as a variant of the Höegh-Krohn model.

</details>


### [200] [Three-dimensional stress-gradient non-locality problem: Displacement field for an arbitrary and a conservative force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39888)
*Slađan Jelić,Dušan Zorica*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在无界域上解析求解了三维应力梯度非局部介质的静力学问题，针对标量型和张量型 Eringen 模型分别获得位移场的解析表达式，并在重力体力下进行图形表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意体力和保守体力情况下，需要获得应力梯度非局部介质三维静力学问题的位移场，以系统比较不同非局部模型（标量型和张量型 Eringen 模型）的解析结果。

Method: 考虑由平衡方程、无穷小应变以及标量型和张量型应力梯度 Eringen 三维模型组成的线性方程组，在无界域上解析求解。由于系统线性，每个模型的解表示为各模型共有的局部型位移场与各模型非局部算子特有的非局部型位移场的叠加。考察了三个标量型模型和五个张量型模型。

Result: 对于任意体力，共得到六种不同的解析位移场表达式：一种对应于某个标量型模型，三种分别对应于单个张量型模型，其余两种表达式各为某一标量型与某一张量型模型所共有。在重力体力情况下，位移场图形化后仅呈现五种不同模型，因为两个张量型模型在体力保守时变得相同。

Conclusion: 通过解析求解与图形对比，揭示了不同标量型和张量型 Eringen 应力梯度非局部模型间的异同，部分模型在任意体力下可共享同一位移场表达式，且张量型模型在保守体力下会发生等价。

Abstract: In order to obtain the displacement field, both generally in the case of an arbitrary body force and specifically in the case of a conservative body force, the mechanical problem of statics of three-dimensional stress-gradient non-local medium is analytically solved on unbounded domain by considering a system of equations consisting of an equilibrium equation, infinitesimal strain, and both scalar- and tensor-type stress-gradient Eringen three-dimensional models. Since the system of equations is linear, for each model the solution is obtained in the form of a superposition of the local-type displacement field, common to each model, and the non-local-type displacement field, specific to the non-locality operator of the model. Among the three scalar-type and five tensor-type considered models, six distinct analytical displacement field expressions are obtained for an arbitrary body force: one expression specific to a scalar-type model, three specific to individual tensor-type models, while each of the remaining two expressions are common to one scalar- and one tensor-type model. The displacement vector fields are graphically represented in the case of gravitational body force, yielding five different models, since two tensor-type models become the same when the force is conservative.

</details>


### [201] [Optimization Inverse Spectral Problems for Sturm--Liouville Operators with Potentials in $L^p(0,1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40231)
*Nur Valeev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维 Sturm–Liouville 算子的优化逆谱问题（OISP）：在有限区间上，寻找与给定先验势 $V_0$ 在 $L^p$ 度量下距离最小、且实现指定有限谱 $\{\lambda_i^*\}$ 的势 $V\in L^p(0,1)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆谱问题要求在给定谱数据下重构势，但解不唯一；作者希望引入先验势 $V_0$ 并求解最优（最接近 $V_0$）的重构，从而获得正则化、稳定的逆问题框架，并服务于声学/电磁谐振器的优化谱设计与诊断等应用。

Method: 将问题建模为 $L^p$ 意义下的约束优化；利用等谱流形及其切空间（由特征函数平方张成）刻画可行集。核心工具是"平方特征函数的微分-代数统一方法"，并引入将平方特征函数线性组合作幂映射以获得最优势的显式解析表示。还提出冻结特征函数的迭代格式及带谱数据连续化的局部 Newton–KKT 方法；用 Baire 范畴论证泛型全局唯一性。

Result: 证明等谱流形的正则性、OISP 解的存在性及最优势的显式解析表示；当平方特征函数线性相关时，相应势可由它们显式表达且为解析函数。对 $p=2$ 证明非退化驻点孤立、且若每个全局极小非退化则全局极小个数有限；对任意 $1<p<\infty$，当 $\lambda_1^*\ge\lambda_1(V_0)$ 时第一特征值唯一，并在 $L^p(0,1)$ 中按 Baire 范畴意义下泛型全局唯一。对 $m=1,2$ 给出在充分高有限对称势垒下存在至少两个全局极小的例子，并明确给出迭代与 Newton–KKT 方法的适用边界。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了 OISP 的正则性、存在性与显式结构问题，刻画了唯一性条件及其泛型性，并揭示了非唯一性（多全局极小）的存在情形；所提数值方法及对非线性边值问题、声学/电磁谐振器诊断与最优谱设计的应用为后续研究与工程提供了基础。

Abstract: We study an optimization inverse spectral problem (OISP) for a one-dimensional Sturm--Liouville operator on a finite interval. The problem is to find a potential $V \in L^p(0,1)$ that realizes a prescribed finite set $λ_1^* < λ_2^* < \dots < λ_m^*$ and has the smallest $L^p(0,1)$-distance from an a priori given potential $V_0 \in L^p(0,1)$.
  We formulate and prove results on the regularity of the isospectral manifold, the existence of an OISP solution, and an explicit analytic representation of the optimal potential by means of a power map applied to a linear combination of squared eigenfunctions.
  Using a unified differential-algebraic approach to squared eigenfunctions, we show that whenever these squares are linearly dependent, the corresponding potential can be expressed in terms of them and is an analytic function.
  For $p=2$, we prove that nondegenerate stationary points are isolated and that the number of global minima is finite provided that each of them is nondegenerate. For every $1<p<\infty$, we establish uniqueness for the first eigenvalue when $λ_1^*\geλ_1(V_0)$ and generic global uniqueness in the sense of Baire category in $L^p(0,1)$.
  A separate section provides examples with at least two global minima for $m=1,2$ in the presence of a sufficiently high finite symmetric barrier. We describe an iteration with frozen eigenfunctions and a local Newton--KKT method with continuation in the spectral data, and state explicitly the limits of their guarantees.
  The final part discusses applications of OISP theory to nonlinear boundary value problems and to applied problems of diagnostics and optimal spectral design of acoustic and electromagnetic resonators.

</details>


### [202] [Three-dimensional QCA that are not stably circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40235)
*Bowen Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 作者证明：对所有奇素数 p，三维手性 Z_p Clifford 量子细胞自动机（QCA）即使在张量任意辅助自由度并与平移复合后，仍不是有限深度量子电路。此前该结论仅在 Clifford 范畴内成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于这些三维手性 QCA 非有限深度电路的结论只在 Clifford 范畴内成立，作者希望突破这一限制，证明在完全一般的（非 Clifford）设定下结论依然成立，从而推导出三维 QCA 模去稳定子电路和平移后的群并非有限生成。

Method: 关键工具是构造了一个可逆子代数的新不变量。作者猜想该不变量等于所承载的任意子理论的总量子维度的平方（模去有理平方）。

Result: 证明了 Haah 及 Sun–Yang–Wang–Tantivasadakarn–Chen 的三维手性 Z_p Clifford QCA，对每个奇素数 p，即使在张量任意辅助自由度并与平移复合后，也不是有限深度量子电路；由此推出三维 QCA 模去稳定子电路和平移的群不是有限生成的。

Conclusion: 非有限深度的结论从 Clifford 范畴推广到了一般情形，并借助新的可逆子代数不变量完成证明；作者进一步猜想该不变量等于所承载任意子理论总量子维度的平方（模去有理平方）。

Abstract: We prove that the three-dimensional chiral $\Z_p$ Clifford quantum cellular automata of Haah and of Sun--Yang--Wang--Tantivasadakarn--Chen are, for every odd prime $p$, not finite-depth quantum circuits, even after tensoring with arbitrary ancillary degrees of freedom and composing with translations. Previously such statements were known only inside the Clifford category. As a consequence, the group of three-dimensional QCA modulo stabilized circuits and translations is not finitely generated. The key ingredient is a new invariant of invertible subalgebras, which we conjecture to be the square of the total quantum dimension of the hosted anyon theory, modulo rational squares.

</details>


### [203] [Symmetries and exact solutions of stochastic differential equations: the CIR model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40316)
*Miguel A. Rodríguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从随机微分方程（SDE）的对称性角度解释了Cox-Ingersoll-Ross（CIR）利率模型中著名的参数临界关系，并利用对称性求得模型解，从而复现了近期关于SDE及其前向Kolmogorov方程对称性关系的研究结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIR过程是利率建模中的经典模型，其参数间存在广为人知的临界关系（即Feller条件），但该临界关系的深层数学根源尚待阐明。作者希望从对称性这一更本质的角度来解释为何会出现这一临界关系。

Method: 作者研究CIR模型对应随机微分方程的对称性（李对称分析），利用所发现的对称性解释参数临界关系的成因，并借助该对称性直接求解模型。

Result: 成功用对称性解释了CIR模型参数间的临界关系，并利用该对称性计算出模型的解；同时复现了一篇近期论文中关于随机微分方程与前向Kolmogorov方程对称性之间关系的更一般性结论。

Conclusion: CIR模型中的临界关系可通过对随机微分方程对称性的研究加以解释，对称性方法不仅揭示了临界条件的来源，还能用于求解模型，并与前向Kolmogorov方程对称性的相关理论相印证。

Abstract: It is well known that in the celebrated Cox-Ingersoll-Ross process for interest rates there exists a critical relation among the model parameters. In the present work, we explain this critical relation by studying the symmetries of the stochastic differential equation and compute the solution of the model using this symmetry. In this way we also recover results of a recent paper where this property has been studied within a broader context by analyzing the relationships among the symmetries of stochastic differential equations and their corresponding forward Kolmogorov equations.

</details>


### [204] [Twinor Theory: Field Equations from $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$ Spin-Tractors: A Twin to Twistor Theory Modelled on $(A)dS_4^{\mathbb C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40435)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Twinor理论作为扭量理论的时空对应物，基于复化(A)dS₄上的全纯约化Cartan几何，构建自旋-牵引丛及其各类分量，给出统一的Twinor场方程，并在平坦无挠Lorentz背景下复现有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统与零静质量方程，进而以伴随曲率和Einstein–Cartan动力学表述非线性引力，无需作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以时空为基础构建扭量理论的对偶框架（Twinor理论），利用全纯约化Cartan几何统一处理自旋、质量归一化、实性条件以及非线性引力与物质相互作用，避免传统扭量理论中时空与旋量描述之间的分离。

Method: 在复化(A)dS₄上建立全纯约化Cartan几何模型；由基本自旋-牵引丛定义Dirac twinors，由其对称幂定义梯子twinors，其极端Lorentz分量定义手征twinors。提出一般twinor场方程DY=J，通过相邻缩并、混合散度的源关系加以约束，而不对其完全对称投影附加方程。在平坦无挠Lorentz背景上结合质量归一化源与消失的附加缩并源及消失混合散度复现有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统；无质量手征实现给出零静质量方程。用完全伴随曲率表述非线性引力，以能动张量与自旋流给出Einstein–Cartan动力学；由伴随Bianchi恒等式约束源相容性，用BGG型序列组织无挠无穷小形变，并以DY=FDY记录曲率相容性。

Result: 在平坦无挠Lorentz背景上，相邻方程结合质量归一化源、消失的附加缩并源及消失的混合散度精确复现了有质量Dirac–Fierz–Pauli系统；无质量手征实现给出零静质量方程。在固定质量归一化下，产生正实质量平方的实性条件为粒子尺度上的反de Sitter实性条件，且独立于宇宙学尺度与背景曲率，因此质量耦合相位在量子化之前就存在于经典联络中。非线性引力由完全伴随曲率表述，Einstein–Cartan动力学由能动张量与自旋流确定而无需作用量。两种手征均被保留，并区分了相互作用高自旋场的附加相容性要求与底层丛构造。

Conclusion: Twinor理论成功提供一个统一的时空Cartan几何框架，既能自然复现自由场的有质量与无质量高自旋方程，又能通过在量子化前就存在的质量耦合相位和免作用量的Einstein–Cartan动力学处理非线性引力与物质耦合；该框架同时澄清了高自旋自洽性与几何构造之间的区别。

Abstract: Twinor theory is formulated as a spacetime-based counterpart to twistor theory using holomorphic reductive Cartan geometry modelled on complexified $(A)dS_4$. The fundamental spin-tractor bundle defines Dirac twinors, its symmetric powers define ladder twinors, and their extremal Lorentz components define chiral twinors. The general \textit{twinor field equation} is $D\mathbf Y=\mathbf J$. Its adjacent contractions and mixed divergence are constrained by source relations; no additional equation is imposed on its completely symmetric projection. On a flat torsion-free Lorentzian background, the adjacent equations with the mass-normalization source and vanishing additional contracted sources, together with vanishing mixed divergence, reproduce the massive \textit{Dirac--Fierz--Pauli system}; the massless chiral realization gives the zero-rest-mass equations. Within the fixed mass normalization, the reality condition yielding a positive real mass squared is the \textit{anti-de Sitter reality condition} on the particle scale, independently of the cosmological scale and background curvature. The mass-coupling phase is therefore present in the classical connection before quantization. Nonlinear gravity is formulated through the full adjoint curvature, with energy--momentum and spin currents specifying Einstein--Cartan dynamics without requiring an action. The adjoint Bianchi identity constrains source compatibility, while a BGG-type sequence organizes torsion-free infinitesimal deformations. Matter interactions enter through the coupled connection and contracted source relations, with curvature compatibility recorded by $D\mathbf J=\mathcal F_D\mathbf Y$. Both chiralities are retained, and the additional consistency requirements for interacting higher-spin fields are distinguished from the underlying bundle construction.

</details>


### [205] [Entropic Fluctuations in Quantum Statistical Mechanics: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40463)
*Tristan Benoist,Laurent Bruneau,Vojkan Jakšić,Aannalisa Panati,Claude-Alain Pillet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述并比较了经典涨落定理（Evans–Searles 瞬时版本与 Gallavotti–Cohen 稳态版本）在量子系统中的若干推广，重点讨论开放量子系统中熵产生的不同定义方式，并通过模理论、量子转移算子的谱共振及自旋-费米子模型展示量子熵涨落的新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典涨落定理刻画了熵产生率的普适统计特征，但如何将其推广到量子系统、尤其是开放量子系统的稳态情形（Gallavotti–Cohen 版本的两时测量熵产生）此前尚不完整。作者希望在量子动力系统的代数框架下补齐这一缺失，并厘清各种量子熵产生定义之间的联系与差异。

Method: 论文综述了四种方法：朴素（naive）方法、两时测量（two-time measurement）、相空间收缩（phase space contraction）和辅助态层析（ancilla state tomography）。借助模理论（modular theory）将经典概念推广到量子域，引入量子正则熵涨落原理，并将各熵产生量的统计性质与量子转移算子的谱共振联系起来，最终应用于自旋-费米子模型这一非平衡统计力学范式模型。

Result: 给出了此前缺失的 Gallavotti–Cohen（稳态）涨落定理到两时测量熵产生的推广，建立了各量子熵产生定义之间的比较，并将其统计性质与量子转移算子的谱共振相关联；在自旋-费米子模型中应用了该框架。

Conclusion: 该系列工作为量子统计力学中熵涨落的本质提供了新的理解，表明模理论是连接经典与量子涨落定理的自然桥梁，并展示了谱共振分析在理解开放量子系统非平衡熵涨落中的价值。

Abstract: The celebrated Evans--Searles, respectively Gallavotti--Cohen, fluctuation theorem concerns certain universal statistical features of the entropy production rate of a classical system in a transient, respectively steady, state. In this paper, we review and compare several possible extensions of these fluctuation theorems to quantum systems. We present, in an introductory way, the approach proposed in a recent series of works [Lett. Math. Phys. 114, 32 (2024): doi:10.1007/s11005-024-01777-0], [Rev. Math. Phys 38, 2461006 (2026): doi:10.1142/S0129055X24610063], [Commun. Math. Phys. 406, 201 (2025): 10.1007/s00220-025-05360-z] and [J. Math. Phys. 67, 022101 (2026): doi:10.1063/5.0311182] including a previously missing extension of the Gallavotti--Cohen (steady state) version of the FT to two-time measurement entropy production in the algebraic setting of quantum dynamical systems. We focus on the open quantum systems, where a small system interacts with several (infinitely extended) reservoirs, and discuss the following approaches: naive, two-time measurement, phase space contraction and ancilla state tomography. We review the connection with modular theory. The latter provides a way to extend the classical notions to the quantum domain. We discuss the quantum version of the principle of regular entropic fluctuations, introduced in the classical context in [Nonlinearity 24, 699 (2011): doi:10.1088/0951-7715/24/3/003]. Finally, we relate the statistical properties of these various notions of entropy production to spectral resonances of quantum transfer operators and apply this approach to a paradigmatic model of non-equilibrium statistical mechanics: the spin-fermion model. The results presented here shed a new light on the nature of entropic fluctuations in quantum statistical mechanics.

</details>


### [206] [Universal Critical Jump for Inverse-Square Ising Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36131)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对所有平移不变铁磁 Ising 链，若 $n^2J_n\to B>0$，则临界乘积关系 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$ 成立，无需对有限核或尾部收敛速率作任何限制；并将跃迁关系推广到连续时间的正 Stieltjes 类 Ohmic 自旋-玻色子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于临界点跃迁关系 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$ 的证明通常依赖于对相互作用核（有限核或尾部衰减速率）的额外条件，本文旨在去除这些限制，在最一般的平移不变铁磁 Ising 链（仅要求 $n^2J_n\to B>0$）下建立该关系。

Method: 证明通过对宏观界面的能量进行下界估计：当临界乘积超过 1 时，构造并估算宏观界面（畴壁）的能量，说明其能量代价不足以抑制界面出现，从而排除 $2\beta_c B m_c^2>1$ 的情形，进而得到等式。文章还分析外场趋于零时磁化强度的衰减速率，并研究关联磁化率的发散行为。

Result: 证明了对所有平移不变铁磁 Ising 链成立 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$。尽管磁化强度在临界点存在非零跃迁，连通磁化率在临界点及其邻近的一个有序区间内均为无穷大；在临界耦合处，当外场趋于零时，过剩磁化强度衰减比场的任意正幂都慢。同时将该跃迁关系推广到连续时间的正 Stieltjes 类 Ohmic 自旋-玻色子模型。

Conclusion: 跃迁关系 $2\beta_c B m_c^2=1$ 在最一般条件下成立，不依赖有限核或尾部收敛速率假设；临界点处磁化跃迁虽非零，但连通磁化率在临界点及邻近有序区间发散，揭示了一类非标准临界行为；结果可类比推广至连续时间模型。

Abstract: For every translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, we prove $2β_cBm_c^2=1$, without restrictions on the finite core or the tail convergence rate. The proof bounds the energy of macroscopic interfaces and rules them out if the critical product exceeds one. Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. We also prove the jump relation in continuous time, for a positive-Stieltjes class of Ohmic spin-boson models.

</details>


### [207] [Generalized Dirac Equation in Metric-Affine Spacetimes: Consistency, Effective Mass, and Spinor Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23609)
*Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala,Ozcan Sert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在四维度规-仿射时空（含曲率、挠率和非度规性）中构造广义 Dirac 方程，旋量联络按 Clifford 代数 cl(1,3) 完备基展开并含八个复耦合常数；一致性条件将独立实参数从 16 个降至 8 个。在静态各向同性对称远平行扇区，哈密顿量形式厄米性给出同样限制；共标架项引入标量和赝标量质量贡献，给出有效质量 m_eff=√((m−4b₃)²+16b₄²) 及可用手征重定义消除的手征相位，即使原质量为零也可有非零有效质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清度规-仿射引力（含非度规性和挠率）中 Dirac 场的最小耦合推广：通过直接协变化与变分方法的一致性条件，约束旋量联络中的自由耦合常数，并研究非度规性与共标架耦合在平坦极限下是否仍能产生可观测的质量效应。

Method: 在四维度规-仿射时空构造广义 Dirac 方程，将旋量联络按 Clifford 代数 cl(1,3) 完备基展开（八个复耦合常数）；要求"直接协变化所得 Dirac 方程"与"对称化拉氏量的变分"一致，导出八个实一致性条件；在静态各向同性对称远平行扇区用弯曲空间 Dirac 内积检验哈密顿量的形式厄米性；推导广义旋量曲率的 Clifford 分解。

Result: 一致性条件将独立实参数由 16 减为 8；静态各向同性对称远平行扇区下的厄米性条件重现了对非度规性和共标架耦合的限制。共标架依赖项在即使闵氏时空也引入标量与赝标量质量贡献，对单个自由 Dirac 场给出单一有效质量 m_eff=√((m−4b₃)²+16b₄²) 与可被常手征场重定义消除的手征相位。当仿射曲率、挠率、非度规性全为零时，广义旋量曲率可非零；m=0 时 b₃²+b₄² 同时决定有效质量平方与残留旋量曲率，建立二者的代数联系。

Conclusion: 度规-仿射框架下的广义 Dirac 理论由八个独立实参数刻画；非度规性与共标架耦合即使在平坦时空也能产生有效质量，且存在一个在平坦极限下存活的旋量曲率项，它通过组合 b₃²+b₄² 与有效质量平方代数相关，提示度规-仿射几何背景可留下可观测的物理痕迹。

Abstract: We formulate a generalized Dirac equation in four-dimensional metric-affine spacetimes with curvature, torsion, and non-metricity. The spinor connection is expanded over the complete basis of the Clifford algebra $cl(1,3)$ and contains eight complex coupling constants. Requiring agreement between direct covariantization of the Dirac equation and variation of the corresponding symmetrized Lagrangian imposes eight real consistency conditions, reducing the number of independent real parameters from sixteen to eight. In the static isotropic symmetric teleparallel sector, formal Hermiticity of the Hamiltonian with respect to the curved-space Dirac inner product reproduces the corresponding restrictions on the non-metricity and coframe couplings. The coframe-dependent terms introduce scalar and pseudoscalar mass contributions that persist in Minkowski spacetime. For a single free Dirac field with constant coefficients, they yield one effective mass, $m_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sqrt{(m-4b_3)^2+16b_4^2}$, and a chiral phase removable by a constant chiral field redefinition. A nonzero effective mass is therefore possible even when the original mass parameter vanishes. We also derive the Clifford decomposition of the generalized spinor curvature and show that it can remain nonzero when affine curvature, torsion, and non-metricity all vanish. When $m=0$, the combination $b_3^2+b_4^2$ determines both the effective mass squared and the surviving spinor curvature, establishing an algebraic link between these contributions.

</details>
